7. PRISMA SCHEMA
============================================================
generator client {
  provider = "prisma-client"
  output   = "../src/generated/prisma"
}

datasource db {
  provider = "postgresql"
}

model KartuKeluarga {
  id Int @id @default(autoincrement())

  noKkHash       String @unique @map("no_kk_hash") @db.VarChar(64)
  noKkCiphertext String @map("no_kk_ciphertext") @db.Text
  noKkIv         String @map("no_kk_iv") @db.VarChar(64)
  noKkAuthTag    String @map("no_kk_auth_tag") @db.VarChar(64)

  kepalaKeluarga String? @map("kepala_keluarga")
  alamat         String?

  rt String? @db.VarChar(3)
  rw String? @db.VarChar(3)

  desaKelurahan String? @map("desa_kelurahan")
  kecamatan     String?
  kabupatenKota String? @map("kabupaten_kota")

  kodePos  String? @map("kode_pos") @db.VarChar(5)
  provinsi String?

  anggota AnggotaKeluarga[]

  createdAt DateTime @default(now()) @map("created_at")
  updatedAt DateTime @updatedAt @map("updated_at")

  @@map("kartu_keluarga")
}

model AnggotaKeluarga {
  id Int @id @default(autoincrement())

  kkId Int @map("kk_id")

  kk KartuKeluarga @relation(fields: [kkId], references: [id], onDelete: Cascade)

  noUrut Int @map("no_urut")

  nama String

  nikHash       String @map("nik_hash") @db.VarChar(64)
  nikCiphertext String @map("nik_ciphertext") @db.Text
  nikIv         String @map("nik_iv") @db.VarChar(64)
  nikAuthTag    String @map("nik_auth_tag") @db.VarChar(64)

  jenisKelamin String? @map("jenis_kelamin")
  tempatLahir  String? @map("tempat_lahir")
  tanggalLahir String? @map("tanggal_lahir")

  agama         String?
  pendidikan    String?
  pekerjaan     String?
  golonganDarah String? @map("golongan_darah")

  ayah             String?
  ibu              String?
  baptisAnak       String? @map("baptis_anak")
  sidiBaptisDewasa String? @map("sidi_baptis_dewasa")
  menikah          String?
  domisili         String?

  pemeriksaanKesehatan PemeriksaanKesehatan[]

  createdAt DateTime @default(now()) @map("created_at")
  updatedAt DateTime @updatedAt @map("updated_at")

  @@unique([kkId, nikHash])
  @@index([kkId])
  @@index([nikHash])
  @@map("anggota_keluarga")
}

model EventKesehatan {
  id Int @id @default(autoincrement())

  nama    String
  tanggal DateTime @db.Date
  lokasi  String?

  isClosed Boolean   @default(false) @map("is_closed")
  closedAt DateTime? @map("closed_at")

  pemeriksaan PemeriksaanKesehatan[]
  petugas     PetugasEvent[]

  createdAt DateTime @default(now()) @map("created_at")
  updatedAt DateTime @updatedAt @map("updated_at")

  @@map("event_kesehatan")
}

model PemeriksaanKesehatan {
  id Int @id @default(autoincrement())

  eventId   Int @map("event_id")
  anggotaId Int @map("anggota_id")

  event   EventKesehatan  @relation(fields: [eventId], references: [id])
  anggota AnggotaKeluarga @relation(fields: [anggotaId], references: [id])

  createdByUserId Int? @map("created_by_user_id")
  updatedByUserId Int? @map("updated_by_user_id")

  createdBy User? @relation("PemeriksaanCreatedBy", fields: [createdByUserId], references: [id], onDelete: SetNull)
  updatedBy User? @relation("PemeriksaanUpdatedBy", fields: [updatedByUserId], references: [id], onDelete: SetNull)

  tensiSistolik  Int? @map("tensi_sistolik")
  tensiDiastolik Int? @map("tensi_diastolik")

  gulaDarah  Int?   @map("gula_darah")
  kolesterol Int?
  asamUrat   Float? @map("asam_urat")

  beratBadan  Float? @map("berat_badan")
  tinggiBadan Float? @map("tinggi_badan")

  keluhan      String?
  hasilRingkas String? @map("hasil_ringkas")

  createdAt DateTime @default(now()) @map("created_at")
  updatedAt DateTime @updatedAt @map("updated_at")
  user      User?    @relation(fields: [userId], references: [id])
  userId    Int?

  @@unique([eventId, anggotaId])
  @@index([anggotaId])
  @@index([eventId])
  @@index([createdByUserId])
  @@index([updatedByUserId])
  @@map("pemeriksaan_kesehatan")
}

model PetugasEvent {
  id Int @id @default(autoincrement())

  eventId Int            @map("event_id")
  event   EventKesehatan @relation(fields: [eventId], references: [id], onDelete: Cascade)

  nama String

  createdAt DateTime @default(now()) @map("created_at")

  @@unique([eventId, nama])
  @@index([eventId])
  @@map("petugas_event")
}

enum UserRole {
  ADMIN
  PETUGAS
}

model User {
  id Int @id @default(autoincrement())

  nama         String
  username     String   @unique
  passwordHash String   @map("password_hash")
  role         UserRole @default(PETUGAS)
  isActive     Boolean  @default(true) @map("is_active")

  sessions Session[]

  pemeriksaanDibuat PemeriksaanKesehatan[] @relation("PemeriksaanCreatedBy")
  pemeriksaanDiubah PemeriksaanKesehatan[] @relation("PemeriksaanUpdatedBy")

  createdAt             DateTime               @default(now()) @map("created_at")
  updatedAt             DateTime               @updatedAt @map("updated_at")
  pemeriksaanKesehatans PemeriksaanKesehatan[]

  @@map("users")
}

model Session {
  id Int @id @default(autoincrement())

  tokenHash String @unique @map("token_hash") @db.VarChar(64)

  userId Int  @map("user_id")
  user   User @relation(fields: [userId], references: [id], onDelete: Cascade)

  expiresAt DateTime @map("expires_at")
  createdAt DateTime @default(now()) @map("created_at")

  @@index([userId])
  @@index([expiresAt])
  @@map("sessions")
}

############################################################
import { defineConfig } from 'vite'
import { devtools } from '@tanstack/devtools-vite'

import { tanstackStart } from '@tanstack/react-start/plugin/vite'

import viteReact from '@vitejs/plugin-react'
import tailwindcss from '@tailwindcss/vite'
import { nitro } from 'nitro/vite'

import { VitePWA } from 'vite-plugin-pwa'

const config = defineConfig({
  resolve: { tsconfigPaths: true },
  plugins: [
    devtools(),
    nitro(),
    tailwindcss(),
    tanstackStart(),
    viteReact(),
    VitePWA({
      registerType: 'autoUpdate',
      injectRegister: 'auto',
      manifest: false,
      includeAssets: [
        'icons/favicon-16x16.png',
        'icons/favicon-32x32.png',
        'icons/favicon-48x48.png',
        'icons/apple-touch-icon.png',
      ],
      workbox: {
        cleanupOutdatedCaches: true,

        globPatterns: [],

        runtimeCaching: [
          {
            urlPattern: ({ request }) =>
              ['script', 'style', 'worker'].includes(request.destination),

            handler: 'StaleWhileRevalidate',

            options: {
              cacheName: 'gkj-static-assets',
            },
          },

          {
            urlPattern: ({ request }) =>
              ['image', 'font'].includes(request.destination),

            handler: 'CacheFirst',

            options: {
              cacheName: 'gkj-media-assets',

              expiration: {
                maxEntries: 100,
                maxAgeSeconds: 60 * 60 * 24 * 30,
              },
            },
          },
        ],
      },
    }),
  ],
})

export default config

import { Link } from '@tanstack/react-router'

import type { ColumnDef } from '@tanstack/react-table'

import {
  ChevronRight,
  HeartPulse,
  MapPin,
  MoreHorizontal,
  Users,
} from 'lucide-react'

import { DataTableColumnHeader } from '@/components/data-table/data-table-column-header'

import { Badge } from '@/components/ui/badge'

import { Button } from '@/components/ui/button'

import {
  DropdownMenu,
  DropdownMenuContent,
  DropdownMenuItem,
  DropdownMenuLabel,
  DropdownMenuSeparator,
  DropdownMenuTrigger,
} from '@/components/ui/dropdown-menu'

import type { AnggotaListItem } from './types'

export const anggotaColumns: ColumnDef<AnggotaListItem>[] = [
  {
    accessorKey: 'nama',

    header: ({ column }) => (
      <DataTableColumnHeader column={column} title="Nama" />
    ),

    cell: ({ row }) => (
      <div className="min-w-47.5">
        <Link
          to="/data-anggota/$id"
          params={{
            id: String(row.original.id),
          }}
          className="font-semibold text-slate-900 transition-colors hover:text-primary hover:underline"
        >
          {row.original.nama}
        </Link>

        <div className="mt-1 text-xs text-muted-foreground">
          Anggota #{row.original.no}
        </div>
      </div>
    ),
  },

  {
    accessorKey: 'nik',

    enableSorting: false,

    header: 'NIK',

    cell: ({ row }) => (
      <code className="whitespace-nowrap rounded-md bg-slate-100 px-2 py-1 font-mono text-xs">
        {row.original.nik}
      </code>
    ),
  },

  {
    accessorKey: 'jenisKelamin',

    enableSorting: false,

    header: 'Jenis Kelamin',

    cell: ({ row }) =>
      row.original.jenisKelamin ? (
        <Badge variant="outline" className="whitespace-nowrap">
          {row.original.jenisKelamin}
        </Badge>
      ) : (
        '-'
      ),
  },

  {
    id: 'lahir',

    enableSorting: false,

    header: 'Tempat / Tgl. Lahir',

    cell: ({ row }) => (
      <div className="min-w-40">
        <div>{row.original.tempatLahir ?? '-'}</div>

        <div className="mt-1 text-xs text-muted-foreground">
          {row.original.tanggalLahir ?? '-'}
        </div>
      </div>
    ),
  },

  {
    id: 'kepalaKeluarga',

    accessorFn: (row) => row.kepalaKeluarga ?? '',

    header: ({ column }) => (
      <DataTableColumnHeader column={column} title="Kepala Keluarga" />
    ),

    cell: ({ row }) => (
      <div className="min-w-45">
        <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
          <Users className="size-4 text-muted-foreground" />

          <Link
            to="/data-kk/$id"
            params={{
              id: String(row.original.kkId),
            }}
            className="font-medium hover:text-primary"
          >
            {row.original.kepalaKeluarga ?? '-'}
          </Link>
        </div>
      </div>
    ),
  },

  {
    id: 'alamat',

    accessorFn: (row) => row.alamat ?? '',

    header: ({ column }) => (
      <DataTableColumnHeader column={column} title="Alamat" />
    ),

    cell: ({ row }) => (
      <div className="flex min-w-57.5 items-start gap-2">
        <MapPin className="mt-0.5 size-4 shrink-0 text-muted-foreground" />

        <div>
          <div className="font-medium">{row.original.alamat ?? '-'}</div>

          <div className="mt-1 text-xs text-muted-foreground">
            {row.original.desaKelurahan ?? '-'}
            {' · '}
            {row.original.kecamatan ?? '-'}
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
    ),
  },

  {
    id: 'actions',

    enableSorting: false,
    enableHiding: false,

    cell: ({ row }) => (
      <div className="flex justify-end">
        <DropdownMenu>
          <DropdownMenuTrigger asChild>
            <Button variant="ghost" size="icon" className="size-8">
              <MoreHorizontal className="size-4" />

              <span className="sr-only">Buka menu</span>
            </Button>
          </DropdownMenuTrigger>

          <DropdownMenuContent align="end">
            <DropdownMenuLabel>Aksi</DropdownMenuLabel>

            <DropdownMenuSeparator />

            <DropdownMenuItem asChild>
              <Link
                to="/data-anggota/$id"
                params={{
                  id: String(row.original.id),
                }}
              >
                <HeartPulse className="size-4" />
                Detail & Riwayat Kesehatan
              </Link>
            </DropdownMenuItem>

            <DropdownMenuItem asChild>
              <Link
                to="/data-kk/$id"
                params={{
                  id: String(row.original.kkId),
                }}
              >
                <ChevronRight className="size-4" />
                Buka KK
              </Link>
            </DropdownMenuItem>
          </DropdownMenuContent>
        </DropdownMenu>
      </div>
    ),
  },
]

import type { ReactNode } from 'react'

import { DataPagination } from './data-pagination'

import type { DataPaginationState, PageSize } from './data-pagination'

type Props = {
  children: ReactNode

  pagination: DataPaginationState

  onPageChange: (page: number) => void

  onPageSizeChange: (size: PageSize) => void
}

export function DataListCard({
  children,
  pagination,
  onPageChange,
  onPageSizeChange,
}: Props) {
  return (
    <section className="overflow-hidden rounded-2xl border border-blue-100/80 bg-white shadow-[0_8px_30px_rgba(15,23,42,0.04)]">
      <div className="p-4 sm:p-5">{children}</div>

      <div className="border-t border-blue-100/70 bg-slate-50/50 px-4 py-4 sm:px-5">
        <DataPagination
          pagination={pagination}
          onPageChange={onPageChange}
          onPageSizeChange={onPageSizeChange}
        />
      </div>
    </section>
  )
}

import type { ReactNode } from 'react'

type Props = {
  icon: ReactNode
  title: string
  description: string
  action?: ReactNode
}

export function DataListEmpty({ icon, title, description, action }: Props) {
  return (
    <div className="rounded-3xl border border-dashed border-blue-200 bg-white/80 px-6 py-16 text-center shadow-sm">
      <div className="mx-auto flex size-16 items-center justify-center rounded-2xl bg-blue-50 text-blue-600">
        {icon}
      </div>

      <h2 className="mt-5 text-xl font-semibold tracking-tight text-slate-900">
        {title}
      </h2>

      <p className="mx-auto mt-2 max-w-md text-sm leading-6 text-slate-500">
        {description}
      </p>

      {action && <div className="mt-6">{action}</div>}
    </div>
  )
}

import type { ReactNode } from 'react'

type Props = {
  icon: ReactNode

  eyebrow: string
  title: string
  description: string

  badge?: ReactNode
  actions?: ReactNode
}

export function DataListHero({
  icon,
  eyebrow,
  title,
  description,
  badge,
  actions,
}: Props) {
  return (
    <section className="mb-6 overflow-hidden rounded-2xl border border-slate-200 bg-white shadow-sm">
      {/* aksen atas */}
      <div className="h-1 bg-linear-to-r from-blue-600 via-blue-500 to-sky-400" />

      <div className="flex flex-col gap-5 px-5 py-5 sm:px-6 sm:py-6 lg:flex-row lg:items-center lg:justify-between">
        {/* INFORMASI */}
        <div className="min-w-0 flex-1">
          <div className="flex items-start gap-4">
            <div className="mt-0.5 flex size-12 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-xl bg-blue-50 text-blue-600 ring-1 ring-inset ring-blue-100">
              {icon}
            </div>

            <div className="min-w-0 flex-1">
              <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2">
                <span className="text-xs font-bold uppercase tracking-[0.16em] text-blue-600">
                  {eyebrow}
                </span>

                {badge}
              </div>

              <h1 className="mt-2 text-2xl font-bold tracking-tight text-slate-900 sm:text-[28px]">
                {title}
              </h1>

              <p className="mt-2 max-w-3xl text-sm leading-6 text-slate-500">
                {description}
              </p>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* ACTION */}
        {actions && (
          <div className="flex w-full shrink-0 flex-col gap-2 sm:w-auto sm:flex-row lg:justify-end">
            {actions}
          </div>
        )}
      </div>
    </section>
  )
}

FILE: src/components/data-list/data-list-stats.tsx
############################################################

import type { ReactNode } from 'react'

export type DataListStat = {
  label: string
  value: string
  icon: ReactNode
}

type Props = {
  items: DataListStat[]
}

export function DataListStats({ items }: Props) {
  if (items.length === 0) {
    return null
  }

  return (
    <div
      className={`mb-6 grid gap-3 ${
        items.length === 2
          ? 'sm:grid-cols-2'
          : items.length === 3
            ? 'sm:grid-cols-3'
            : 'sm:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-4'
      }`}
    >
      {items.map((item) => (
        <div
          key={item.label}
          className="flex items-center gap-3 rounded-2xl border border-blue-100/80 bg-white/90 px-4 py-4 shadow-sm"
        >
          <div className="flex size-10 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-xl bg-blue-50 text-blue-600">
            {item.icon}
          </div>

          <div>
            <div className="text-xs font-medium text-slate-400">
              {item.label}
            </div>

            <div className="mt-0.5 text-lg font-bold tracking-tight text-slate-900">
              {item.value}
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      ))}
    </div>
  )
}


############################################################
FILE: src/components/data-list/data-pagination.tsx
############################################################

import { ChevronLeft, ChevronRight } from 'lucide-react'

import { Button } from '@/components/ui/button'
import {
  Select,
  SelectContent,
  SelectItem,
  SelectTrigger,
  SelectValue,
} from '@/components/ui/select'

export type DataPaginationState = {
  page: number
  limit: number
  total: number
  totalPages: number
}

export type PageSize = 10 | 20 | 50 | 100

type Props = {
  pagination: DataPaginationState

  onPageChange: (page: number) => void

  onPageSizeChange: (size: PageSize) => void
}

export function DataPagination({
  pagination,
  onPageChange,
  onPageSizeChange,
}: Props) {
  const totalPages = Math.max(pagination.totalPages, 1)

  const from =
    pagination.total === 0 ? 0 : (pagination.page - 1) * pagination.limit + 1

  const to = Math.min(
    pagination.page * pagination.limit,

    pagination.total,
  )

  return (
    <div className="flex flex-col gap-4 sm:flex-row sm:items-center sm:justify-between">
      <div className="text-xs text-slate-500">
        Menampilkan <strong>{from}</strong>
        {' – '}
        <strong>{to}</strong>
        {' dari '}
        <strong>{pagination.total.toLocaleString('id-ID')}</strong> data
      </div>

      <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-3">
        <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
          <span className="whitespace-nowrap text-xs text-slate-500">
            Baris
          </span>

          <Select
            value={String(pagination.limit)}
            onValueChange={(value) => {
              const size = Number(value)

              if (size === 10 || size === 20 || size === 50 || size === 100) {
                onPageSizeChange(size)
              }
            }}
          >
            <SelectTrigger className="h-9 w-20 bg-white">
              <SelectValue />
            </SelectTrigger>

            <SelectContent>
              <SelectItem value="10">10</SelectItem>

              <SelectItem value="20">20</SelectItem>

              <SelectItem value="50">50</SelectItem>

              <SelectItem value="100">100</SelectItem>
            </SelectContent>
          </Select>
        </div>

        <span className="whitespace-nowrap text-xs text-slate-500">
          Halaman <strong>{pagination.page}</strong>
          {' dari '}
          <strong>{totalPages}</strong>
        </span>

        <Button
          type="button"
          variant="outline"
          size="icon"
          disabled={pagination.page <= 1}
          onClick={() => onPageChange(pagination.page - 1)}
          aria-label="Halaman sebelumnya"
        >
          <ChevronLeft className="size-4" />
        </Button>

        <Button
          type="button"
          variant="outline"
          size="icon"
          disabled={pagination.page >= totalPages}
          onClick={() => onPageChange(pagination.page + 1)}
          aria-label="Halaman berikutnya"
        >
          <ChevronRight className="size-4" />
        </Button>
      </div>
    </div>
  )
}


############################################################
FILE: src/components/data-table/data-table-column-header.tsx
############################################################

import type { Column } from '@tanstack/react-table'

import { ArrowDown, ArrowUp, ChevronsUpDown, EyeOff } from 'lucide-react'

import {
  DropdownMenu,
  DropdownMenuContent,
  DropdownMenuItem,
  DropdownMenuSeparator,
  DropdownMenuTrigger,
} from '@/components/ui/dropdown-menu'

type Props<TData, TValue> = {
  column: Column<TData, TValue>

  title: string
}

export function DataTableColumnHeader<TData, TValue>({
  column,
  title,
}: Props<TData, TValue>) {
  if (!column.getCanSort()) {
    return <span className="font-semibold">{title}</span>
  }

  const sorted = column.getIsSorted()

  return (
    <DropdownMenu>
      <DropdownMenuTrigger asChild>
        <button
          type="button"
          className="-ml-1 inline-flex h-8 items-center gap-1 rounded-md px-1 text-xs font-semibold text-slate-600 transition hover:text-slate-900"
        >
          {title}

          {sorted === 'desc' ? (
            <ArrowDown className="size-3.5 text-blue-600" />
          ) : sorted === 'asc' ? (
            <ArrowUp className="size-3.5 text-blue-600" />
          ) : (
            <ChevronsUpDown className="size-3.5 text-slate-400" />
          )}
        </button>
      </DropdownMenuTrigger>

      <DropdownMenuContent align="start">
        <DropdownMenuItem onClick={() => column.toggleSorting(false)}>
          <ArrowUp className="size-4" />
          Urut naik
        </DropdownMenuItem>

        <DropdownMenuItem onClick={() => column.toggleSorting(true)}>
          <ArrowDown className="size-4" />
          Urut turun
        </DropdownMenuItem>

        {column.getCanHide() && (
          <>
            <DropdownMenuSeparator />

            <DropdownMenuItem onClick={() => column.toggleVisibility(false)}>
              <EyeOff className="size-4" />
              Sembunyikan
            </DropdownMenuItem>
          </>
        )}
      </DropdownMenuContent>
    </DropdownMenu>
  )
}

import type { Table as TanStackTable } from '@tanstack/react-table'

import { Columns3 } from 'lucide-react'

import { Button } from '@/components/ui/button'

import {
  DropdownMenu,
  DropdownMenuCheckboxItem,
  DropdownMenuContent,
  DropdownMenuLabel,
  DropdownMenuSeparator,
  DropdownMenuTrigger,
} from '@/components/ui/dropdown-menu'

type Props<TData> = {
  table: TanStackTable<TData>

  columnLabels?: Partial<Record<string, string>>
}

export function DataTableViewOptions<TData>({
  table,
  columnLabels = {},
}: Props<TData>) {
  const columns = table
    .getAllColumns()
    .filter(
      (column) =>
        typeof column.accessorFn !== 'undefined' && column.getCanHide(),
    )

  if (columns.length === 0) {
    return null
  }

  return (
    <DropdownMenu>
      <DropdownMenuTrigger asChild>
        <Button
          variant="outline"
          className="
    h-10
    w-full
    rounded-lg
    border-slate-200
    bg-white
    px-3
    text-slate-600
    shadow-none
    sm:w-auto
  "
        >
          <Columns3 className="size-4" />
          Kolom
        </Button>
      </DropdownMenuTrigger>

      <DropdownMenuContent align="end" className="w-56">
        <DropdownMenuLabel>Tampilkan kolom</DropdownMenuLabel>

        <DropdownMenuSeparator />

        {columns.map((column) => (
          <DropdownMenuCheckboxItem
            key={column.id}
            checked={column.getIsVisible()}
            onCheckedChange={(value) => column.toggleVisibility(Boolean(value))}
          >
            {columnLabels[column.id] ?? column.id}
          </DropdownMenuCheckboxItem>
        ))}
      </DropdownMenuContent>
    </DropdownMenu>
  )
}


############################################################
FILE: src/components/data-table/data-table.tsx
############################################################

import {
  flexRender,
  getCoreRowModel,
  useReactTable,
} from '@tanstack/react-table'

import type {
  ColumnDef,
  OnChangeFn,
  SortingState,
  VisibilityState,
} from '@tanstack/react-table'

import { Search, SearchX, X } from 'lucide-react'

import { useEffect, useState } from 'react'

import { Input } from '@/components/ui/input'

import {
  Select,
  SelectContent,
  SelectItem,
  SelectTrigger,
  SelectValue,
} from '@/components/ui/select'

import {
  Table,
  TableBody,
  TableCell,
  TableHead,
  TableHeader,
  TableRow,
} from '@/components/ui/table'

import { DataTableViewOptions } from './data-table-view-options'

export type DataTableSearchOption = {
  value: string
  label: string
  placeholder?: string
}

type Props<TData> = {
  columns: ColumnDef<TData>[]
  data: TData[]

  searchValue: string
  searchMode: string

  searchOptions: DataTableSearchOption[]

  columnLabels?: Partial<Record<string, string>>

  sorting: SortingState

  onSearchChange: (value: string) => void

  onSearchModeChange: (value: string) => void

  onSortingChange: OnChangeFn<SortingState>

  emptyTitle?: string
  emptyDescription?: string
}

export function DataTable<TData>({
  columns,
  data,

  searchValue,
  searchMode,
  searchOptions,

  columnLabels,

  sorting,

  onSearchChange,
  onSearchModeChange,
  onSortingChange,

  emptyTitle = 'Data tidak ditemukan',
  emptyDescription = 'Coba ubah kata pencarian atau pilihan pencarian.',
}: Props<TData>) {
  const [query, setQuery] = useState(searchValue)

  const [columnVisibility, setColumnVisibility] = useState<VisibilityState>({})

  useEffect(() => {
    setQuery(searchValue)
  }, [searchValue])

  useEffect(() => {
    if (query === searchValue) {
      return
    }

    const timeout = window.setTimeout(() => {
      onSearchChange(query)
    }, 400)

    return () => {
      window.clearTimeout(timeout)
    }
  }, [query, searchValue, onSearchChange])

  const table = useReactTable({
    data,
    columns,

    manualSorting: true,

    state: {
      sorting,
      columnVisibility,
    },

    onSortingChange,

    onColumnVisibilityChange: setColumnVisibility,

    getCoreRowModel: getCoreRowModel(),
  })

  const currentSearchOption = searchOptions.find(
    (option) => option.value === searchMode,
  )

  const placeholder = currentSearchOption?.placeholder ?? 'Cari data...'

  const hasSearch = searchValue.trim().length > 0

  function clearSearch() {
    setQuery('')

    onSearchChange('')
  }

  return (
    <div>
      {/* TOOLBAR */}
      <div className="mb-5 flex flex-col gap-3 xl:flex-row xl:items-center xl:justify-between">
        <div className="flex min-w-0 flex-1 flex-col gap-2 sm:flex-row xl:max-w-3xl">
          <Select value={searchMode} onValueChange={onSearchModeChange}>
            <SelectTrigger
              className="
    w-full shrink-0
    rounded-lg border-slate-200 bg-white
    shadow-none
    data-[size=default]:h-10
    sm:w-40
  "
            >
              <SelectValue />
            </SelectTrigger>

            <SelectContent>
              {searchOptions.map((option) => (
                <SelectItem key={option.value} value={option.value}>
                  {option.label}
                </SelectItem>
              ))}
            </SelectContent>
          </Select>

          <div className="relative min-w-0 flex-1">
            <Search className="pointer-events-none absolute left-3 top-1/2 size-4 -translate-y-1/2 text-slate-400" />

            <Input
              value={query}
              onChange={(event) => setQuery(event.target.value)}
              placeholder={placeholder}
              className="
    h-10
    w-full
    rounded-lg
    border-slate-200
    bg-white
    pl-9
    pr-10
    shadow-none
  "
            />

            {query && (
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={clearSearch}
                className="absolute right-2 top-1/2 flex size-7 -translate-y-1/2 items-center justify-center rounded-md text-slate-400 transition hover:bg-slate-100 hover:text-slate-700"
                aria-label="Hapus pencarian"
              >
                <X className="size-4" />
              </button>
            )}
          </div>
        </div>

        <DataTableViewOptions table={table} columnLabels={columnLabels} />
      </div>

      {searchMode === 'nik' && (
        <div className="-mt-2 mb-4 text-xs text-slate-400">
          Pencarian NIK menggunakan nomor lengkap.
        </div>
      )}

      {/* TABLE */}
      <div className="overflow-hidden rounded-xl border border-slate-200">
        <Table>
          <TableHeader>
            {table.getHeaderGroups().map((headerGroup) => (
              <TableRow
                key={headerGroup.id}
                className="border-slate-200 bg-slate-50/80 hover:bg-slate-50/80"
              >
                {headerGroup.headers.map((header) => (
                  <TableHead
                    key={header.id}
                    className="h-11 whitespace-nowrap px-4 text-xs font-semibold text-slate-600"
                  >
                    {header.isPlaceholder
                      ? null
                      : flexRender(
                          header.column.columnDef.header,

                          header.getContext(),
                        )}
                  </TableHead>
                ))}
              </TableRow>
            ))}
          </TableHeader>

          <TableBody>
            {table.getRowModel().rows.length > 0 ? (
              table.getRowModel().rows.map((row) => (
                <TableRow
                  key={row.id}
                  className="border-slate-100 transition-colors hover:bg-slate-50/70"
                >
                  {row.getVisibleCells().map((cell) => (
                    <TableCell key={cell.id} className="px-4 py-3 align-top">
                      {flexRender(
                        cell.column.columnDef.cell,

                        cell.getContext(),
                      )}
                    </TableCell>
                  ))}
                </TableRow>
              ))
            ) : (
              <TableRow className="hover:bg-transparent">
                <TableCell
                  colSpan={table.getVisibleLeafColumns().length}
                  className="h-56"
                >
                  <div className="flex flex-col items-center justify-center text-center">
                    <div className="flex size-12 items-center justify-center rounded-xl bg-slate-100 text-slate-400">
                      <SearchX className="size-5" />
                    </div>

                    <div className="mt-4 font-semibold text-slate-800">
                      {emptyTitle}
                    </div>

                    <p className="mt-1 max-w-sm text-xs leading-5 text-slate-500">
                      {emptyDescription}
                    </p>

                    {hasSearch && (
                      <button
                        type="button"
                        className="mt-4 text-sm font-medium text-blue-600 hover:text-blue-700"
                        onClick={clearSearch}
                      >
                        Hapus pencarian
                      </button>
                    )}
                  </div>
                </TableCell>
              </TableRow>
            )}
          </TableBody>
        </Table>
      </div>
    </div>
  )
}

import { Link } from '@tanstack/react-router'

import { ArrowRight, HeartPulse } from 'lucide-react'

import { Button } from '@/components/ui/button'

type LatestEvent = {
  id: number
  nama: string
  tanggal: string
  lokasi: string | null
  isClosed: boolean
  jumlahPemeriksaan: number
}

type HomeLatestEventProps = {
  event: LatestEvent | null
}

export function HomeLatestEvent({ event }: HomeLatestEventProps) {
  if (!event) {
    return null
  }

  return (
    <section className="mt-4 overflow-hidden rounded-2xl border border-slate-200 bg-white shadow-sm">
      <div className="flex flex-col gap-4 px-5 py-5 sm:flex-row sm:items-center sm:justify-between sm:px-6">
        <div className="flex min-w-0 items-start gap-3">
          <div className="flex size-10 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-xl bg-blue-50 text-blue-600">
            <HeartPulse className="size-4" />
          </div>

          <div className="min-w-0">
            <div className="text-xs font-medium text-slate-400">
              Event Kesehatan Terbaru
            </div>

            <div className="mt-1 flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2">
              <div className="font-semibold text-slate-900">{event.nama}</div>

              <span
                className={[
                  'rounded-full px-2 py-0.5 text-[10px] font-medium',
                  event.isClosed
                    ? 'bg-slate-100 text-slate-500'
                    : 'bg-emerald-50 text-emerald-700',
                ].join(' ')}
              >
                {event.isClosed ? 'Selesai' : 'Aktif'}
              </span>
            </div>

            <div className="mt-1.5 flex flex-wrap gap-x-4 gap-y-1 text-xs text-slate-500">
              <span>{formatTanggal(event.tanggal)}</span>

              {event.lokasi && <span>{event.lokasi}</span>}

              <span>
                {event.jumlahPemeriksaan.toLocaleString('id-ID')} pemeriksaan
              </span>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>

        <Button
          variant="outline"
          className="h-10 shrink-0 border-slate-200 bg-white px-4 shadow-none"
          asChild
        >
          <Link
            to="/event-kesehatan/$id"
            params={{
              id: String(event.id),
            }}
            preload="intent"
          >
            Buka Event
            <ArrowRight className="size-4" />
          </Link>
        </Button>
      </div>
    </section>
  )
}

function formatTanggal(value: string) {
  const [year, month, day] = value.split('-').map(Number)

  return new Intl.DateTimeFormat('id-ID', {
    day: 'numeric',
    month: 'long',
    year: 'numeric',
  }).format(new Date(year, month - 1, day))
}

############################################################
FILE: src/components/kesehatan/create-event-form.tsx
############################################################

import { zodResolver } from '@hookform/resolvers/zod'
import { CalendarPlus, Plus, UserRound, UsersRound, X } from 'lucide-react'
import { useState } from 'react'
import { useForm } from 'react-hook-form'
import { toast } from 'sonner'

import { Button } from '@/components/ui/button'
import { Card, CardContent, CardHeader, CardTitle } from '@/components/ui/card'
import { Input } from '@/components/ui/input'

import { createEventKesehatan } from '@/server/kesehatan'
import type { CreateEventKesehatanInput } from '@/server/kesehatan'
import { createEventKesehatanSchema } from '@/server/kesehatan/schemas'

import { FormField } from './form-field'

type Props = {
  onCreated: () => void | Promise<void>
}

export function CreateEventForm({ onCreated }: Props) {
  const [petugasInput, setPetugasInput] = useState('')

  const form = useForm<CreateEventKesehatanInput>({
    resolver: zodResolver(createEventKesehatanSchema),

    defaultValues: {
      nama: '',
      tanggal: '',
      lokasi: '',
      petugas: [],
    },
  })

  const petugas = form.watch('petugas')

  function handleTambahPetugas() {
    const value = petugasInput.trim()

    if (!value) {
      return
    }

    const exists = petugas.some(
      (item) => item.toLowerCase() === value.toLowerCase(),
    )

    if (exists) {
      toast.error('Petugas tersebut sudah ditambahkan.')

      return
    }

    form.setValue('petugas', [...petugas, value], {
      shouldDirty: true,
      shouldValidate: true,
    })

    setPetugasInput('')
  }

  function handleHapusPetugas(nama: string) {
    form.setValue(
      'petugas',

      petugas.filter((item) => item !== nama),

      {
        shouldDirty: true,
        shouldValidate: true,
      },
    )
  }

  async function onSubmit(values: CreateEventKesehatanInput) {
    try {
      await createEventKesehatan({
        data: values,
      })

      toast.success('Event kesehatan berhasil dibuat.')

      form.reset({
        nama: '',
        tanggal: '',
        lokasi: '',
        petugas: [],
      })

      setPetugasInput('')

      await onCreated()
    } catch (error) {
      toast.error('Gagal membuat event', {
        description:
          error instanceof Error
            ? error.message
            : 'Terjadi kesalahan pada server.',
      })
    }
  }

  const isSubmitting = form.formState.isSubmitting

  return (
    <Card className="overflow-hidden rounded-2xl border-slate-200 bg-white shadow-sm">
      <CardHeader className="border-b border-slate-200 px-5 py-4">
        <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
          <div className="flex size-10 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-xl bg-blue-50 text-blue-600">
            <CalendarPlus className="size-4" />
          </div>

          <div className="min-w-0">
            <CardTitle className="text-base font-semibold text-slate-900">
              Buat Event
            </CardTitle>

            <p className="mt-0.5 text-xs text-slate-500">
              Tambahkan kegiatan kesehatan baru.
            </p>
          </div>
        </div>
      </CardHeader>

      <CardContent className="p-5">
        <form className="space-y-5" onSubmit={form.handleSubmit(onSubmit)}>
          <FormField
            label="Nama Event"
            error={form.formState.errors.nama?.message}
          >
            <Input
              {...form.register('nama')}
              disabled={isSubmitting}
              placeholder="Contoh: Cek Kesehatan Oktober 2026"
              className="h-10 rounded-lg border-slate-200 bg-white shadow-none"
            />
          </FormField>

          <FormField
            label="Tanggal"
            error={form.formState.errors.tanggal?.message}
          >
            <Input
              type="date"
              {...form.register('tanggal')}
              disabled={isSubmitting}
              className="h-10 rounded-lg border-slate-200 bg-white shadow-none"
            />
          </FormField>

          <FormField
            label="Lokasi"
            error={form.formState.errors.lokasi?.message}
          >
            <Input
              {...form.register('lokasi')}
              disabled={isSubmitting}
              placeholder="Lokasi kegiatan"
              className="h-10 rounded-lg border-slate-200 bg-white shadow-none"
            />
          </FormField>

          <div className="border-t border-slate-100 pt-5">
            <div className="mb-3 flex items-center justify-between gap-3">
              <div>
                <div className="flex items-center gap-2 text-sm font-medium text-slate-800">
                  <UsersRound className="size-4 text-slate-500" />
                  Petugas Event
                </div>

                <p className="mt-1 text-xs text-slate-400">
                  Tambahkan petugas yang bertugas pada event ini.
                </p>
              </div>

              {petugas.length > 0 && (
                <span className="shrink-0 rounded-md bg-slate-100 px-2 py-1 text-[11px] font-medium text-slate-500">
                  {petugas.length} petugas
                </span>
              )}
            </div>

            <div className="flex gap-2">
              <div className="relative min-w-0 flex-1">
                <UserRound className="pointer-events-none absolute left-3 top-1/2 size-4 -translate-y-1/2 text-slate-400" />

                <Input
                  value={petugasInput}
                  disabled={isSubmitting}
                  placeholder="Nama petugas"
                  className="h-10 rounded-lg border-slate-200 bg-white pl-9 shadow-none"
                  onChange={(event) => setPetugasInput(event.target.value)}
                  onKeyDown={(event) => {
                    if (event.key === 'Enter') {
                      event.preventDefault()

                      handleTambahPetugas()
                    }
                  }}
                />
              </div>

              <Button
                type="button"
                variant="outline"
                className="h-10 shrink-0 rounded-lg border-slate-200 bg-white px-3"
                disabled={isSubmitting || !petugasInput.trim()}
                onClick={handleTambahPetugas}
              >
                <Plus className="size-4" />

                <span className="hidden sm:inline">Tambah</span>
              </Button>
            </div>

            {petugas.length > 0 && (
              <div className="mt-3 flex flex-wrap gap-2">
                {petugas.map((nama) => (
                  <div
                    key={nama}
                    className="inline-flex max-w-full items-center gap-2 rounded-lg border border-slate-200 bg-slate-50 px-2.5 py-1.5 text-xs text-slate-700"
                  >
                    <span className="truncate">{nama}</span>

                    <button
                      type="button"
                      disabled={isSubmitting}
                      onClick={() => handleHapusPetugas(nama)}
                      className="flex size-5 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded text-slate-400 transition hover:bg-white hover:text-red-600 disabled:pointer-events-none disabled:opacity-50"
                      aria-label={`Hapus ${nama}`}
                    >
                      <X className="size-3.5" />
                    </button>
                  </div>
                ))}
              </div>
            )}

            {form.formState.errors.petugas?.message && (
              <p className="mt-2 text-xs text-destructive">
                {form.formState.errors.petugas.message}
              </p>
            )}
          </div>

          <div className="border-t border-slate-100 pt-5">
            <Button
              type="submit"
              className="h-10 w-full rounded-lg"
              disabled={isSubmitting}
            >
              <CalendarPlus className="size-4" />

              {isSubmitting ? 'Menyimpan...' : 'Buat Event'}
            </Button>
          </div>
        </form>
      </CardContent>
    </Card>
  )
}


############################################################
FILE: src/components/kesehatan/daftar-pemeriksaan-event.tsx
############################################################

import {
  ChevronDown,
  ChevronUp,
  Clock3,
  Pencil,
  Search,
  Stethoscope,
  UserRound,
  X,
} from 'lucide-react'
import { useEffect, useMemo, useState } from 'react'

import { PemeriksaanForm } from '@/components/kesehatan/pemeriksaan-form'
import { DataPagination } from '@/components/data-list/data-pagination'

import type { PageSize } from '@/components/data-list/data-pagination'

import { Button } from '@/components/ui/button'
import { Card, CardContent, CardHeader, CardTitle } from '@/components/ui/card'
import { Input } from '@/components/ui/input'

import { usePemeriksaanEvent } from '@/components/kesehatan/use-pemeriksaan-event'

import type { PemeriksaanEventItem } from '@/components/kesehatan/use-pemeriksaan-event'

type PetugasEventItem = {
  id: number
  nama: string
}

type Props = {
  eventId: number

  refreshKey: number
  readOnly?: boolean

  onChanged: () => void | Promise<void>
}

export function DaftarPemeriksaanEvent({
  eventId,
  refreshKey,
  readOnly = false,
  onChanged,
}: Props) {
  const { data, isLoading, error } = usePemeriksaanEvent({
    eventId,
    refreshKey,
  })

  const [q, setQ] = useState('')

  const [page, setPage] = useState(1)

  const [limit, setLimit] = useState<PageSize>(10)

  const [expandedId, setExpandedId] = useState<number | null>(null)

  const [editingId, setEditingId] = useState<number | null>(null)

  useEffect(() => {
    if (readOnly) {
      setEditingId(null)
    }
  }, [readOnly])

  const filteredData = useMemo(() => {
    const query = q.trim().toLowerCase()

    if (!query) {
      return data
    }

    return data.filter((item) => {
      const searchable = [item.nama, item.nik]
        .filter(Boolean)
        .join(' ')
        .toLowerCase()

      return searchable.includes(query)
    })
  }, [data, q])

  const total = filteredData.length

  const totalPages = Math.max(Math.ceil(total / limit), 1)

  const safePage = Math.min(page, totalPages)

  const visibleData = filteredData.slice(
    (safePage - 1) * limit,
    safePage * limit,
  )

  useEffect(() => {
    if (page > totalPages) {
      setPage(totalPages)
    }
  }, [page, totalPages])

  function handleSearchChange(value: string) {
    setQ(value)
    setPage(1)
    setExpandedId(null)
    setEditingId(null)
  }

  function handlePageChange(nextPage: number) {
    setPage(nextPage)
    setExpandedId(null)
    setEditingId(null)
  }

  function handlePageSizeChange(nextLimit: PageSize) {
    setLimit(nextLimit)
    setPage(1)
    setExpandedId(null)
    setEditingId(null)
  }

  if (isLoading) {
    return (
      <Card className="rounded-2xl border-slate-200 bg-white shadow-sm">
        <CardContent className="py-10 text-center text-sm text-slate-500">
          Memuat daftar pemeriksaan...
        </CardContent>
      </Card>
    )
  }

  if (error) {
    return (
      <Card className="rounded-2xl border-red-200 bg-white shadow-sm">
        <CardContent className="px-6 py-10 text-center">
          <div className="text-sm font-semibold text-red-800">
            Daftar pemeriksaan gagal dimuat
          </div>

          <p className="mt-1 text-xs leading-5 text-red-700">{error}</p>
        </CardContent>
      </Card>
    )
  }

  return (
    <Card className="overflow-hidden rounded-2xl border-slate-200 bg-white shadow-sm">
      <CardHeader className="border-b border-slate-200 px-5 py-4 sm:px-6">
        <div className="flex flex-col gap-4 sm:flex-row sm:items-center sm:justify-between">
          <div className="flex min-w-0 items-center gap-3">
            <div className="flex size-10 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-xl bg-blue-50 text-blue-600">
              <Stethoscope className="size-4" />
            </div>

            <div className="min-w-0">
              <CardTitle className="text-base font-semibold text-slate-900">
                Pemeriksaan Event Ini
              </CardTitle>

              <p className="mt-0.5 text-xs text-slate-500">
                Daftar warga yang sudah diperiksa pada event ini.
              </p>
            </div>
          </div>

          <span className="w-fit shrink-0 rounded-md bg-slate-100 px-2.5 py-1 text-[11px] font-medium text-slate-500">
            {data.length.toLocaleString('id-ID')} warga
          </span>
        </div>
      </CardHeader>

      {data.length === 0 ? (
        <CardContent className="p-6">
          <div className="rounded-xl border border-dashed border-slate-200 bg-slate-50/50 px-6 py-10 text-center">
            <div className="mx-auto flex size-11 items-center justify-center rounded-xl bg-white text-slate-400 ring-1 ring-slate-200">
              <Stethoscope className="size-4" />
            </div>

            <div className="mt-3 text-sm font-semibold text-slate-700">
              Belum ada pemeriksaan
            </div>

            <p className="mt-1 text-xs leading-5 text-slate-500">
              Data warga yang sudah diperiksa pada event ini akan muncul di
              sini.
            </p>
          </div>
        </CardContent>
      ) : (
        <>
          {/* TOOLBAR */}
          <div className="border-b border-slate-200 px-4 py-4 sm:px-5">
            <div className="relative max-w-md">
              <Search className="pointer-events-none absolute left-3 top-1/2 size-4 -translate-y-1/2 text-slate-400" />

              <Input
                value={q}
                placeholder="Cari nama atau NIK..."
                className="h-10 rounded-lg border-slate-200 bg-white pl-9 pr-10 shadow-none"
                onChange={(event) => handleSearchChange(event.target.value)}
              />

              {q && (
                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() => handleSearchChange('')}
                  className="absolute right-2 top-1/2 flex size-7 -translate-y-1/2 items-center justify-center rounded-md text-slate-400 transition hover:bg-slate-100 hover:text-slate-700"
                  aria-label="Hapus pencarian"
                >
                  <X className="size-4" />
                </button>
              )}
            </div>

            {q && (
              <p className="mt-2 text-xs text-slate-500">
                {total.toLocaleString('id-ID')} warga ditemukan
              </p>
            )}
          </div>

          {/* LIST */}
          {visibleData.length > 0 ? (
            <div className="divide-y divide-slate-100">
              {visibleData.map((item) => (
                <PemeriksaanItem
                  key={item.id}
                  item={item}
                  eventId={eventId}
                  petugas={item.petugas}
                  expanded={expandedId === item.id}
                  editing={editingId === item.id}
                  readOnly={readOnly}
                  onToggle={() => {
                    setExpandedId((current) =>
                      current === item.id ? null : item.id,
                    )

                    if (editingId === item.id) {
                      setEditingId(null)
                    }
                  }}
                  onEdit={() => {
                    setExpandedId(item.id)

                    setEditingId(item.id)
                  }}
                  onCancelEdit={() => {
                    setEditingId(null)
                  }}
                  onSaved={async () => {
                    setEditingId(null)

                    await onChanged()
                  }}
                />
              ))}
            </div>
          ) : (
            <div className="px-6 py-12 text-center">
              <div className="text-sm font-semibold text-slate-700">
                Warga tidak ditemukan
              </div>

              <p className="mt-1 text-xs text-slate-500">
                Coba ubah nama atau NIK yang dicari.
              </p>
            </div>
          )}

          {/* PAGINATION */}
          {total > 0 && (
            <div className="border-t border-slate-200 bg-slate-50/60 px-4 py-4 sm:px-5">
              <DataPagination
                pagination={{
                  page: safePage,

                  limit,

                  total,

                  totalPages,
                }}
                onPageChange={handlePageChange}
                onPageSizeChange={handlePageSizeChange}
              />
            </div>
          )}
        </>
      )}
    </Card>
  )
}

type PemeriksaanItemProps = {
  item: PemeriksaanEventItem

  eventId: number

  petugas: PetugasEventItem[]

  expanded: boolean
  editing: boolean
  readOnly: boolean

  onToggle: () => void
  onEdit: () => void
  onCancelEdit: () => void

  onSaved: () => void | Promise<void>
}

function PemeriksaanItem({
  item,
  eventId,
  petugas,
  expanded,
  editing,
  readOnly,
  onToggle,
  onEdit,
  onCancelEdit,
  onSaved,
}: PemeriksaanItemProps) {
  const petugasText =
    item.petugas.length > 0
      ? item.petugas.map((value) => value.nama).join(', ')
      : '-'

  return (
    <div className={expanded ? 'bg-slate-50/30' : 'bg-white'}>
      {/* ROW */}
      <div className="flex items-center gap-3 px-4 py-4 sm:px-5">
        <button
          type="button"
          className="grid min-w-0 flex-1 gap-3 text-left sm:grid-cols-[minmax(0,1.5fr)_140px_130px]"
          onClick={onToggle}
        >
          <div className="min-w-0">
            <div className="truncate text-sm font-semibold text-slate-900">
              {item.nama}
            </div>

            <div className="mt-1 truncate text-xs text-slate-500">
              NIK {item.nik}
            </div>
          </div>

          <div className="hidden sm:block">
            <div className="text-[11px] font-medium text-slate-400">
              Tekanan Darah
            </div>

            <div className="mt-1 text-sm font-medium tabular-nums text-slate-700">
              {formatTensi(item.tensiSistolik, item.tensiDiastolik)}
            </div>
          </div>

          <div className="hidden sm:block">
            <div className="text-[11px] font-medium text-slate-400">
              Gula Darah
            </div>

            <div className="mt-1 text-sm font-medium tabular-nums text-slate-700">
              {formatNilai(item.gulaDarah, 'mg/dL')}
            </div>
          </div>
        </button>

        {!readOnly && !editing && (
          <Button
            type="button"
            size="sm"
            variant="outline"
            className="hidden h-9 rounded-lg border-slate-200 bg-white shadow-none sm:flex"
            onClick={onEdit}
          >
            <Pencil className="size-3.5" />
            Edit
          </Button>
        )}

        <Button
          type="button"
          size="icon-sm"
          variant="ghost"
          className="shrink-0"
          onClick={onToggle}
          aria-label={expanded ? 'Tutup detail' : 'Buka detail'}
        >
          {expanded ? (
            <ChevronUp className="size-4" />
          ) : (
            <ChevronDown className="size-4" />
          )}
        </Button>
      </div>

      {/* MOBILE SUMMARY */}
      <div className="flex gap-4 px-4 pb-3 text-xs text-slate-500 sm:hidden">
        <span>
          Tensi: {formatTensi(item.tensiSistolik, item.tensiDiastolik)}
        </span>

        <span>Gula: {formatNilai(item.gulaDarah, 'mg/dL')}</span>
      </div>

      {/* EXPANDED */}
      {expanded && (
        <div className="border-t border-slate-100 bg-white px-4 py-5 sm:px-5">
          {editing ? (
            <div className="rounded-xl border border-slate-200 bg-slate-50/50 p-4 sm:p-5">
              <PemeriksaanForm
                eventId={eventId}
                anggotaId={item.anggotaId}
                petugas={petugas}
                initialValues={{
                  id: item.id,

                  tensiSistolik: item.tensiSistolik,

                  tensiDiastolik: item.tensiDiastolik,

                  gulaDarah: item.gulaDarah,

                  kolesterol: item.kolesterol,

                  asamUrat: item.asamUrat,

                  beratBadan: item.beratBadan,

                  tinggiBadan: item.tinggiBadan,

                  keluhan: item.keluhan,

                  hasilRingkas: item.hasilRingkas,
                }}
                onCancel={onCancelEdit}
                onSaved={onSaved}
              />
            </div>
          ) : (
            <>
              {!readOnly && (
                <div className="mb-4 flex justify-end sm:hidden">
                  <Button
                    type="button"
                    size="sm"
                    variant="outline"
                    onClick={onEdit}
                  >
                    <Pencil className="size-3.5" />
                    Edit Pemeriksaan
                  </Button>
                </div>
              )}

              <PemeriksaanDetail item={item} petugasText={petugasText} />
            </>
          )}
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  )
}

function PemeriksaanDetail({
  item,
  petugasText,
}: {
  item: PemeriksaanEventItem
  petugasText: string
}) {
  const createdByText = item.createdBy
    ? `${item.createdBy.nama} (${formatRole(item.createdBy.role)})`
    : '-'

  const updatedByText = item.updatedBy
    ? `${item.updatedBy.nama} (${formatRole(item.updatedBy.role)})`
    : '-'

  const hasBeenEdited = item.updatedAt !== item.createdAt

  return (
    <div className="space-y-5">
      {/* HASIL PEMERIKSAAN */}
      <div className="grid gap-3 sm:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-3">
        <Metric
          label="Tekanan Darah"
          value={formatTensi(item.tensiSistolik, item.tensiDiastolik)}
        />

        <Metric
          label="Gula Darah"
          value={formatNilai(item.gulaDarah, 'mg/dL')}
        />

        <Metric
          label="Kolesterol"
          value={formatNilai(item.kolesterol, 'mg/dL')}
        />

        <Metric label="Asam Urat" value={formatNilai(item.asamUrat, 'mg/dL')} />

        <Metric
          label="Berat Badan"
          value={formatNilai(item.beratBadan, 'kg')}
        />

        <Metric
          label="Tinggi Badan"
          value={formatNilai(item.tinggiBadan, 'cm')}
        />
      </div>

      {/* CATATAN */}
      <div className="grid gap-4 border-t border-slate-100 pt-4 lg:grid-cols-2">
        <TextDetail label="Keluhan" value={item.keluhan} />

        <TextDetail label="Hasil Ringkas" value={item.hasilRingkas} />
      </div>

      {/* PETUGAS EVENT */}
      <div className="border-t border-slate-100 pt-4">
        <TextDetail label="Petugas Event" value={petugasText} />
      </div>

      {/* AUDIT */}
      <div className="border-t border-slate-100 pt-4">
        <div className="mb-3 text-xs font-semibold uppercase tracking-[0.12em] text-slate-400">
          Riwayat Pencatatan
        </div>

        <div className="grid gap-3 sm:grid-cols-2">
          <AuditItem
            icon={<UserRound className="size-4" />}
            label="Dicatat oleh"
            value={createdByText}
            description={formatDateTime(item.createdAt)}
          />

          <AuditItem
            icon={<Clock3 className="size-4" />}
            label={
              hasBeenEdited ? 'Terakhir diubah oleh' : 'Terakhir diperbarui'
            }
            value={updatedByText}
            description={formatDateTime(item.updatedAt)}
          />
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  )
}

function Metric({ label, value }: { label: string; value: string }) {
  return (
    <div className="rounded-lg bg-slate-50 px-3.5 py-3">
      <div className="text-[11px] font-medium text-slate-400">{label}</div>

      <div className="mt-1 text-sm font-semibold tabular-nums text-slate-800">
        {value}
      </div>
    </div>
  )
}

function TextDetail({ label, value }: { label: string; value: string | null }) {
  return (
    <div>
      <div className="text-xs font-medium text-slate-400">{label}</div>

      <div className="mt-1 whitespace-pre-wrap text-sm leading-6 text-slate-700">
        {value || '-'}
      </div>
    </div>
  )
}

function AuditItem({
  icon,
  label,
  value,
  description,
}: {
  icon: React.ReactNode
  label: string
  value: string
  description: string
}) {
  return (
    <div className="rounded-xl border border-slate-200 bg-slate-50/60 px-4 py-3.5">
      <div className="flex items-start gap-3">
        <div className="flex size-9 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-lg bg-white text-slate-500 ring-1 ring-slate-200">
          {icon}
        </div>

        <div className="min-w-0">
          <div className="text-xs font-medium text-slate-400">{label}</div>

          <div className="mt-1 truncate text-sm font-semibold text-slate-800">
            {value}
          </div>

          <div className="mt-0.5 text-xs text-slate-500">{description}</div>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  )
}

function formatTensi(sistolik: number | null, diastolik: number | null) {
  if (sistolik === null && diastolik === null) {
    return '-'
  }

  return `${sistolik ?? '-'}/${diastolik ?? '-'} mmHg`
}

function formatNilai(value: number | null, suffix: string) {
  if (value === null) {
    return '-'
  }

  return `${value.toLocaleString('id-ID')} ${suffix}`
}

function formatRole(role: 'ADMIN' | 'PETUGAS') {
  return role === 'ADMIN' ? 'Administrator' : 'Petugas'
}

function formatDateTime(value: string) {
  const date = new Date(value)

  if (Number.isNaN(date.getTime())) {
    return '-'
  }

  return new Intl.DateTimeFormat('id-ID', {
    dateStyle: 'medium',
    timeStyle: 'short',
  }).format(date)
}


############################################################
FILE: src/components/kesehatan/edit-event-dialog.tsx
############################################################

import { zodResolver } from '@hookform/resolvers/zod'
import { useEffect } from 'react'
import { useForm } from 'react-hook-form'
import { toast } from 'sonner'

import { Button } from '@/components/ui/button'
import {
  Dialog,
  DialogContent,
  DialogFooter,
  DialogHeader,
  DialogTitle,
} from '@/components/ui/dialog'
import { Input } from '@/components/ui/input'

import { updateEventKesehatan } from '@/server/kesehatan'
import { updateEventKesehatanSchema } from '@/server/kesehatan/schemas'
import type { UpdateEventKesehatanInput } from '@/server/kesehatan/schemas'
import { FormField } from './form-field'

type Props = {
  open: boolean

  onOpenChange: (open: boolean) => void

  event: {
    id: number
    nama: string
    tanggal: string
    lokasi: string | null
  }

  onSaved: () => void | Promise<void>
}

export function EditEventDialog({ open, onOpenChange, event, onSaved }: Props) {
  const form = useForm<UpdateEventKesehatanInput>({
    resolver: zodResolver(updateEventKesehatanSchema),

    defaultValues: {
      id: event.id,
      nama: event.nama,
      tanggal: event.tanggal,
      lokasi: event.lokasi ?? '',
    },
  })

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!open) {
      return
    }

    form.reset({
      id: event.id,
      nama: event.nama,
      tanggal: event.tanggal,
      lokasi: event.lokasi ?? '',
    })
  }, [open, event.id, event.nama, event.tanggal, event.lokasi, form])

  async function onSubmit(values: UpdateEventKesehatanInput) {
    try {
      await updateEventKesehatan({
        data: values,
      })

      toast.success('Event berhasil diperbarui.')

      onOpenChange(false)

      await onSaved()
    } catch (error) {
      toast.error('Gagal memperbarui event', {
        description:
          error instanceof Error
            ? error.message
            : 'Terjadi kesalahan pada server.',
      })
    }
  }

  return (
    <Dialog
      open={open}
      onOpenChange={(nextOpen) => {
        if (form.formState.isSubmitting) {
          return
        }

        onOpenChange(nextOpen)
      }}
    >
      <DialogContent className="sm:max-w-lg">
        <form onSubmit={form.handleSubmit(onSubmit)}>
          <DialogHeader>
            <DialogTitle>Edit Event Kesehatan</DialogTitle>
          </DialogHeader>

          <div className="space-y-4 py-5">
            <FormField
              label="Nama Event"
              error={form.formState.errors.nama?.message}
            >
              <Input
                {...form.register('nama')}
                disabled={form.formState.isSubmitting}
                placeholder="Nama event"
              />
            </FormField>

            <FormField
              label="Tanggal"
              error={form.formState.errors.tanggal?.message}
            >
              <Input
                type="date"
                {...form.register('tanggal')}
                disabled={form.formState.isSubmitting}
              />
            </FormField>

            <FormField
              label="Lokasi"
              error={form.formState.errors.lokasi?.message}
            >
              <Input
                {...form.register('lokasi')}
                disabled={form.formState.isSubmitting}
                placeholder="Lokasi kegiatan"
              />
            </FormField>
          </div>

          <DialogFooter>
            <Button
              type="button"
              variant="outline"
              disabled={form.formState.isSubmitting}
              onClick={() => onOpenChange(false)}
            >
              Batal
            </Button>

            <Button type="submit" disabled={form.formState.isSubmitting}>
              {form.formState.isSubmitting
                ? 'Menyimpan...'
                : 'Simpan Perubahan'}
            </Button>
          </DialogFooter>
        </form>
      </DialogContent>
    </Dialog>
  )
}


############################################################
FILE: src/components/kesehatan/event-health-header.tsx
############################################################

import { CalendarHeart, MapPin, Pencil, Trash2, XCircle } from 'lucide-react'

import { Button } from '@/components/ui/button'

type Props = {
  nama: string
  tanggal: string
  lokasi: string | null
  jumlahPemeriksaan: number
  isClosed: boolean

  isClosing: boolean
  isDeleting: boolean

  onEdit: () => void
  onClose: () => void | Promise<void>
  onDelete: () => void | Promise<void>
}

export function EventHealthHeader({
  nama,
  tanggal,
  lokasi,
  jumlahPemeriksaan,
  isClosed,
  isClosing,
  isDeleting,
  onEdit,
  onClose,
  onDelete,
}: Props) {
  const deleteDisabled = isDeleting || jumlahPemeriksaan > 0

  return (
    <header className="mb-6">
      <div className="mb-2 flex items-center gap-2 text-xs font-semibold uppercase tracking-[0.14em] text-primary">
        <CalendarHeart className="size-4" />
        Event Kesehatan
      </div>

      <h1 className="text-2xl font-bold text-slate-900 sm:text-3xl">{nama}</h1>

      <div className="mt-2 flex flex-wrap gap-x-5 gap-y-1 text-sm text-muted-foreground">
        <span>{formatTanggal(tanggal)}</span>

        {lokasi && (
          <span className="flex items-center gap-1">
            <MapPin className="size-3.5" />
            {lokasi}
          </span>
        )}

        <span>{jumlahPemeriksaan.toLocaleString('id-ID')} pemeriksaan</span>
      </div>

      <div className="mt-4 flex flex-wrap gap-2">
        {isClosed ? (
          <div className="rounded-full bg-muted px-3 py-1 text-xs font-medium text-muted-foreground">
            Selesai
          </div>
        ) : (
          <>
            <Button
              type="button"
              variant="outline"
              disabled={isClosing || isDeleting}
              onClick={onEdit}
            >
              <Pencil className="size-4" />
              Edit Event
            </Button>

            <Button
              type="button"
              variant="outline"
              disabled={isClosing || isDeleting}
              onClick={() => void onClose()}
            >
              <XCircle className="size-4" />

              {isClosing ? 'Menutup...' : 'Close Event'}
            </Button>

            <Button
              type="button"
              variant="destructive"
              disabled={deleteDisabled || isClosing}
              title={
                jumlahPemeriksaan > 0
                  ? 'Event yang sudah memiliki pemeriksaan tidak dapat dihapus.'
                  : 'Hapus event'
              }
              onClick={() => void onDelete()}
            >
              <Trash2 className="size-4" />

              {isDeleting ? 'Menghapus...' : 'Delete'}
            </Button>
          </>
        )}
      </div>
    </header>
  )
}

function formatTanggal(value: string) {
  const [year, month, day] = value.split('-').map(Number)

  return new Intl.DateTimeFormat('id-ID', {
    day: 'numeric',
    month: 'long',
    year: 'numeric',
  }).format(new Date(year, month - 1, day))
}


############################################################
FILE: src/components/kesehatan/event-kesehatan-header.tsx
############################################################

import { useRouter } from '@tanstack/react-router'

import {
  CalendarDays,
  CalendarHeart,
  CheckCircle2,
  ClipboardList,
  MapPin,
  Pencil,
  Trash2,
  XCircle,
} from 'lucide-react'

import { useState } from 'react'
import { toast } from 'sonner'

import { EditEventDialog } from '@/components/kesehatan/edit-event-dialog'
import { Button } from '@/components/ui/button'

import { closeEventKesehatan, deleteEventKesehatan } from '@/server/kesehatan'

type EventData = {
  id: number
  nama: string
  tanggal: string
  lokasi: string | null
  jumlahPemeriksaan: number
  isClosed: boolean
}

type Props = {
  event: EventData
  onChanged: () => void | Promise<void>
}

export function EventKesehatanHeader({ event, onChanged }: Props) {
  const router = useRouter()

  const [editOpen, setEditOpen] = useState(false)

  const [isClosing, setIsClosing] = useState(false)

  const [isDeleting, setIsDeleting] = useState(false)

  async function handleClose() {
    const confirmed = window.confirm(
      'Tutup event ini? Setelah ditutup, data tidak dapat ditambah atau diubah.',
    )

    if (!confirmed) {
      return
    }

    setIsClosing(true)

    try {
      await closeEventKesehatan({
        data: {
          id: event.id,
        },
      })

      toast.success('Event berhasil ditutup.')

      await onChanged()
    } catch (error) {
      toast.error('Gagal menutup event', {
        description:
          error instanceof Error
            ? error.message
            : 'Terjadi kesalahan pada server.',
      })
    } finally {
      setIsClosing(false)
    }
  }

  async function handleDelete() {
    const confirmed = window.confirm(
      'Hapus event ini? Tindakan ini tidak dapat dibatalkan.',
    )

    if (!confirmed) {
      return
    }

    setIsDeleting(true)

    try {
      await deleteEventKesehatan({
        data: {
          id: event.id,
        },
      })

      toast.success('Event berhasil dihapus.')

      await router.navigate({
        to: '/event-kesehatan',
      })
    } catch (error) {
      toast.error('Gagal menghapus event', {
        description:
          error instanceof Error
            ? error.message
            : 'Terjadi kesalahan pada server.',
      })
    } finally {
      setIsDeleting(false)
    }
  }

  const deleteDisabled = isClosing || isDeleting || event.jumlahPemeriksaan > 0

  const actionDisabled = isClosing || isDeleting

  return (
    <>
      <section className="mb-6 overflow-hidden rounded-2xl border border-slate-200 bg-white shadow-sm">
        <div className="h-1 bg-linear-to-r from-blue-600 via-blue-500 to-sky-400" />

        <div className="p-5 sm:p-6">
          <div className="flex flex-col gap-5 lg:flex-row lg:items-start lg:justify-between">
            {/* INFORMASI EVENT */}
            <div className="min-w-0 flex-1">
              <div className="flex items-start gap-4">
                <div className="mt-0.5 flex size-12 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-xl bg-blue-50 text-blue-600 ring-1 ring-inset ring-blue-100">
                  <CalendarHeart className="size-5" />
                </div>

                <div className="min-w-0 flex-1">
                  <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2">
                    <span className="text-xs font-bold uppercase tracking-[0.16em] text-blue-600">
                      Event Kesehatan
                    </span>

                    {event.isClosed ? (
                      <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 rounded-full bg-slate-100 px-2.5 py-1 text-[11px] font-medium text-slate-600">
                        <CheckCircle2 className="size-3.5" />
                        Selesai
                      </span>
                    ) : (
                      <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 rounded-full bg-emerald-50 px-2.5 py-1 text-[11px] font-medium text-emerald-700">
                        <span className="size-1.5 rounded-full bg-emerald-500" />
                        Aktif
                      </span>
                    )}
                  </div>

                  <h1 className="mt-2 text-2xl font-bold tracking-tight text-slate-900 sm:text-[28px]">
                    {event.nama}
                  </h1>

                  <div className="mt-4 flex flex-wrap gap-x-5 gap-y-2 text-sm text-slate-500">
                    <span className="flex items-center gap-1.5">
                      <CalendarDays className="size-4 text-slate-400" />

                      {formatTanggal(event.tanggal)}
                    </span>

                    {event.lokasi && (
                      <span className="flex min-w-0 items-center gap-1.5">
                        <MapPin className="size-4 shrink-0 text-slate-400" />

                        <span className="truncate">{event.lokasi}</span>
                      </span>
                    )}

                    <span className="flex items-center gap-1.5">
                      <ClipboardList className="size-4 text-slate-400" />
                      {event.jumlahPemeriksaan.toLocaleString('id-ID')}{' '}
                      pemeriksaan
                    </span>
                  </div>
                </div>
              </div>
            </div>

            {/* ACTION */}
            {!event.isClosed && (
              <div className="flex w-full shrink-0 flex-col gap-2 sm:flex-row lg:w-auto lg:flex-col xl:flex-row">
                <Button
                  type="button"
                  variant="outline"
                  className="h-10 w-full rounded-lg border-slate-200 bg-white px-4 text-slate-700 shadow-none hover:bg-slate-50 sm:w-auto"
                  disabled={actionDisabled}
                  onClick={() => setEditOpen(true)}
                >
                  <Pencil className="size-4" />
                  Edit Event
                </Button>

                <Button
                  type="button"
                  variant="outline"
                  className="h-10 w-full rounded-lg border-slate-200 bg-white px-4 text-slate-700 shadow-none hover:bg-slate-50 sm:w-auto"
                  disabled={actionDisabled}
                  onClick={() => void handleClose()}
                >
                  <XCircle className="size-4" />

                  {isClosing ? 'Menutup...' : 'Tutup Event'}
                </Button>

                <Button
                  type="button"
                  variant="destructive"
                  className="h-10 w-full rounded-lg px-4 sm:w-auto"
                  disabled={deleteDisabled}
                  title={
                    event.jumlahPemeriksaan > 0
                      ? 'Event yang sudah memiliki pemeriksaan tidak dapat dihapus.'
                      : 'Hapus event'
                  }
                  onClick={() => void handleDelete()}
                >
                  <Trash2 className="size-4" />

                  {isDeleting ? 'Menghapus...' : 'Hapus'}
                </Button>
              </div>
            )}
          </div>

          {!event.isClosed && event.jumlahPemeriksaan > 0 && (
            <div className="mt-5 border-t border-slate-100 pt-4 text-xs leading-5 text-slate-400">
              Event yang sudah memiliki data pemeriksaan tidak dapat dihapus.
              Gunakan Tutup Event setelah kegiatan selesai.
            </div>
          )}
        </div>
      </section>

      {!event.isClosed && (
        <EditEventDialog
          open={editOpen}
          onOpenChange={setEditOpen}
          event={{
            id: event.id,
            nama: event.nama,
            tanggal: event.tanggal,
            lokasi: event.lokasi,
          }}
          onSaved={onChanged}
        />
      )}
    </>
  )
}

function formatTanggal(value: string) {
  const [year, month, day] = value.split('-').map(Number)

  return new Intl.DateTimeFormat('id-ID', {
    day: 'numeric',
    month: 'long',
    year: 'numeric',
  }).format(new Date(year, month - 1, day))
}


############################################################
FILE: src/components/kesehatan/event-list-toolbar.tsx
############################################################

import { Search, RotateCcw, X } from 'lucide-react'

import { useEffect, useState } from 'react'

import { Button } from '@/components/ui/button'
import { Input } from '@/components/ui/input'

import {
  Select,
  SelectContent,
  SelectItem,
  SelectTrigger,
  SelectValue,
} from '@/components/ui/select'

export type EventStatus = 'all' | 'aktif' | 'selesai'

type Props = {
  searchValue: string

  status: EventStatus

  tahun?: number

  onSearchChange: (value: string) => void

  onStatusChange: (value: EventStatus) => void

  onYearChange: (value: number | undefined) => void

  onReset: () => void
}

export function EventListToolbar({
  searchValue,
  status,
  tahun,

  onSearchChange,
  onStatusChange,
  onYearChange,
  onReset,
}: Props) {
  const [query, setQuery] = useState(searchValue)

  const [yearInput, setYearInput] = useState(tahun ? String(tahun) : '')

  useEffect(() => {
    setQuery(searchValue)
  }, [searchValue])

  useEffect(() => {
    setYearInput(tahun ? String(tahun) : '')
  }, [tahun])

  useEffect(() => {
    if (query === searchValue) {
      return
    }

    const timeout = window.setTimeout(() => {
      onSearchChange(query.trim())
    }, 400)

    return () => {
      window.clearTimeout(timeout)
    }
  }, [query, searchValue, onSearchChange])

  const hasFilter =
    query.trim().length > 0 || status !== 'all' || Boolean(tahun)

  function clearSearch() {
    setQuery('')
    onSearchChange('')
  }

  function commitYear() {
    const value = yearInput.trim()

    if (!value) {
      onYearChange(undefined)

      return
    }

    const year = Number(value)

    if (Number.isInteger(year) && year >= 2000 && year <= 2100) {
      onYearChange(year)

      return
    }

    setYearInput(tahun ? String(tahun) : '')
  }

  function handleReset() {
    setQuery('')
    setYearInput('')

    onReset()
  }

  return (
    <div className="flex flex-col gap-3 xl:flex-row xl:items-center">
      <div className="relative min-w-0 flex-1">
        <Search className="pointer-events-none absolute left-3 top-1/2 size-4 -translate-y-1/2 text-slate-400" />

        <Input
          value={query}
          onChange={(event) => setQuery(event.target.value)}
          placeholder="Cari nama event atau lokasi..."
          className="h-10 rounded-lg border-slate-200 bg-white pl-9 pr-10 shadow-none"
        />

        {query && (
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={clearSearch}
            className="absolute right-2 top-1/2 flex size-7 -translate-y-1/2 items-center justify-center rounded-md text-slate-400 transition hover:bg-slate-100 hover:text-slate-700"
            aria-label="Hapus pencarian"
          >
            <X className="size-4" />
          </button>
        )}
      </div>

      <Select
        value={status}
        onValueChange={(value) => {
          if (value === 'all' || value === 'aktif' || value === 'selesai') {
            onStatusChange(value)
          }
        }}
      >
        <SelectTrigger className="w-full rounded-lg border-slate-200 bg-white shadow-none data-[size=default]:h-10 sm:w-36">
          <SelectValue />
        </SelectTrigger>

        <SelectContent>
          <SelectItem value="all">Semua status</SelectItem>

          <SelectItem value="aktif">Aktif</SelectItem>

          <SelectItem value="selesai">Selesai</SelectItem>
        </SelectContent>
      </Select>

      <Input
        value={yearInput}
        inputMode="numeric"
        maxLength={4}
        placeholder="Tahun"
        onChange={(event) => {
          const value = event.target.value.replace(/\D/g, '')

          setYearInput(value)
        }}
        onBlur={commitYear}
        onKeyDown={(event) => {
          if (event.key === 'Enter') {
            event.currentTarget.blur()
          }
        }}
        className="h-10 w-full rounded-lg border-slate-200 bg-white shadow-none sm:w-28"
      />

      {hasFilter && (
        <Button
          type="button"
          variant="outline"
          className="h-10 rounded-lg border-slate-200 bg-white"
          onClick={handleReset}
        >
          <RotateCcw className="size-4" />
          Reset
        </Button>
      )}
    </div>
  )
}


############################################################
FILE: src/components/kesehatan/event-list.tsx
