import {
  ArrowLeft,
  CheckCircle2,
  LoaderCircle,
  MapPin,
  Search,
  SearchX,
  UserRound,
  UsersRound,
  X,
} from 'lucide-react'
import { useRef, useState } from 'react'

import { SelectedWargaKesehatan } from '@/components/kesehatan/selected-warga-kesehatan'
import { useWargaKesehatanSearch } from '@/components/kesehatan/use-warga-kesehatan-search'

import type { WargaKesehatan } from '@/components/kesehatan/use-warga-kesehatan-search'

import { Button } from '@/components/ui/button'
import { Card, CardContent, CardHeader, CardTitle } from '@/components/ui/card'
import { Input } from '@/components/ui/input'

type PetugasEventItem = {
  id: number
  nama: string
}

type Props = {
  eventId: number
  petugas: PetugasEventItem[]
  refreshKey: number
  onChanged: () => void | Promise<void>
}

const MAX_VISIBLE_RESULTS = 10

export function WargaKesehatanSection({
  eventId,
  petugas,
  refreshKey,
  onChanged,
}: Props) {
  const searchInputRef = useRef<HTMLInputElement>(null)

  const { q, setQ, results, isSearching, hasSearched, error, markAsExamined } =
    useWargaKesehatanSearch({
      eventId,
      refreshKey,
    })

  const [selected, setSelected] = useState<WargaKesehatan | null>(null)

  const [savedWargaName, setSavedWargaName] = useState<string | null>(null)

  async function handleSaved() {
    if (!selected) {
      return
    }

    const anggotaId = selected.id
    const wargaName = selected.nama

    /*
     * Tandai hasil pencarian lokal terlebih dahulu
     * supaya warga langsung terlihat sudah diperiksa.
     */
    markAsExamined(anggotaId)

    /*
     * Siapkan workflow untuk warga berikutnya.
     */
    setSelected(null)
    setQ('')
    setSavedWargaName(wargaName)

    await onChanged()

    /*
     * Setelah kembali ke pencarian, langsung fokus
     * sehingga petugas dapat mengetik warga berikutnya.
     */
    requestAnimationFrame(() => {
      searchInputRef.current?.focus()
    })
  }

  function handleSelect(warga: WargaKesehatan) {
    if (warga.sudahDiperiksa) {
      return
    }

    setSavedWargaName(null)
    setSelected(warga)
  }

  function handleChangeWarga() {
    setSelected(null)

    requestAnimationFrame(() => {
      searchInputRef.current?.focus()
    })
  }

  function handleSearchChange(value: string) {
    setSavedWargaName(null)
    setQ(value)
  }

  function handleClearSearch() {
    setQ('')

    requestAnimationFrame(() => {
      searchInputRef.current?.focus()
    })
  }

  const query = q.trim()

  const showEmpty =
    query.length > 0 &&
    hasSearched &&
    !isSearching &&
    results.length === 0 &&
    !error

  const visibleResults = results.slice(0, MAX_VISIBLE_RESULTS)

  const availableCount = results.filter((warga) => !warga.sudahDiperiksa).length

  return (
    <section className="space-y-4">
      {/* SUCCESS */}
      {savedWargaName ? (
        <div className="flex items-start gap-3 rounded-2xl border border-green-200 bg-green-50 px-4 py-4 text-green-800 shadow-sm sm:px-5">
          <div className="flex size-10 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-xl bg-green-100 text-green-700">
            <CheckCircle2 className="size-5" />
          </div>

          <div className="min-w-0">
            <div className="text-sm font-semibold">
              Pemeriksaan berhasil disimpan
            </div>

            <p className="mt-1 text-sm leading-6 text-green-700">
              Data pemeriksaan{' '}
              <span className="font-semibold">{savedWargaName}</span> telah
              tersimpan. Silakan cari warga berikutnya.
            </p>
          </div>
        </div>
      ) : null}

      {/* SEARCH */}
      <Card
        className={[
          'overflow-hidden rounded-2xl border-slate-200 bg-white shadow-sm',
          selected ? 'hidden lg:block' : '',
        ].join(' ')}
      >
        <CardHeader className="border-b border-slate-200 px-5 py-4 sm:px-6">
          <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
            <div className="flex size-10 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-xl bg-blue-50 text-blue-600">
              <Search className="size-4" />
            </div>

            <div className="min-w-0">
              <CardTitle className="text-base font-semibold text-slate-900">
                Cari Warga
              </CardTitle>

              <p className="mt-0.5 text-xs text-slate-500">
                Cari warga yang akan menjalani pemeriksaan.
              </p>
            </div>
          </div>
        </CardHeader>

        <CardContent className="p-5 sm:p-6">
          <div className="relative">
            <Search className="pointer-events-none absolute left-3 top-1/2 size-4 -translate-y-1/2 text-slate-400" />

            <Input
              ref={searchInputRef}
              value={q}
              autoComplete="off"
              placeholder="Ketik nama atau NIK lengkap..."
              className="h-11 rounded-lg border-slate-200 bg-white pl-9 pr-11 shadow-none"
              onChange={(event) => handleSearchChange(event.target.value)}
              autoFocus
            />

            <div className="absolute right-3 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2">
              {isSearching ? (
                <LoaderCircle className="size-4 animate-spin text-slate-400" />
              ) : q ? (
                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={handleClearSearch}
                  className="flex size-6 items-center justify-center rounded-md text-slate-400 transition-colors hover:bg-slate-100 hover:text-slate-700"
                  aria-label="Hapus pencarian"
                >
                  <X className="size-4" />
                </button>
              ) : null}
            </div>
          </div>

          <div className="mt-2 flex flex-col gap-1 text-xs leading-5 text-slate-400 sm:flex-row sm:items-center sm:justify-between">
            <p>
              Nama dapat diketik sebagian. Untuk NIK, masukkan nomor lengkap.
            </p>

            {query && results.length > 0 ? (
              <p className="shrink-0">
                {availableCount.toLocaleString('id-ID')} belum diperiksa
              </p>
            ) : null}
          </div>
        </CardContent>
      </Card>

      {/* ERROR */}
      {error ? (
        <div className="rounded-xl border border-red-200 bg-red-50/60 px-4 py-3 text-sm text-red-700">
          {error}
        </div>
      ) : null}

      {/* MOBILE: KEMBALI */}
      {selected ? (
        <div className="lg:hidden">
          <Button
            type="button"
            variant="outline"
            className="h-10 rounded-lg border-slate-200 bg-white"
            onClick={handleChangeWarga}
          >
            <ArrowLeft className="size-4" />
            Ganti warga
          </Button>
        </div>
      ) : null}

      {/* WORKSPACE */}
      <div className="grid gap-4 lg:grid-cols-[340px_minmax(0,1fr)] lg:items-start">
        {/* HASIL PENCARIAN */}
        <div className={selected ? 'hidden lg:block' : 'block'}>
          {visibleResults.length > 0 ? (
            <Card className="overflow-hidden rounded-2xl border-slate-200 bg-white shadow-sm lg:sticky lg:top-24">
              <CardHeader className="border-b border-slate-200 px-4 py-4">
                <div className="flex items-center justify-between gap-3">
                  <div className="min-w-0">
                    <CardTitle className="text-sm font-semibold text-slate-900">
                      Hasil Pencarian
                    </CardTitle>

                    <p className="mt-0.5 text-xs text-slate-500">
                      Pilih warga yang akan diperiksa.
                    </p>
                  </div>

                  <span className="shrink-0 rounded-md bg-slate-100 px-2 py-1 text-[11px] font-medium text-slate-500">
                    {results.length.toLocaleString('id-ID')} hasil
                  </span>
                </div>
              </CardHeader>

              <CardContent className="p-0">
                <div className="divide-y divide-slate-100">
                  {visibleResults.map((warga) => {
                    const isSelected = selected?.id === warga.id

                    const isDisabled = warga.sudahDiperiksa

                    return (
                      <button
                        key={warga.id}
                        type="button"
                        disabled={isDisabled}
                        onClick={() => handleSelect(warga)}
                        className={[
                          'flex w-full items-start gap-3 px-4 py-3.5 text-left transition-colors',
                          isDisabled
                            ? 'cursor-not-allowed bg-slate-50/80 opacity-65'
                            : isSelected
                              ? 'bg-blue-50/70'
                              : 'hover:bg-slate-50',
                        ].join(' ')}
                      >
                        <div
                          className={[
                            'mt-0.5 flex size-9 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-lg',
                            isDisabled
                              ? 'bg-green-50 text-green-600'
                              : isSelected
                                ? 'bg-blue-100 text-blue-600'
                                : 'bg-slate-100 text-slate-500',
                          ].join(' ')}
                        >
                          {isDisabled ? (
                            <CheckCircle2 className="size-4" />
                          ) : (
                            <UserRound className="size-4" />
                          )}
                        </div>

                        <div className="min-w-0 flex-1">
                          <div className="flex items-start justify-between gap-2">
                            <span className="truncate text-sm font-semibold text-slate-900">
                              {warga.nama}
                            </span>

                            {isDisabled ? (
                              <span className="shrink-0 rounded-md bg-green-50 px-2 py-0.5 text-[10px] font-semibold text-green-700">
                                Selesai
                              </span>
                            ) : null}
                          </div>

                          <div className="mt-1 truncate text-xs text-slate-500">
                            NIK {warga.nik}
                          </div>

                          {warga.alamat || warga.desaKelurahan ? (
                            <div className="mt-1.5 flex min-w-0 items-center gap-1 text-xs text-slate-400">
                              <MapPin className="size-3 shrink-0" />

                              <span className="truncate">
                                {[warga.alamat, warga.desaKelurahan]
                                  .filter(Boolean)
                                  .join(', ')}
                              </span>
                            </div>
                          ) : null}
                        </div>
                      </button>
                    )
                  })}
                </div>

                {results.length > MAX_VISIBLE_RESULTS ? (
                  <div className="border-t border-slate-100 bg-slate-50/60 px-4 py-3 text-center text-xs leading-5 text-slate-500">
                    Menampilkan {MAX_VISIBLE_RESULTS} dari{' '}
                    {results.length.toLocaleString('id-ID')} hasil. Persempit
                    pencarian untuk menemukan warga lainnya.
                  </div>
                ) : null}
              </CardContent>
            </Card>
          ) : showEmpty ? (
            <div className="rounded-2xl border border-dashed border-slate-200 bg-white px-5 py-10 text-center">
              <div className="mx-auto flex size-11 items-center justify-center rounded-xl bg-slate-100 text-slate-400">
                <SearchX className="size-4" />
              </div>

              <div className="mt-3 text-sm font-semibold text-slate-700">
                Warga tidak ditemukan
              </div>

              <p className="mt-1 text-xs leading-5 text-slate-500">
                Periksa kembali nama atau NIK yang dimasukkan.
              </p>
            </div>
          ) : (
            <div className="hidden rounded-2xl border border-dashed border-slate-200 bg-white px-5 py-10 text-center lg:block">
              <div className="mx-auto flex size-11 items-center justify-center rounded-xl bg-slate-100 text-slate-400">
                <UsersRound className="size-4" />
              </div>

              <div className="mt-3 text-sm font-semibold text-slate-700">
                Cari warga
              </div>

              <p className="mt-1 text-xs leading-5 text-slate-500">
                Ketik nama atau NIK pada pencarian di atas.
              </p>
            </div>
          )}
        </div>

        {/* FORM PEMERIKSAAN */}
        <div className={selected ? 'block' : 'hidden lg:block'}>
          {selected ? (
            <SelectedWargaKesehatan
              eventId={eventId}
              warga={selected}
              petugas={petugas}
              refreshKey={refreshKey}
              onSaved={handleSaved}
            />
          ) : (
            <div className="flex min-h-72 items-center justify-center rounded-2xl border border-dashed border-slate-200 bg-white px-6 py-12 text-center">
              <div>
                <div className="mx-auto flex size-12 items-center justify-center rounded-xl bg-blue-50 text-blue-500">
                  <UserRound className="size-5" />
                </div>

                <div className="mt-4 font-semibold text-slate-800">
                  Pilih warga
                </div>

                <p className="mx-auto mt-1 max-w-sm text-sm leading-6 text-slate-500">
                  Pilih salah satu warga dari hasil pencarian untuk membuka form
                  pemeriksaan.
                </p>
              </div>
            </div>
          )}
        </div>
      </div>
    </section>
  )
}


############################################################
FILE: src/components/kk/create/create-kk-dialog.tsx
############################################################

import { zodResolver } from '@hookform/resolvers/zod'
import { IdCard, Loader2, Save } from 'lucide-react'
import { useEffect } from 'react'
import { useForm } from 'react-hook-form'
import { toast } from 'sonner'

import { Button } from '@/components/ui/button'
import {
  Dialog,
  DialogContent,
  DialogDescription,
  DialogFooter,
  DialogHeader,
  DialogTitle,
} from '@/components/ui/dialog'

import { kkSchema } from '@/lib/kk-parser/schema'
import type { ValidatedKK } from '@/lib/kk-parser/schema'

import { saveKK } from '@/server/kk.functions'

import { createEmptyKK, normalizeKKFormValues } from '../form/kk-form-defaults'

import { KKFormFields } from '../form/kk-form-fields'

type Props = {
  open: boolean

  onOpenChange: (open: boolean) => void

  onCreated?: () => void | Promise<void>

  initialValues?: ValidatedKK | null
}

export function CreateKKDialog({
  open,
  onOpenChange,
  onCreated,
  initialValues,
}: Props) {
  const form = useForm<ValidatedKK>({
    resolver: zodResolver(kkSchema),

    defaultValues: createEmptyKK(),
  })

  const {
    reset,
    handleSubmit,

    formState: { isSubmitting },
  } = form

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!open) {
      return
    }

    reset(initialValues ?? createEmptyKK())
  }, [open, initialValues, reset])

  function resetForm() {
    reset(createEmptyKK())
  }

  function handleOpenChange(nextOpen: boolean) {
    if (!nextOpen && isSubmitting) {
      return
    }

    onOpenChange(nextOpen)
  }

  async function onSubmit(values: ValidatedKK) {
    const normalizedValues = normalizeKKFormValues(values)

    try {
      const response = await saveKK({
        data: normalizedValues,
      })

      toast.success('Data berhasil disimpan', {
        description: `${response.memberCount.toLocaleString(
          'id-ID',
        )} anggota keluarga berhasil disimpan.`,
      })

      resetForm()

      onOpenChange(false)

      await onCreated?.()
    } catch (error) {
      const message =
        error instanceof Error
          ? error.message
          : 'Terjadi kesalahan pada server.'

      toast.error('Gagal menyimpan data', {
        description: message,
      })
    }
  }

  return (
    <Dialog open={open} onOpenChange={handleOpenChange}>
      <DialogContent
        className="
          flex
          max-h-[92dvh]
          w-[calc(100vw-1.5rem)]
          max-w-[calc(100vw-1.5rem)]
          flex-col
          gap-0
          overflow-hidden
          rounded-2xl
          border-slate-200
          bg-white
          p-0
          shadow-xl
          sm:w-[calc(100vw-3rem)]
          sm:max-w-5xl
        "
      >
        {/* HEADER */}
        <DialogHeader className="shrink-0 border-b border-slate-200 bg-white px-5 py-4 pr-12 sm:px-6 sm:py-5">
          <div className="flex items-start gap-3">
            <div className="flex size-10 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-xl bg-blue-50 text-blue-600">
              <IdCard className="size-4" />
            </div>

            <div className="min-w-0">
              <DialogTitle className="text-base font-semibold text-slate-900 sm:text-lg">
                Tambah Kartu Keluarga
              </DialogTitle>

              <DialogDescription className="mt-1 max-w-2xl text-xs leading-5 text-slate-500 sm:text-sm">
                Masukkan data Kartu Keluarga beserta anggota keluarga yang
                terdaftar di dalamnya.
              </DialogDescription>
            </div>
          </div>
        </DialogHeader>

        {/* FORM AREA */}
        <form
          id="create-kk-form"
          onSubmit={handleSubmit(onSubmit)}
          className="min-h-0 flex-1 overflow-x-hidden overflow-y-auto bg-slate-50/40"
        >
          <div className="p-4 sm:p-6">
            <KKFormFields form={form} disabled={isSubmitting} />
          </div>
        </form>

        {/* FOOTER */}
        <DialogFooter className="shrink-0 border-t border-slate-200 bg-white px-4 pt-4 pb-5 sm:px-6 sm:pt-4 sm:pb-6">
          <div className="flex w-full flex-col-reverse gap-2 sm:flex-row sm:justify-end">
            <Button
              type="button"
              variant="outline"
              className="h-10 rounded-lg border-slate-200 bg-white px-5 shadow-none"
              disabled={isSubmitting}
              onClick={() => handleOpenChange(false)}
            >
              Batal
            </Button>

            <Button
              type="submit"
              form="create-kk-form"
              className="h-10 rounded-lg px-5 sm:min-w-40"
              disabled={isSubmitting}
            >
              {isSubmitting ? (
                <>
                  <Loader2 className="size-4 animate-spin" />
                  Menyimpan...
                </>
              ) : (
                <>
                  <Save className="size-4" />
                  Simpan Data
                </>
              )}
            </Button>
          </div>
        </DialogFooter>
      </DialogContent>
    </Dialog>
  )
}


############################################################
FILE: src/components/kk/detail/gender-badge.tsx
############################################################

import { Badge } from '@/components/ui/badge'

type Props = {
  value: string | null
}

export function GenderBadge({ value }: Props) {
  if (!value) {
    return <span className="text-sm text-slate-400">-</span>
  }

  const normalized = value.trim().toLowerCase()

  const isMale = normalized === 'laki-laki' || normalized === 'l'

  const isFemale = normalized === 'perempuan' || normalized === 'p'

  return (
    <Badge
      variant="outline"
      className={[
        'whitespace-nowrap rounded-full px-2.5 py-1 text-[11px] font-medium shadow-none',
        isMale
          ? 'border-blue-200 bg-blue-50 text-blue-700'
          : isFemale
            ? 'border-rose-200 bg-rose-50 text-rose-700'
            : 'border-slate-200 bg-slate-50 text-slate-600',
      ].join(' ')}
    >
      {value}
    </Badge>
  )
}

type Props = {
  label: string
  value: string | null
  mono?: boolean
}

export function InfoItem({ label, value, mono = false }: Props) {
  return (
    <div className="min-w-0 px-5 py-4 sm:px-6">
      <div className="text-xs font-medium text-slate-400">{label}</div>

      <div
        className={[
          'mt-1 min-w-0 text-sm font-semibold leading-6 text-slate-900',
          mono ? 'font-mono tabular-nums' : '',
        ].join(' ')}
      >
        {value || '-'}
      </div>
    </div>
  )
}

import { Link } from '@tanstack/react-router'
import {
  ArrowLeft,
  Eye,
  EyeOff,
  IdCard,
  Loader2,
  MapPin,
  Pencil,
  ShieldAlert,
  ShieldCheck,
  Users,
} from 'lucide-react'

import { Button } from '@/components/ui/button'

type Props = {
  kepalaKeluarga: string | null
  desaKelurahan: string | null
  kecamatan: string | null
  jumlahAnggota: number

  sensitiveVisible: boolean
  sensitiveLoading: boolean

  onEdit: () => void
  onToggleSensitive: () => void
}

export function KKDetailHeader({
  kepalaKeluarga,
  desaKelurahan,
  kecamatan,
  jumlahAnggota,
  sensitiveVisible,
  sensitiveLoading,
  onEdit,
  onToggleSensitive,
}: Props) {
  const lokasi = [desaKelurahan, kecamatan].filter(Boolean).join(', ')

  return (
    <>
      {/* BACK */}
      <div className="mb-4">
        <Button
          variant="ghost"
          size="sm"
          className="-ml-2 h-9 rounded-lg px-2 text-slate-500 hover:bg-white hover:text-slate-900"
          asChild
        >
          <Link to="/data-kk">
            <ArrowLeft className="size-4" />
            Kembali ke Data KK
          </Link>
        </Button>
      </div>

      {/* HEADER */}
      <section className="mb-6 overflow-hidden rounded-2xl border border-slate-200 bg-white shadow-sm">
        <div className="h-1 bg-linear-to-r from-blue-600 via-blue-500 to-sky-400" />

        <div className="p-5 sm:p-6">
          <div className="flex flex-col gap-5 lg:flex-row lg:items-start lg:justify-between">
            {/* INFORMASI */}
            <div className="min-w-0 flex-1">
              <div className="flex items-start gap-4">
                <div className="mt-0.5 flex size-12 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-xl bg-blue-50 text-blue-600 ring-1 ring-inset ring-blue-100">
                  <IdCard className="size-5" />
                </div>

                <div className="min-w-0 flex-1">
                  <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2">
                    <span className="text-xs font-bold uppercase tracking-[0.16em] text-blue-600">
                      Kartu Keluarga
                    </span>

                    {sensitiveVisible ? (
                      <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 rounded-full bg-red-50 px-2.5 py-1 text-[11px] font-medium text-red-700">
                        <ShieldAlert className="size-3.5" />
                        Data sensitif terbuka
                      </span>
                    ) : (
                      <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 rounded-full bg-slate-100 px-2.5 py-1 text-[11px] font-medium text-slate-600">
                        <ShieldCheck className="size-3.5" />
                        Data sensitif terlindungi
                      </span>
                    )}
                  </div>

                  <h1 className="mt-2 text-2xl font-bold tracking-tight text-slate-900 sm:text-[28px]">
                    {kepalaKeluarga ?? 'Kartu Keluarga'}
                  </h1>

                  <div className="mt-4 flex flex-wrap gap-x-5 gap-y-2 text-sm text-slate-500">
                    <span className="flex min-w-0 items-center gap-1.5">
                      <MapPin className="size-4 shrink-0 text-slate-400" />

                      <span className="truncate">
                        {lokasi || 'Lokasi belum tersedia'}
                      </span>
                    </span>

                    <span className="flex items-center gap-1.5">
                      <Users className="size-4 text-slate-400" />
                      {jumlahAnggota.toLocaleString('id-ID')} anggota keluarga
                    </span>
                  </div>
                </div>
              </div>
            </div>

            {/* ACTIONS */}
            <div className="flex w-full shrink-0 flex-col gap-2 sm:flex-row lg:w-auto">
              <Button
                type="button"
                variant="outline"
                className="h-10 w-full rounded-lg border-slate-200 bg-white px-4 text-slate-700 shadow-none hover:bg-slate-50 sm:w-auto"
                disabled={sensitiveLoading}
                onClick={onEdit}
              >
                <Pencil className="size-4" />
                Edit Data
              </Button>

              <Button
                type="button"
                variant="outline"
                className={[
                  'h-10 w-full rounded-lg px-4 shadow-none sm:w-auto',
                  sensitiveVisible
                    ? 'border-red-200 bg-red-50 text-red-700 hover:bg-red-100 hover:text-red-800'
                    : 'border-slate-200 bg-white text-slate-700 hover:bg-slate-50',
                ].join(' ')}
                disabled={sensitiveLoading}
                onClick={onToggleSensitive}
              >
                {sensitiveLoading ? (
                  <Loader2 className="size-4 animate-spin" />
                ) : sensitiveVisible ? (
                  <EyeOff className="size-4" />
                ) : (
                  <Eye className="size-4" />
                )}

                {sensitiveLoading
                  ? 'Memuat...'
                  : sensitiveVisible
                    ? 'Sembunyikan Data Sensitif'
                    : 'Tampilkan Data Sensitif'}
              </Button>
            </div>
          </div>

          {/* PRIVACY NOTICE */}
          {sensitiveVisible && (
            <div className="mt-5 flex items-start gap-2.5 border-t border-slate-100 pt-4 text-xs leading-5 text-red-600">
              <ShieldAlert className="mt-0.5 size-3.5 shrink-0" />

              <span>
                Nomor KK dan NIK sedang ditampilkan. Sembunyikan kembali data
                sensitif setelah selesai digunakan.
              </span>
            </div>
          )}
        </div>
      </section>
    </>
  )
}


############################################################
FILE: src/components/kk/detail/kk-info-card.tsx

import { IdCard, MapPin } from 'lucide-react'

import { Card, CardContent, CardHeader, CardTitle } from '@/components/ui/card'

import { InfoItem } from './info-item'

type Props = {
  noKK: string

  kepalaKeluarga: string | null
  alamat: string | null

  rt: string | null
  rw: string | null

  desaKelurahan: string | null
  kecamatan: string | null
  kabupatenKota: string | null
  kodePos: string | null
  provinsi: string | null
}

export function KKInfoCard({
  noKK,
  kepalaKeluarga,
  alamat,
  rt,
  rw,
  desaKelurahan,
  kecamatan,
  kabupatenKota,
  kodePos,
  provinsi,
}: Props) {
  const rtRw = rt || rw ? `${rt ?? '-'} / ${rw ?? '-'}` : null

  return (
    <Card className="overflow-hidden rounded-2xl border-slate-200 bg-white shadow-sm">
      <CardHeader className="border-b border-slate-200 px-5 py-4 sm:px-6">
        <div className="flex items-start gap-3">
          <div className="flex size-10 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-xl bg-blue-50 text-blue-600">
            <IdCard className="size-4" />
          </div>

          <div className="min-w-0">
            <CardTitle className="text-base font-semibold text-slate-900">
              Informasi Kartu Keluarga
            </CardTitle>

            <p className="mt-0.5 text-xs leading-5 text-slate-500">
              Identitas utama dan alamat keluarga
            </p>
          </div>
        </div>
      </CardHeader>

      <CardContent className="p-0">
        {/* IDENTITAS UTAMA */}
        <div className="grid border-b border-slate-100 md:grid-cols-2">
          <InfoItem label="Nomor KK" value={noKK} mono />

          <InfoItem label="Kepala Keluarga" value={kepalaKeluarga} />
        </div>

        {/* ALAMAT */}
        <div className="px-5 py-4 sm:px-6">
          <div className="mb-3 flex items-center gap-2 text-xs font-semibold uppercase tracking-[0.12em] text-slate-400">
            <MapPin className="size-3.5" />
            Alamat
          </div>

          <div className="grid gap-x-6 gap-y-5 sm:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-3">
            <InfoBlock
              label="Alamat"
              value={alamat}
              className="sm:col-span-2 lg:col-span-2"
            />

            <InfoBlock label="RT / RW" value={rtRw} />

            <InfoBlock label="Desa / Kelurahan" value={desaKelurahan} />

            <InfoBlock label="Kecamatan" value={kecamatan} />

            <InfoBlock label="Kabupaten / Kota" value={kabupatenKota} />

            <InfoBlock label="Provinsi" value={provinsi} />

            <InfoBlock label="Kode Pos" value={kodePos} />
          </div>
        </div>
      </CardContent>
    </Card>
  )
}

function InfoBlock({
  label,
  value,
  className = '',
}: {
  label: string
  value: string | null
  className?: string
}) {
  return (
    <div className={className}>
      <div className="text-xs font-medium text-slate-400">{label}</div>

      <div className="mt-1 text-sm font-medium leading-6 text-slate-800">
        {value || '-'}
      </div>
    </div>
  )
}

type Props = {
  member: KKMemberView
  nik: string

export function KKMemberMobile({ member, nik, sensitiveVisible }: Props) {
  return (
    <article className="px-5 py-5">
      {/* IDENTITAS */}
      <div className="flex items-start justify-between gap-4">

      {/* NIK */}

        className={[
          'mt-4 rounded-xl border px-3.5 py-3',
          sensitiveVisible
            ? 'border-amber-200 bg-amber-50/70'
            : 'border-slate-200 bg-slate-50',
        ].join(' ')}
      >
        <div
          className={[
            'text-[11px] font-medium',

          ].join(' ')}

        </div>

          className={[
            'mt-1 break-all font-mono text-sm font-semibold tabular-nums',
            sensitiveVisible ? 'text-amber-900' : 'text-slate-700',
          ].join(' ')}
        >
          {nik}
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* DATA PRIBADI */}
      <div className="mt-5 grid grid-cols-2 gap-x-5 gap-y-4">
        <MobileInfo label="Tempat Lahir" value={member.tempatLahir} />

        <div>
          <div className="text-xs font-medium text-slate-400">

          </div>

          <div className="mt-1 flex items-center gap-1.5 text-sm font-medium text-slate-700">
            <CalendarDays className="size-3.5 shrink-0 text-slate-400" />

            {member.tanggalLahir ?? '-'}
          </div>
        </div>

        <MobileInfo label="Agama" value={member.agama} />

        <MobileInfo label="Gol. Darah" value={member.golonganDarah} />

        <div className="col-span-2 border-t border-slate-100 pt-4">

          <MobileInfo label="Pekerjaan" value={member.pekerjaan} />
        </div>

        <div className="col-span-2 mt-1 border-t border-slate-100 pt-4">
          <div className="mb-3 text-[11px] font-semibold uppercase tracking-[0.12em] text-slate-400">
            Keluarga & Data Gerejawi
          </div>
        </div>

        <MobileInfo label="Ayah" value={member.ayah} />

        <MobileInfo label="Ibu" value={member.ibu} />

        <MobileInfo label="Domisili" value={member.domisili} />

        <MobileInfo label="Baptis Anak" value={member.baptisAnak} />

        <MobileInfo
          label="Sidi / Baptis Dewasa"
          value={member.sidiBaptisDewasa}
        />

}

function MobileInfo({ label, value }: { label: string; value: string | null }) {

      <div className="mt-1 text-sm font-medium leading-6 text-slate-700">

type Props = {

export function KKMembersCard({ members, sensitiveVisible, getNik }: Props) {
  return (
    <Card className="overflow-hidden rounded-2xl border-slate-200 bg-white shadow-sm">
      <CardHeader className="border-b border-slate-200 px-5 py-4 sm:px-6">

              <Users className="size-4" />

              Data anggota keluarga belum tersedia pada Kartu Keluarga ini.

          <>

                <KKMemberMobile
                  key={member.id}

                  sensitiveVisible={sensitiveVisible}
                />
              ))}

############################################################
FILE: src/components/kk/edit-kk-dialog.tsx
############################################################

import { zodResolver } from '@hookform/resolvers/zod'
import { Loader2, Pencil, Save, TriangleAlert } from 'lucide-react'
import { useEffect, useState } from 'react'
import { useForm } from 'react-hook-form'
import { toast } from 'sonner'

import { Button } from '@/components/ui/button'

import {
  Dialog,
  DialogContent,
  DialogDescription,
  DialogFooter,
  DialogHeader,
  DialogTitle,
} from '@/components/ui/dialog'

import { kkSchema } from '@/lib/kk-parser/schema'
import type { ValidatedKK } from '@/lib/kk-parser/schema'

import { getKKPrivateLocal, updateKKLocal } from '@/server/kk.local.functions'

import { createEmptyKK, normalizeKKFormValues } from './form/kk-form-defaults'

import { KKFormFields } from './form/kk-form-fields'

type Props = {
  id: number

  open: boolean

  onOpenChange: (open: boolean) => void

  onSaved?: () => void | Promise<void>
}

type PrivateKKResponse = Awaited<ReturnType<typeof getKKPrivateLocal>>

function normalizeJenisKelamin(
  value: string | null,
): 'LAKI-LAKI' | 'PEREMPUAN' | null {
  if (value === 'LAKI-LAKI') {
    return 'LAKI-LAKI'
  }

  if (value === 'PEREMPUAN') {
    return 'PEREMPUAN'
  }

  return null
}

function toFormValues(data: PrivateKKResponse['kk']): ValidatedKK {
  return {
    noKK: data.noKK,

    kepalaKeluarga: data.kepalaKeluarga ?? '',

    alamat: data.alamat,

    rt: data.rt,

    rw: data.rw,

    desaKelurahan: data.desaKelurahan,

    kecamatan: data.kecamatan,

    kabupatenKota: data.kabupatenKota,

    kodePos: data.kodePos,

    provinsi: data.provinsi,

    anggota: data.anggota.map((member, index) => ({
      id: member.id,

      no: index + 1,

      nama: member.nama,

      nik: member.nik,

      jenisKelamin: normalizeJenisKelamin(member.jenisKelamin),

      tempatLahir: member.tempatLahir,

      tanggalLahir: member.tanggalLahir,

      agama: member.agama,

      pendidikan: member.pendidikan,

      pekerjaan: member.pekerjaan,

      golonganDarah: member.golonganDarah,

      ayah: member.ayah,
      ibu: member.ibu,
      baptisAnak: member.baptisAnak,
      sidiBaptisDewasa: member.sidiBaptisDewasa,
      menikah: member.menikah,
      domisili: member.domisili,
    })),
  }
}

export function EditKKDialog({ id, open, onOpenChange, onSaved }: Props) {
  const [isLoading, setIsLoading] = useState(false)

  const [loadError, setLoadError] = useState<string | null>(null)

  const form = useForm<ValidatedKK>({
    resolver: zodResolver(kkSchema),

    defaultValues: createEmptyKK(),
  })

  const {
    reset,
    handleSubmit,

    formState: { isSubmitting },
  } = form

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!open) {
      return
    }

    let cancelled = false

    async function loadData() {
      setIsLoading(true)
      setLoadError(null)

      try {
        const response = await getKKPrivateLocal({
          data: {
            id,
          },
        })

        if (cancelled) {
          return
        }

        reset(toFormValues(response.kk))
      } catch (error) {
        if (cancelled) {
          return
        }

        const message =
          error instanceof Error ? error.message : 'Gagal mengambil data.'

        setLoadError(message)

        toast.error('Gagal membuka data', {
          description: message,
        })
      } finally {
        if (!cancelled) {
          setIsLoading(false)
        }
      }
    }

    void loadData()

    return () => {
      cancelled = true
    }
  }, [open, id, reset])

  function handleOpenChange(nextOpen: boolean) {
    if (!nextOpen && isSubmitting) {
      return
    }

    onOpenChange(nextOpen)
  }

  async function onSubmit(values: ValidatedKK) {
    const normalizedValues = normalizeKKFormValues(values)

    try {
      await updateKKLocal({
        data: {
          id,
          kk: normalizedValues,
        },
      })

      toast.success('Data berhasil diperbarui', {
        description: 'Perubahan Kartu Keluarga berhasil disimpan.',
      })

      onOpenChange(false)

      await onSaved?.()
    } catch (error) {
      const message =
        error instanceof Error
          ? error.message
          : 'Terjadi kesalahan pada server.'

      toast.error('Gagal memperbarui data', {
        description: message,
      })
    }
  }

  const disabled = isLoading || isSubmitting

  return (
    <Dialog open={open} onOpenChange={handleOpenChange}>
      <DialogContent
        className="
          flex
          max-h-[92dvh]
          w-[calc(100vw-1.5rem)]
          max-w-[calc(100vw-1.5rem)]
          flex-col
          gap-0
          overflow-hidden
          rounded-2xl
          border-slate-200
          bg-white
          p-0
          shadow-xl
          sm:w-[calc(100vw-3rem)]
          sm:max-w-5xl
        "
      >
        {/* HEADER */}
        <DialogHeader className="shrink-0 border-b border-slate-200 bg-white px-5 py-4 pr-12 sm:px-6 sm:py-5">
          <div className="flex items-start gap-3">
            <div className="flex size-10 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-xl bg-blue-50 text-blue-600">
              <Pencil className="size-4" />
            </div>

            <div className="min-w-0">
              <DialogTitle className="text-base font-semibold text-slate-900 sm:text-lg">
                Edit Kartu Keluarga
              </DialogTitle>

              <DialogDescription className="mt-1 max-w-2xl text-xs leading-5 text-slate-500 sm:text-sm">
                Perbarui informasi Kartu Keluarga dan data anggota keluarga.
              </DialogDescription>
            </div>
          </div>
        </DialogHeader>

        {isLoading ? (
          /* LOADING */
          <div className="flex min-h-0 flex-1 items-center justify-center bg-slate-50/40 px-6 py-16">
            <div className="text-center">
              <div className="mx-auto flex size-11 items-center justify-center rounded-xl bg-white text-blue-600 ring-1 ring-slate-200">
                <Loader2 className="size-5 animate-spin" />
              </div>

              <div className="mt-3 text-sm font-medium text-slate-700">
                Memuat data Kartu Keluarga...
              </div>

              <p className="mt-1 text-xs text-slate-500">
                Data sensitif sedang dibuka dengan aman.
              </p>
            </div>
          </div>
        ) : loadError ? (
          /* ERROR */
          <div className="flex min-h-0 flex-1 items-center justify-center bg-slate-50/40 px-5 py-12 sm:px-6">
            <div className="w-full max-w-md rounded-xl border border-red-200 bg-white p-5 text-center">
              <div className="mx-auto flex size-11 items-center justify-center rounded-xl bg-red-50 text-red-600">
                <TriangleAlert className="size-5" />
              </div>

              <div className="mt-3 text-sm font-semibold text-slate-800">
                Data tidak dapat dibuka
              </div>

              <p className="mt-1 text-xs leading-5 text-slate-500">
                {loadError}
              </p>

              <Button
                type="button"
                variant="outline"
                className="mt-4 h-9 rounded-lg border-slate-200 bg-white"
                onClick={() => handleOpenChange(false)}
              >
                Tutup
              </Button>
            </div>
          </div>
        ) : (
          /* FORM */
          <form
            id="edit-kk-form"
            onSubmit={handleSubmit(onSubmit)}
            className="flex min-h-0 flex-1 flex-col"
          >
            {/* SCROLL AREA */}
            <div className="min-h-0 flex-1 overflow-x-hidden overflow-y-auto bg-slate-50/40">
              <div className="p-4 sm:p-6">
                <KKFormFields form={form} disabled={disabled} />
              </div>
            </div>

            {/* FOOTER */}
            <DialogFooter className="shrink-0 border-t border-slate-200 bg-white px-4 pt-4 pb-5 sm:px-6 sm:pt-4 sm:pb-6">
              <div className="flex w-full flex-col-reverse gap-2 sm:flex-row sm:justify-end">
                <Button
                  type="button"
                  variant="outline"
                  className="h-10 rounded-lg border-slate-200 bg-white px-5 shadow-none"
                  disabled={disabled}
                  onClick={() => handleOpenChange(false)}
                >
                  Batal
                </Button>

                <Button
                  type="submit"
                  form="edit-kk-form"
                  className="h-10 rounded-lg px-5 sm:min-w-44"
                  disabled={disabled}
                >
                  {isSubmitting ? (
                    <>
                      <Loader2 className="size-4 animate-spin" />
                      Menyimpan...
                    </>
                  ) : (
                    <>
                      <Save className="size-4" />
                      Simpan Perubahan
                    </>
                  )}
                </Button>
              </div>
            </DialogFooter>
          </form>
        )}
      </DialogContent>
    </Dialog>
  )
}


############################################################
FILE: src/components/kk/form/kk-form-defaults.ts
############################################################

import type { ValidatedKK } from '@/lib/kk-parser/schema'

export function createEmptyMember(no: number): ValidatedKK['anggota'][number] {
  return {
    no,
    nama: '',
    nik: '',
    jenisKelamin: null,
    tempatLahir: null,
    tanggalLahir: null,
    agama: null,
    pendidikan: null,
    pekerjaan: null,
    golonganDarah: null,

    ayah: null,
    ibu: null,
    baptisAnak: null,
    sidiBaptisDewasa: null,
    menikah: null,
    domisili: null,
  }
}

export function createEmptyKK(): ValidatedKK {
  return {
    noKK: '',
    kepalaKeluarga: '',
    alamat: null,
    rt: null,
    rw: null,
    desaKelurahan: null,
    kecamatan: null,
    kabupatenKota: null,
    kodePos: null,
    provinsi: null,
    anggota: [createEmptyMember(1)],
  }
}

export function normalizeKKFormValues(values: ValidatedKK): ValidatedKK {
  return {
    ...values,

    anggota: values.anggota.map((member, index) => ({
      ...member,
      no: index + 1,
    })),
  }
}


############################################################
FILE: src/components/kk/form/kk-form-fields.tsx
############################################################

import { Controller, useFieldArray } from 'react-hook-form'

import type { Control, UseFormReturn } from 'react-hook-form'

import { IdCard, Plus, Trash2, UserRound, Users } from 'lucide-react'

import { Button } from '@/components/ui/button'

import {
  Field,
  FieldError,
  FieldGroup,
  FieldLabel,
} from '@/components/ui/field'

import { Input } from '@/components/ui/input'

import {
  Select,
  SelectContent,
  SelectItem,
  SelectTrigger,
  SelectValue,
} from '@/components/ui/select'

import type { ValidatedKK } from '@/lib/kk-parser/schema'

import { createEmptyMember } from './kk-form-defaults'

type Props = {
  form: UseFormReturn<ValidatedKK>
  disabled?: boolean
}

type RootTextFieldName =
  | 'noKK'
  | 'kepalaKeluarga'
  | 'alamat'
  | 'rt'
  | 'rw'
  | 'desaKelurahan'
  | 'kecamatan'
  | 'kabupatenKota'
  | 'kodePos'
  | 'provinsi'

type MemberTextFieldKey =
  | 'nama'
  | 'nik'
  | 'tempatLahir'
  | 'tanggalLahir'
  | 'agama'
  | 'pendidikan'
  | 'pekerjaan'
  | 'golonganDarah'
  | 'ayah'
  | 'ibu'
  | 'baptisAnak'
  | 'sidiBaptisDewasa'
  | 'menikah'
  | 'domisili'

type MemberTextFieldName = `anggota.${number}.${MemberTextFieldKey}`

type TextFieldName = RootTextFieldName | MemberTextFieldName

type GenderFieldName = `anggota.${number}.jenisKelamin`

export function KKFormFields({ form, disabled = false }: Props) {
  const anggota = useFieldArray({
    control: form.control,
    name: 'anggota',
  })

  function handleAddMember() {
    anggota.append(createEmptyMember(anggota.fields.length + 1))
  }

  function handleRemoveMember(index: number) {
    anggota.remove(index)
  }

  return (
    <div className="min-w-0 space-y-5">
      <KKSection control={form.control} disabled={disabled} />

      <section className="min-w-0 overflow-hidden rounded-xl border border-slate-200 bg-white">
        {/* HEADER ANGGOTA */}
        <div className="flex min-w-0 flex-col gap-3 border-b border-slate-200 px-4 py-4 sm:flex-row sm:items-center sm:justify-between sm:px-5">
          <div className="min-w-0">
            <div className="flex items-center gap-2.5">
              <div className="flex size-9 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-lg bg-blue-50 text-blue-600">
                <Users className="size-4" />
              </div>

              <div className="min-w-0">
                <h3 className="text-sm font-semibold text-slate-900">
                  Anggota Keluarga
                </h3>

                <p className="mt-0.5 text-xs text-slate-500">
                  {anggota.fields.length.toLocaleString('id-ID')} anggota
                  keluarga
                </p>
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>

          <Button
            type="button"
            variant="outline"
            className="h-9 shrink-0 rounded-lg border-slate-200 bg-white px-3 shadow-none"
            disabled={disabled}
            onClick={handleAddMember}
          >
            <Plus className="size-4" />
            Tambah Anggota
          </Button>
        </div>

        {/* LIST ANGGOTA */}
        <div className="space-y-3 bg-slate-50/40 p-3 sm:p-4">
          {anggota.fields.map((memberField, index) => (
            <MemberCard
              key={memberField.id}
              index={index}
              control={form.control}
              disabled={disabled}
              canRemove={anggota.fields.length > 1}
              onRemove={() => handleRemoveMember(index)}
            />
          ))}
        </div>
      </section>
    </div>
  )
}

function KKSection({
  control,
  disabled,
}: {
  control: Control<ValidatedKK>
  disabled: boolean
}) {
  return (
    <section className="min-w-0 overflow-hidden rounded-xl border border-slate-200 bg-white">
      <div className="border-b border-slate-200 px-4 py-4 sm:px-5">
        <div className="flex items-center gap-2.5">
          <div className="flex size-9 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-lg bg-blue-50 text-blue-600">
            <IdCard className="size-4" />
          </div>

          <div>
            <h3 className="text-sm font-semibold text-slate-900">
              Informasi Kartu Keluarga
            </h3>

            <p className="mt-0.5 text-xs leading-5 text-slate-500">
              Informasi utama dan alamat Kartu Keluarga.
            </p>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>

      <div className="p-4 sm:p-5">
        <FieldGroup className="grid min-w-0 gap-x-4 gap-y-3 md:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-3">
          <TextField
            control={control}
            name="noKK"
            label="Nomor KK"
            nullable={false}
            inputMode="numeric"
            autoComplete="off"
            disabled={disabled}
          />

          <TextField
            control={control}
            name="kepalaKeluarga"
            label="Nama Kepala Keluarga"
            nullable={false}
            disabled={disabled}
          />

          <TextField
            control={control}
            name="alamat"
            label="Alamat"
            disabled={disabled}
          />

          <TextField
            control={control}
            name="rt"
            label="RT"
            inputMode="numeric"
            disabled={disabled}
          />

          <TextField
            control={control}
            name="rw"
            label="RW"
            inputMode="numeric"
            disabled={disabled}
          />

          <TextField
            control={control}
            name="desaKelurahan"
            label="Desa / Kelurahan"
            disabled={disabled}
          />

          <TextField
            control={control}
            name="kecamatan"
            label="Kecamatan"
            disabled={disabled}
          />

          <TextField

          <TextField

        </FieldGroup>
      </div>
    </section>

function MemberCard({
  index,
  control,
  disabled,
  canRemove,
  onRemove,
}: {
  index: number

  control: Control<ValidatedKK>

  disabled: boolean
  canRemove: boolean

  onRemove: () => void
}) {
  return (
    <div className="min-w-0 overflow-hidden rounded-xl border border-slate-200 bg-white">
      {/* HEADER MEMBER */}
      <div className="flex min-w-0 items-center justify-between gap-3 border-b border-slate-100 px-4 py-3">
        <div className="flex min-w-0 items-center gap-3">
          <div className="flex size-8 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-lg bg-slate-100 text-slate-500">
            <UserRound className="size-4" />
          </div>

          <div className="min-w-0">
            <div className="truncate text-sm font-semibold text-slate-900">
              Anggota {index + 1}
            </div>

            <div className="truncate text-[11px] text-slate-400">
              Data anggota keluarga
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>

        {canRemove && (
          <Button
            type="button"
            variant="ghost"
            size="icon"
            className="size-8 shrink-0 rounded-lg text-slate-400 hover:bg-red-50 hover:text-red-600"
            disabled={disabled}
            onClick={onRemove}
            aria-label={`Hapus anggota ${index + 1}`}
          >
            <Trash2 className="size-4" />
          </Button>
        )}
      </div>

      {/* FIELDS */}
      <div className="p-4">
        <div className="grid min-w-0 gap-x-4 gap-y-3 md:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-3">
          <TextField
            control={control}
            name={`anggota.${index}.nama`}
            label="Nama Lengkap"
            nullable={false}
            disabled={disabled}
          />

          <TextField
            control={control}
            name={`anggota.${index}.nik`}
            label="NIK"
            nullable={false}
            inputMode="numeric"
            autoComplete="off"
            disabled={disabled}
          />

          <GenderField
            control={control}
            name={`anggota.${index}.jenisKelamin`}
            disabled={disabled}
          />

          <TextField
            control={control}
            name={`anggota.${index}.tempatLahir`}
            label="Tempat Lahir"
            disabled={disabled}
          />

          <TextField
            control={control}
            name={`anggota.${index}.tanggalLahir`}
            label="Tanggal Lahir"
            placeholder="DD-MM-YYYY"
            disabled={disabled}
          />

          <TextField
            control={control}
            name={`anggota.${index}.agama`}
            label="Agama"
            disabled={disabled}
          />

          <TextField
            control={control}
            name={`anggota.${index}.pendidikan`}
            label="Pendidikan"
            disabled={disabled}
          />

          <TextField
            control={control}
            name={`anggota.${index}.pekerjaan`}
            label="Pekerjaan"
            disabled={disabled}
          />

          <TextField
            control={control}
            name={`anggota.${index}.golonganDarah`}
            label="Golongan Darah"
            disabled={disabled}
          />
        </div>

        <div className="mt-5 border-t border-slate-100 pt-5">
          <div className="mb-3">
            <div className="text-xs font-semibold uppercase tracking-[0.12em] text-slate-400">
              Keluarga & Data Gerejawi
            </div>
          </div>

          <div className="grid min-w-0 gap-x-4 gap-y-3 md:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-3">
            <TextField

              label="Ayah"

            />

            <TextField
              control={control}

            />

type TextFieldProps = {
  control: Control<ValidatedKK>

  name: TextFieldName

  label: string

  placeholder?: string

  nullable?: boolean

  disabled?: boolean

  inputMode?:
    'text' | 'numeric' | 'decimal' | 'tel' | 'email' | 'url' | 'search'

  autoComplete?: string
}

function TextField({
  control,
  name,
  label,
  placeholder,
  nullable = true,
  disabled = false,
  inputMode,
  autoComplete,
}: TextFieldProps) {
  return (
    <Controller
      control={control}
      name={name}
      render={({ field, fieldState }) => (
        <Field className="min-w-0 gap-1.5" data-invalid={fieldState.invalid}>
          <FieldLabel
            htmlFor={field.name}
            className="text-sm font-medium text-slate-700"
          >
            {label}
          </FieldLabel>

          <Input
            id={field.name}
            name={field.name}
            ref={field.ref}
            onBlur={field.onBlur}
            placeholder={placeholder}
            inputMode={inputMode}
            autoComplete={autoComplete}
            disabled={disabled}
            aria-invalid={fieldState.invalid}
            className="h-10 w-full min-w-0 rounded-lg border-slate-200 bg-white shadow-none"
            value={field.value == null ? '' : field.value}
            onChange={(event) => {
              const value = event.target.value

              field.onChange(nullable ? value || null : value)
            }}
          />

          {fieldState.error && <FieldError errors={[fieldState.error]} />}
        </Field>
      )}
    />
  )
}

function GenderField({
  control,
  name,
  disabled,
}: {
  control: Control<ValidatedKK>

  name: GenderFieldName

  disabled: boolean
}) {
  return (
    <Controller
      control={control}
      name={name}
      render={({ field, fieldState }) => (
        <Field className="min-w-0 gap-1.5" data-invalid={fieldState.invalid}>
          <FieldLabel className="text-sm font-medium text-slate-700">
            Jenis Kelamin
          </FieldLabel>

          <Select
            value={field.value ?? ''}
            disabled={disabled}
            onValueChange={(value) => {
              if (value === 'LAKI-LAKI' || value === 'PEREMPUAN') {
                field.onChange(value)
              }
            }}
          >
            <SelectTrigger
              className="w-full min-w-0 rounded-lg border-slate-200 bg-white shadow-none data-[size=default]:h-10"
              aria-invalid={fieldState.invalid}
            >
              <SelectValue placeholder="Pilih jenis kelamin" />
            </SelectTrigger>

            <SelectContent>
              <SelectItem value="LAKI-LAKI">LAKI-LAKI</SelectItem>

              <SelectItem value="PEREMPUAN">PEREMPUAN</SelectItem>
            </SelectContent>
          </Select>

          {fieldState.error && <FieldError errors={[fieldState.error]} />}
        </Field>
      )}
    />
  )
}

import type { ColumnDef } from '@tanstack/react-table'

import { ChevronRight, MapPin, MoreHorizontal, Users } from 'lucide-react'

import { Link } from '@tanstack/react-router'

import { Badge } from '@/components/ui/badge'
import { Button } from '@/components/ui/button'

import {
  DropdownMenu,
  DropdownMenuContent,
  DropdownMenuItem,
  DropdownMenuLabel,
  DropdownMenuSeparator,
  DropdownMenuTrigger,
} from '@/components/ui/dropdown-menu'

import { DataTableColumnHeader } from '@/components/data-table/data-table-column-header'

import type { KKListItem } from './types'

export const kkColumns: ColumnDef<KKListItem>[] = [
  {
    accessorKey: 'kepalaKeluarga',

    header: ({ column }) => (
      <DataTableColumnHeader column={column} title="Kepala Keluarga" />
    ),

    cell: ({ row }) => {
      const item = row.original

      return (
        <div className="min-w-[190px]">
          <Link
            to="/data-kk/$id"
            params={{
              id: String(item.id),
            }}
            className="font-semibold text-slate-900 transition-colors hover:text-primary"
          >
            {item.kepalaKeluarga ?? '-'}
          </Link>

          <div className="mt-1 text-xs text-muted-foreground">
            ID #{item.id}
          </div>
        </div>
      )
    },
  },

  {
    accessorKey: 'alamat',

    header: ({ column }) => (
      <DataTableColumnHeader column={column} title="Alamat" />
    ),

    cell: ({ row }) => {
      const item = row.original

      return (
        <div className="min-w-[210px]">
          <div className="font-medium">{item.alamat ?? '-'}</div>

          {(item.rt || item.rw) && (
            <div className="mt-1 text-xs text-muted-foreground">
              RT {item.rt ?? '-'}
              {' / '}
              RW {item.rw ?? '-'}
            </div>
          )}
        </div>
      )
    },
  },

  {
    id: 'wilayah',

    accessorFn: (row) =>
      [row.desaKelurahan, row.kecamatan, row.kabupatenKota, row.provinsi]
        .filter(Boolean)
        .join(' '),

    header: ({ column }) => (
      <DataTableColumnHeader column={column} title="Wilayah" />
    ),

    cell: ({ row }) => {
      const item = row.original

      return (
        <div className="flex min-w-[220px] items-start gap-2">
          <MapPin className="mt-0.5 size-4 shrink-0 text-muted-foreground" />

          <div>
            <div className="font-medium">{item.desaKelurahan ?? '-'}</div>

            <div className="mt-1 text-xs text-muted-foreground">
              {item.kecamatan ?? '-'}
              {' · '}
              {item.kabupatenKota ?? '-'}
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      )
    },
  },

  {
    accessorKey: 'jumlahAnggota',

    header: ({ column }) => (
      <DataTableColumnHeader column={column} title="Anggota" />
    ),

    cell: ({ row }) => (
      <Badge
        variant="secondary"
        className="gap-1.5 whitespace-nowrap rounded-full"
      >
        <Users className="size-3" />
        {row.original.jumlahAnggota} orang
      </Badge>
    ),
  },

  {
    accessorKey: 'createdAt',

    header: ({ column }) => (
      <DataTableColumnHeader column={column} title="Dibuat" />
    ),

    cell: ({ row }) => (
      <span className="whitespace-nowrap text-sm">
        {formatDate(row.original.createdAt)}
      </span>
    ),

    sortingFn: (rowA, rowB) =>
      new Date(rowA.original.createdAt).getTime() -
      new Date(rowB.original.createdAt).getTime(),
  },

  {
    id: 'actions',

    enableSorting: false,
    enableHiding: false,

    cell: ({ row }) => {
      const item = row.original

      return (
        <div className="flex justify-end">
          <DropdownMenu>
            <DropdownMenuTrigger asChild>
              <Button variant="ghost" size="icon" className="size-8">
                <MoreHorizontal className="size-4" />

                <span className="sr-only">Buka menu</span>
              </Button>
            </DropdownMenuTrigger>

            <DropdownMenuContent align="end">
              <DropdownMenuLabel>Aksi</DropdownMenuLabel>

              <DropdownMenuSeparator />

              <DropdownMenuItem asChild>
                <Link
                  to="/data-kk/$id"
                  params={{
                    id: String(item.id),
                  }}
                >
                  <ChevronRight className="size-4" />
                  Buka Detail
                </Link>
              </DropdownMenuItem>
            </DropdownMenuContent>
          </DropdownMenu>
        </div>
      )
    },
  },
]

function formatDate(value: string) {
  return new Date(value).toLocaleDateString('id-ID', {
    day: '2-digit',
    month: 'short',
    year: 'numeric',
  })
}

import { Eraser, FileText, Play, TriangleAlert } from 'lucide-react'

import { Button } from '@/components/ui/button'
import {
  Card,
  CardContent,
  CardFooter,
  CardHeader,
  CardTitle,
} from '@/components/ui/card'
import { Textarea } from '@/components/ui/textarea'

type Props = {
  text: string
  error: string | null
  canClear: boolean

  onTextChange: (value: string) => void

  onParse: () => void
  onClear: () => void
}

export function ParserInputCard({
  text,
  error,
  canClear,
  onTextChange,
  onParse,
  onClear,
}: Props) {
  const hasText = text.trim().length > 0

  return (
    <Card className="flex h-full min-w-0 flex-col overflow-hidden rounded-2xl border-slate-200 bg-white shadow-sm">
      <CardHeader className="border-b border-slate-200 px-5 py-4 sm:px-6">
        <div className="flex items-start justify-between gap-4">
          <div className="flex min-w-0 items-start gap-3">
            <div className="flex size-10 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-xl bg-blue-50 text-blue-600">
              <FileText className="size-4" />
            </div>

            <div className="min-w-0">
              <div className="text-[11px] font-bold uppercase tracking-[0.14em] text-blue-600">
                Langkah 1
              </div>

              <CardTitle className="mt-1 text-base font-semibold text-slate-900">
                Input Teks
              </CardTitle>

              <p className="mt-0.5 text-xs leading-5 text-slate-500">
                Tempel teks hasil ekstraksi Kartu Keluarga untuk diproses.
              </p>
            </div>
          </div>

          {text && (
            <span className="shrink-0 rounded-md bg-slate-100 px-2.5 py-1 text-[11px] font-medium tabular-nums text-slate-500">
              {text.length.toLocaleString('id-ID')} karakter
            </span>
          )}
        </div>
      </CardHeader>

      <CardContent className="flex-1 p-0">
        <Textarea
          value={text}
          onChange={(event) => onTextChange(event.target.value)}
          placeholder={`Tempel teks Kartu Keluarga di sini...

Contoh:

KARTU KELUARGA
No. 1234567890123456
Nama Kepala Keluarga ...
Alamat ...
...`}
          spellCheck={false}
          className="
            min-h-95
            resize-y
            rounded-none
            border-0
            bg-slate-50/40
            p-5
            font-mono
            text-[13px]
            leading-6
            shadow-none
            focus-visible:ring-0
            sm:min-h-110
            lg:min-h-125
          "
        />

        {error && (
          <div className="mx-4 mb-4 flex items-start gap-3 rounded-xl border border-red-200 bg-red-50/60 px-4 py-3.5 text-red-700 sm:mx-5 sm:mb-5">
            <div className="mt-0.5 flex size-8 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-lg bg-white text-red-600 ring-1 ring-red-200">
              <TriangleAlert className="size-4" />
            </div>

            <div className="min-w-0">
              <div className="text-sm font-semibold">Parsing gagal</div>

              <p className="mt-1 text-xs leading-5 text-red-600">{error}</p>
            </div>
          </div>
        )}
      </CardContent>

      <CardFooter className="border-t border-slate-200 bg-white px-4 py-4 sm:px-5">
        <div className="flex w-full flex-col-reverse gap-2 sm:flex-row sm:justify-end">
          <Button
            type="button"
            variant="outline"
            className="h-10 rounded-lg border-slate-200 bg-white px-4 shadow-none"
            onClick={onClear}
            disabled={!canClear}
          >
            <Eraser className="size-4" />
            Bersihkan
          </Button>

          <Button
            type="button"
            className="h-10 rounded-lg px-5 sm:min-w-32"
            onClick={onParse}
            disabled={!hasText}
          >
            <Play className="size-4" />
            Proses Teks
          </Button>
        </div>
      </CardFooter>
    </Card>
  )
}

import { Braces, Copy, Eye, EyeOff } from 'lucide-react'

import type { KKData } from '@/lib/kk-parser/types'

import { Badge } from '@/components/ui/badge'
import { Button } from '@/components/ui/button'
import { Card, CardContent, CardHeader, CardTitle } from '@/components/ui/card'

type Props = {
  data: KKData | null
  isValid: boolean
  showSensitive: boolean

  onToggleSensitive: () => void
  onCopy: () => void
}

export function ParserPreviewCard({
  data,
  isValid,
  showSensitive,
  onToggleSensitive,
  onCopy,
}: Props) {
  return (
    <Card className="flex h-full min-w-0 flex-col overflow-hidden rounded-2xl border-slate-200 bg-white shadow-sm">
      {/* HEADER */}
      <CardHeader className="shrink-0 border-b border-slate-200 px-5 py-4 sm:px-6">
        <div className="flex items-start justify-between gap-4">
          <div className="flex min-w-0 items-start gap-3">
            <div className="flex size-10 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-xl bg-blue-50 text-blue-600">
              <Braces className="size-4" />
            </div>

            <div className="min-w-0">
              <div className="text-[11px] font-bold uppercase tracking-[0.14em] text-blue-600">
                Langkah 2
              </div>

              <CardTitle className="mt-1 text-base font-semibold text-slate-900">
                Hasil Data
              </CardTitle>

              <p className="mt-0.5 text-xs leading-5 text-slate-500">
                Periksa hasil data terstruktur sebelum disimpan ke database.
              </p>
            </div>
          </div>

          {data && (
            <Badge
              variant="outline"
              className={[
                'shrink-0 rounded-full px-2.5 py-1 text-[11px] font-medium shadow-none',
                isValid
                  ? 'border-emerald-200 bg-emerald-50 text-emerald-700'
                  : 'border-amber-200 bg-amber-50 text-amber-700',
              ].join(' ')}
            >
              {isValid ? 'Data Valid' : 'Perlu Diperiksa'}
            </Badge>
          )}
        </div>
      </CardHeader>

      {!data ? (
        /* EMPTY */
        <CardContent className="flex min-h-125 flex-1 items-center justify-center bg-slate-50/30 p-6 sm:min-h-140">
          <div className="max-w-sm text-center">
            <div className="mx-auto flex size-12 items-center justify-center rounded-xl bg-white text-slate-400 ring-1 ring-slate-200">
              <Braces className="size-5" />
            </div>

            <div className="mt-4 text-sm font-semibold text-slate-700">
              Belum ada hasil
            </div>

            <p className="mt-1 text-xs leading-5 text-slate-500">
              Tempel teks Kartu Keluarga pada panel Input Teks, kemudian pilih
              Proses Teks.
            </p>
          </div>
        </CardContent>
      ) : (
        <>
          {/* TOOLBAR */}
          <div className="flex min-h-14 shrink-0 flex-col gap-2 border-b border-slate-200 bg-slate-50/60 px-4 py-3 sm:flex-row sm:items-center sm:justify-between sm:px-5">
            <Button
              type="button"
              variant="ghost"
              size="sm"
              className={[
                'h-9 justify-start rounded-lg px-3 shadow-none',
                showSensitive
                  ? 'text-amber-700 hover:bg-amber-50 hover:text-amber-800'
                  : 'text-slate-600 hover:bg-white hover:text-slate-900',
              ].join(' ')}
              onClick={onToggleSensitive}
            >
              {showSensitive ? (
                <>
                  <EyeOff className="size-4" />
                  Sembunyikan Data Sensitif
                </>
              ) : (
                <>
                  <Eye className="size-4" />
                  Tampilkan Data Sensitif
                </>
              )}
            </Button>

            <Button
              type="button"
              variant="outline"
              size="sm"
              className="h-9 rounded-lg border-slate-200 bg-white px-3 shadow-none"
              onClick={onCopy}
            >
              <Copy className="size-4" />
              Salin JSON
            </Button>
          </div>

          {/* JSON */}
          <CardContent className="min-h-125 flex-1 overflow-auto bg-slate-950 p-0">
            <pre className="min-w-max p-5 font-mono text-xs leading-6 text-slate-200 sm:p-6">
              {JSON.stringify(data, null, 2)}
            </pre>
          </CardContent>
        </>
      )}
    </Card>
  )
}

import { CheckCircle2, Database, Loader2, ShieldCheck } from 'lucide-react'

import { Button } from '@/components/ui/button'
import { Card, CardContent } from '@/components/ui/card'

type Props = {
  canSave: boolean
  isSaving: boolean
  isSaved: boolean
  onSave: () => void
}

export function ParserSaveCard({ canSave, isSaving, isSaved, onSave }: Props) {
  return (
    <Card className="overflow-hidden rounded-2xl border-slate-200 bg-white shadow-sm">
      <CardContent className="p-5 sm:p-6">
        <div className="flex flex-col gap-5 sm:flex-row sm:items-center sm:justify-between">
          <div className="min-w-0">
            <div className="flex items-start gap-3">
              <div
                className={[
                  'flex size-10 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-xl',
                  isSaved
                    ? 'bg-emerald-50 text-emerald-600'
                    : 'bg-blue-50 text-blue-600',
                ].join(' ')}
              >
                {isSaved ? (
                  <CheckCircle2 className="size-4" />
                ) : (
                  <Database className="size-4" />
                )}
              </div>

              <div className="min-w-0">
                <div className="text-[11px] font-bold uppercase tracking-[0.14em] text-blue-600">
                  Langkah 3
                </div>

                <div className="mt-1 text-base font-semibold text-slate-900">
                  Simpan Data
                </div>

                <p className="mt-1 max-w-2xl text-xs leading-5 text-slate-500">
                  Data akan diperiksa kembali sebelum disimpan. Nomor KK dan NIK
                  diamankan sebelum masuk ke database.
                </p>

                <div className="mt-3 flex items-center gap-2 text-xs text-slate-500">
                  <ShieldCheck className="size-3.5 text-slate-400" />
                  Data sensitif diproses sesuai mekanisme perlindungan aplikasi.
                </div>

                {isSaved && (
                  <div className="mt-3 inline-flex items-center gap-2 rounded-lg bg-emerald-50 px-3 py-2 text-xs font-medium text-emerald-700">
                    <CheckCircle2 className="size-4" />
                    Data telah berhasil tersimpan.
                  </div>
                )}
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>

          <Button
            type="button"
            className="h-10 w-full shrink-0 rounded-lg px-5 sm:w-auto sm:min-w-48"
            disabled={!canSave || isSaving || isSaved}
            onClick={onSave}
          >
            {isSaving ? (
              <>
                <Loader2 className="size-4 animate-spin" />
                Menyimpan...
              </>
            ) : isSaved ? (
              <>
                <CheckCircle2 className="size-4" />
                Tersimpan
              </>
            ) : (
              <>
                <Database className="size-4" />
                Simpan ke Database
              </>
            )}
          </Button>
        </div>
      </CardContent>
    </Card>
  )
}

FILE: src/components/kk/parser/parser-validation-card.tsx
############################################################

import { CheckCircle2, TriangleAlert } from 'lucide-react'

type Props = {
  isValid: boolean
  errors: string[]
}

export function ParserValidationCard({ isValid, errors }: Props) {
  if (isValid) {
    return (
      <div className="flex items-start gap-3 rounded-xl border border-emerald-200 bg-emerald-50/60 px-4 py-3.5">
        <div className="flex size-9 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-lg bg-white text-emerald-600 ring-1 ring-emerald-200">
          <CheckCircle2 className="size-4" />
        </div>

        <div className="min-w-0">
          <div className="text-sm font-semibold text-emerald-800">
            Data berhasil divalidasi
          </div>

          <p className="mt-0.5 text-xs leading-5 text-emerald-700">
            Struktur data sudah sesuai dan dapat dilanjutkan ke proses
            penyimpanan.
          </p>
        </div>
      </div>
    )
  }

  return (
    <div className="rounded-xl border border-amber-200 bg-amber-50/60 px-4 py-3.5">
      <div className="flex items-start gap-3">
        <div className="flex size-9 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-lg bg-white text-amber-600 ring-1 ring-amber-200">
          <TriangleAlert className="size-4" />
        </div>

        <div className="min-w-0 flex-1">
          <div className="text-sm font-semibold text-amber-900">
            Data belum dapat disimpan
          </div>

          <p className="mt-0.5 text-xs leading-5 text-amber-700">
            Periksa kembali hasil parsing berikut.
          </p>
        </div>

        {errors.length > 0 && (
          <span className="shrink-0 rounded-md bg-white px-2 py-1 text-[11px] font-medium text-amber-700 ring-1 ring-amber-200">
            {errors.length.toLocaleString('id-ID')} masalah
          </span>
        )}
      </div>

      {errors.length > 0 && (
        <div className="mt-3 border-t border-amber-200/70 pt-3">
          <ul className="space-y-1.5">
            {errors.map((message, index) => (
              <li
                key={`${message}-${index}`}
                className="flex items-start gap-2 text-xs leading-5 text-amber-800"
              >
                <span className="mt-1.75 size-1 shrink-0 rounded-full bg-amber-500" />

                <span className="min-w-0 wrap-break-word font-mono">
                  {message}
                </span>
              </li>
            ))}
          </ul>
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  )
}

############################################################
FILE: src/components/layout/auth-user-menu.tsx
############################################################

import { getRouteApi, Link, useRouter } from '@tanstack/react-router'
import {
  ChevronDown,
  KeyRound,
  LogOut,
  ShieldCheck,
  UserRound,
  UsersRound,
} from 'lucide-react'
import { useState } from 'react'

import { Button } from '@/components/ui/button'
import {
  DropdownMenu,
  DropdownMenuContent,
  DropdownMenuItem,
  DropdownMenuLabel,
  DropdownMenuSeparator,
  DropdownMenuTrigger,
} from '@/components/ui/dropdown-menu'

import { logout } from '@/server/auth/actions.functions'

const rootRoute = getRouteApi('__root__')

export function AuthUserMenu() {
  const router = useRouter()

  const { user } = rootRoute.useRouteContext()

  const [isLoggingOut, setIsLoggingOut] = useState(false)

  if (!user) {
    return null
  }

  async function handleLogout() {
    if (isLoggingOut) {
      return
    }

    setIsLoggingOut(true)

    try {
      await logout()

      await router.navigate({
        to: '/login',
        replace: true,
      })
    } finally {
      setIsLoggingOut(false)
    }
  }

  const roleLabel = user.role === 'ADMIN' ? 'Administrator' : 'Petugas'

  return (
    <DropdownMenu>
      <DropdownMenuTrigger asChild>
        <Button
          type="button"
          variant="ghost"
          className="ml-2 h-10 gap-2 border border-slate-200 bg-white px-3 text-slate-700 shadow-sm hover:border-blue-200 hover:bg-blue-50"
        >
          <div className="flex size-7 items-center justify-center rounded-lg bg-blue-50 text-blue-600">
            <UserRound className="size-4" />
          </div>

          <div className="hidden text-left xl:block">
            <div className="max-w-32 truncate text-xs font-semibold text-slate-800">
              {user.nama}
            </div>

            <div className="text-[10px] text-slate-400">{roleLabel}</div>
          </div>

          <ChevronDown className="size-3.5 text-slate-400" />
        </Button>
      </DropdownMenuTrigger>

      <DropdownMenuContent
        align="end"
        sideOffset={10}
        className="w-64 rounded-xl border-blue-100/80 p-2 shadow-xl shadow-slate-900/5"
      >
        <DropdownMenuLabel className="px-3 py-2">
          <div className="font-semibold text-slate-800">{user.nama}</div>

          <div className="mt-1 text-xs font-normal text-slate-400">
            @{user.username}
          </div>
        </DropdownMenuLabel>

        <DropdownMenuSeparator />

        <div className="flex items-center gap-2 px-3 py-2 text-xs text-slate-500">
          <ShieldCheck className="size-4 text-blue-600" />
          {roleLabel}
        </div>

        <DropdownMenuSeparator />

        <DropdownMenuItem asChild className="rounded-lg">
          <Link to="/akun">
            <KeyRound className="size-4" />
            Ganti Password
          </Link>
        </DropdownMenuItem>

        {user.role === 'ADMIN' ? (
          <>
            <DropdownMenuItem asChild className="rounded-lg">
              <Link to="/admin/users">
                <UsersRound className="size-4" />
                Kelola User
              </Link>
            </DropdownMenuItem>

            <DropdownMenuSeparator />
          </>
        ) : null}

        <DropdownMenuItem
          variant="destructive"
          disabled={isLoggingOut}
          onSelect={() => void handleLogout()}
          className="rounded-lg"
        >
          <LogOut className="size-4" />

          {isLoggingOut ? 'Keluar...' : 'Keluar'}
        </DropdownMenuItem>
      </DropdownMenuContent>
    </DropdownMenu>
  )
}

export function SiteFooter() {

                <FooterLink to="/data-anggota">
                  <UsersRound className="size-4" />
                  Data Anggota
                </FooterLink>
              </nav>
            </div>
          ) : null}

          {/* MENU USER */}
          {user ? (
            <div>
              <h3 className="text-sm font-semibold text-slate-900">Menu</h3>

              <nav className="mt-4 space-y-3">
                {user.role === 'ADMIN' ? (
                  <>
                    <FooterLink to="/">
                      <Home className="size-4" />
                      Beranda
                    </FooterLink>

                    <FooterLink to="/event-kesehatan">
                      <HeartPulse className="size-4" />
                      Kesehatan

                    <FooterLink to="/akun">
                      <KeyRound className="size-4" />
                      Ganti Password
                    </FooterLink>
                  </>
                )}
              </nav>
