          ) : null}
        </div>

        <div className="mt-8 flex flex-col gap-2 border-t border-blue-100 pt-5 text-xs text-slate-500 sm:flex-row sm:items-center sm:justify-between">
          <p>© {year} GKJ Slogohimo. Seluruh hak dilindungi.</p>

          <p>Sistem Data Jemaat GKJ Slogohimo</p>
        </div>
      </div>
    </footer>
  )
}

type FooterLinkProps = {
  to:
    | '/'
    | '/data-kk'
    | '/data-anggota'
    | '/event-kesehatan'
    | '/fitur/parser-kk'
    | '/fitur/import-excel'
    | '/akun'

  children: ReactNode
}

function FooterLink({ to, children }: FooterLinkProps) {
  return (
    <Link
      to={to}
      className="flex items-center gap-2 text-sm text-slate-500 transition-colors hover:text-blue-600"
    >
      {children}
    </Link>
  )
}

  getRouteApi,

  DropdownMenuLabel,

                    align="center"
                    sideOffset={10}
                    className="w-64 rounded-xl border-blue-100/80 p-2 shadow-xl shadow-slate-900/5"
                  >
                    <DropdownMenuLabel className="px-3 py-2 text-xs font-semibold uppercase tracking-[0.12em] text-slate-400">
                      Database Jemaat
                    </DropdownMenuLabel>

                    <DropdownMenuSeparator />

                    <DropdownMenuItem asChild className="rounded-lg p-0">
                      <Link
                        to="/data-kk"
                        className="flex w-full items-start gap-3 px-3 py-2.5"
                      >
                        <div className="mt-0.5 flex size-8 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-lg bg-blue-50 text-blue-600">
                          <Database className="size-4" />
                        </div>

                        <div>
                          <div className="text-sm font-medium text-slate-800">
                            Data Kartu Keluarga
                          </div>

                          <div className="mt-0.5 text-xs text-slate-400">
                            Kelola database keluarga
                          </div>
                        </div>
                      </Link>
                    </DropdownMenuItem>

                    <DropdownMenuItem asChild className="mt-1 rounded-lg p-0">
                      <Link
                        to="/data-anggota"
                        className="flex w-full items-start gap-3 px-3 py-2.5"
                      >
                        <div className="mt-0.5 flex size-8 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-lg bg-blue-50 text-blue-600">
                          <UsersRound className="size-4" />
                        </div>

                        <div>
                          <div className="text-sm font-medium text-slate-800">
                            Data Anggota
                          </div>

                          <div className="mt-0.5 text-xs text-slate-400">
                            Lihat seluruh anggota jemaat
                          </div>
                        </div>
                      </Link>
                    </DropdownMenuItem>
                  </DropdownMenuContent>
                </DropdownMenu>

                <DesktopLink to="/event-kesehatan" active={kesehatanActive}>
                  Kesehatan
                </DesktopLink>

                <DropdownMenu>
                  <DropdownMenuTrigger asChild>
                    <button type="button" className={navClass(fiturActive)}>
                      Fitur
                      <ChevronDown className="size-3.5 opacity-60" />
                    </button>
                  </DropdownMenuTrigger>

                  <DropdownMenuContent
                    align="end"
                    sideOffset={10}
                    className="w-64 rounded-xl border-blue-100/80 p-2 shadow-xl shadow-slate-900/5"
                  >
                    <DropdownMenuLabel className="px-3 py-2 text-xs font-semibold uppercase tracking-[0.12em] text-slate-400">
                      Fitur
                    </DropdownMenuLabel>

                    <DropdownMenuSeparator />

                    <DropdownMenuItem asChild className="rounded-lg p-0">
                      <Link
                        to="/fitur/parser-kk"
                        className="flex w-full items-start gap-3 px-3 py-2.5"
                      >
                        <div className="mt-0.5 flex size-8 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-lg bg-blue-50 text-blue-600">
                          <FileJson2 className="size-4" />
                        </div>

                        <div>
                          <div className="text-sm font-medium text-slate-800">
                            Parser KK
                          </div>

                          <div className="mt-0.5 text-xs text-slate-400">
                            Konversi data KK menjadi terstruktur
                          </div>
                        </div>
                      </Link>
                    </DropdownMenuItem>

                    <DropdownMenuItem asChild className="mt-1 rounded-lg p-0">
                      <Link
                        to="/fitur/import-excel"
                        className="flex w-full items-start gap-3 px-3 py-2.5"
                      >
                        <div className="mt-0.5 flex size-8 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-lg bg-emerald-50 text-emerald-600">
                          <FileSpreadsheet className="size-4" />
                        </div>

                        <div>
                          <div className="text-sm font-medium text-slate-800">
                            Import Excel
                          </div>

                          <div className="mt-0.5 text-xs text-slate-400">
                            Import banyak data jemaat dari Excel
                          </div>
                        </div>
                      </Link>
                    </DropdownMenuItem>
                  </DropdownMenuContent>
                </DropdownMenu>
              </>
            ) : (
              <DesktopLink to="/event-kesehatan" active={kesehatanActive}>
                Kesehatan
              </DesktopLink>
            )}

            <AuthUserMenu />
          </nav>
        ) : null}

        {/* MOBILE BUTTON */}
        {user ? (
          <div className="ml-auto lg:hidden">
            <button
              type="button"
              className="flex size-10 items-center justify-center rounded-xl border border-slate-200/80 bg-white text-slate-600 shadow-sm transition-colors hover:border-blue-200 hover:bg-blue-50 hover:text-blue-600"
              aria-label={mobileOpen ? 'Tutup menu' : 'Buka menu'}
              aria-expanded={mobileOpen}
              onClick={() => setMobileOpen((current) => !current)}
            >
              {mobileOpen ? (
                <X className="size-5" />
              ) : (
                <Menu className="size-5" />
              )}
            </button>
          </div>
        ) : null}
      </div>

      {/* MOBILE NAV */}
      {user && mobileOpen ? (
        <div className="border-t border-blue-100/70 bg-white/95 shadow-lg backdrop-blur-xl lg:hidden">
          <nav className="mx-auto w-full max-w-7xl px-4 py-4 sm:px-6">
            <div className="space-y-1">
              {user.role === 'ADMIN' ? (
                <>
                  <MobileLink
                    to="/"
                    active={pathname === '/'}
                    onClick={closeMobileMenu}
                  >
                    Beranda
                  </MobileLink>

                  <MobileSection title="Data Jemaat" />

                  <MobileLink
                    to="/data-kk"
                    active={pathname.startsWith('/data-kk')}
                    onClick={closeMobileMenu}
                    icon={<Database className="size-4" />}
                  >
                    Data Kartu Keluarga
                  </MobileLink>

                  <MobileLink
                    to="/data-anggota"
                    active={pathname.startsWith('/data-anggota')}
                    onClick={closeMobileMenu}
                    icon={<UsersRound className="size-4" />}
                  >
                    Data Anggota
                  </MobileLink>

                  <MobileSection title="Pelayanan" />

                  <MobileLink
                    to="/event-kesehatan"
                    active={kesehatanActive}
                    onClick={closeMobileMenu}
                    icon={<HeartPulse className="size-4" />}
                  >
                    Kesehatan
                  </MobileLink>

                  <MobileSection title="Fitur" />

                  <MobileLink
                    to="/fitur/parser-kk"
                    active={fiturActive}
                    onClick={closeMobileMenu}
                    icon={<FileJson2 className="size-4" />}
                  >
                    Parser KK
                  </MobileLink>

                  <MobileLink
                    to="/fitur/import-excel"
                    active={pathname.startsWith('/fitur/import-excel')}
                    onClick={closeMobileMenu}
                    icon={<FileSpreadsheet className="size-4" />}
                  >
                    Import Excel
                  </MobileLink>

                  <MobileSection title="Administrator" />

                  <MobileLink
                    to="/admin/users"
                    active={pathname.startsWith('/admin/users')}
                    onClick={closeMobileMenu}
                    icon={<UsersRound className="size-4" />}
                  >
                    Kelola User
                  </MobileLink>
                </>
              ) : (
                <>
                  <MobileSection title="Pelayanan" />

                  <MobileLink
                    to="/event-kesehatan"
                    active={kesehatanActive}
                    onClick={closeMobileMenu}
                    icon={<HeartPulse className="size-4" />}
                  >
                    Kesehatan
                  </MobileLink>
                </>
              )}

              <MobileSection title="Akun" />

              <MobileLink
                to="/akun"
                active={pathname === '/akun'}
                onClick={closeMobileMenu}
                icon={<KeyRound className="size-4" />}
              >
                Ganti Password
              </MobileLink>

              <div className="mt-4 border-t border-slate-100 pt-4">
                <div className="mb-3 px-3">
                  <div className="text-sm font-semibold text-slate-800">
                    {user.nama}
                  </div>

                  <div className="mt-0.5 text-xs text-slate-400">
                    {user.role === 'ADMIN'
                      ? 'Administrator'
                      : 'Petugas Kesehatan'}
                  </div>
                </div>

                <MobileLogoutButton />
              </div>
            </div>
          </nav>
        </div>
      ) : null}
    </header>
  )
}

type DesktopLinkProps = {
  to: '/' | '/event-kesehatan'

  active: boolean
  children: ReactNode
}

function DesktopLink({ to, active, children }: DesktopLinkProps) {
  return (
    <Link to={to} className={navClass(active)}>
      {children}
    </Link>
  )
}

function navClass(active: boolean) {
  return [
    'relative flex h-10 items-center gap-1.5 rounded-full px-4',
    'text-sm font-medium transition-[background-color,color,box-shadow] duration-200',
    'focus-visible:outline-none focus-visible:ring-2 focus-visible:ring-blue-500/30',
    active
      ? 'bg-blue-50 text-blue-700 shadow-[inset_0_0_0_1px_rgba(59,130,246,0.08)]'
      : 'text-slate-600 hover:bg-slate-50 hover:text-blue-600',
  ].join(' ')
}

type MobileLinkProps = {
  to:
    | '/'
    | '/data-kk'
    | '/data-anggota'
    | '/event-kesehatan'
    | '/fitur/parser-kk'
    | '/admin/users'
    | '/akun'
    | '/fitur/import-excel'

  active: boolean
  onClick: () => void
  icon?: ReactNode
  children: ReactNode
}

function MobileLink({ to, active, onClick, icon, children }: MobileLinkProps) {
  return (
    <Link
      to={to}
      onClick={onClick}
      className={
        active
          ? 'flex items-center gap-3 rounded-xl bg-blue-50 px-3.5 py-3 text-sm font-medium text-blue-700'
          : 'flex items-center gap-3 rounded-xl px-3.5 py-3 text-sm font-medium text-slate-600 transition-colors hover:bg-slate-50 hover:text-blue-600'
      }
    >
      {icon ? (
        <div
          className={
            active
              ? 'flex size-8 items-center justify-center rounded-lg bg-blue-100 text-blue-600'
              : 'flex size-8 items-center justify-center rounded-lg bg-slate-100 text-slate-500'
          }
        >
          {icon}
        </div>
      ) : null}

      {children}
    </Link>
  )
}

function MobileSection({ title }: { title: string }) {
  return (
    <div className="px-3 pb-1 pt-5 text-[10px] font-bold uppercase tracking-[0.16em] text-slate-400">
      {title}
    </div>
  )
}

function MobileLogoutButton() {
  const router = useRouter()

  async function handleLogout() {
    await logout()

    await router.navigate({
      to: '/login',
      replace: true,
    })
  }

  return (
    <button
      type="button"
      onClick={() => void handleLogout()}
      className="flex w-full items-center gap-3 rounded-xl px-3 py-2.5 text-sm font-medium text-red-600 transition-colors hover:bg-red-50"
    >
      <LogOut className="size-4" />
      Keluar
    </button>
  )
}

import * as React from 'react'
import { cva } from 'class-variance-authority'
import type { VariantProps } from 'class-variance-authority'
import { cn } from 'cn'

const alertVariants = cva(
  "group/alert relative grid w-full gap-0.5 rounded-lg border px-2.5 py-2 text-left text-sm has-data-[slot=alert-action]:relative has-data-[slot=alert-action]:pr-18 has-[>svg]:grid-cols-[auto_1fr] has-[>svg]:gap-x-2 *:[svg]:row-span-2 *:[svg]:translate-y-0.5 *:[svg]:text-current *:[svg:not([class*='size-'])]:size-4",
  {
    variants: {
      variant: {
        default: 'bg-card text-card-foreground',
        destructive:
          'bg-card text-destructive *:data-[slot=alert-description]:text-destructive/90 *:[svg]:text-current',
      },
    },
    defaultVariants: {
      variant: 'default',
    },
  },
)

function Alert({
  className,
  variant,
  ...props
}: React.ComponentProps<'div'> & VariantProps<typeof alertVariants>) {
  return (
    <div
      data-slot="alert"
      role="alert"
      className={cn(alertVariants({ variant }), className)}
      {...props}
    />
  )
}

function AlertTitle({ className, ...props }: React.ComponentProps<'div'>) {
  return (
    <div
      data-slot="alert-title"
      className={cn(
        'font-medium group-has-[>svg]/alert:col-start-2 [&_a]:underline [&_a]:underline-offset-3 [&_a]:hover:text-foreground',
        className,
      )}
      {...props}
    />
  )
}

function AlertDescription({
  className,
  ...props
}: React.ComponentProps<'div'>) {
  return (
    <div
      data-slot="alert-description"
      className={cn(
        'text-sm text-balance text-muted-foreground md:text-pretty [&_a]:underline [&_a]:underline-offset-3 [&_a]:hover:text-foreground [&_p:not(:last-child)]:mb-4',
        className,
      )}
      {...props}
    />
  )
}

function AlertAction({ className, ...props }: React.ComponentProps<'div'>) {
  return (
    <div
      data-slot="alert-action"
      className={cn('absolute top-2 right-2', className)}
      {...props}
    />
  )
}

export { Alert, AlertTitle, AlertDescription, AlertAction }

import * as React from 'react'
import { cva } from 'class-variance-authority'
import type { VariantProps } from 'class-variance-authority'
import { cn } from 'cn'
import { Slot } from 'radix-ui'

const badgeVariants = cva(
  'group/badge inline-flex h-5 w-fit shrink-0 items-center justify-center gap-1 overflow-hidden rounded-4xl border border-transparent px-2 py-0.5 text-xs font-medium whitespace-nowrap transition-all focus-visible:border-ring focus-visible:ring-[3px] focus-visible:ring-ring/50 has-data-[icon=inline-end]:pr-1.5 has-data-[icon=inline-start]:pl-1.5 aria-invalid:border-destructive aria-invalid:ring-destructive/20 dark:aria-invalid:ring-destructive/40 [&>svg]:pointer-events-none [&>svg]:size-3!',
  {
    variants: {
      variant: {
        default: 'bg-primary text-primary-foreground [a]:hover:bg-primary/80',
        secondary:
          'bg-secondary text-secondary-foreground [a]:hover:bg-secondary/80',
        destructive:
          'bg-destructive/10 text-destructive focus-visible:ring-destructive/20 dark:bg-destructive/20 dark:focus-visible:ring-destructive/40 [a]:hover:bg-destructive/20',
        outline:
          'border-border text-foreground [a]:hover:bg-muted [a]:hover:text-muted-foreground',
        ghost:
          'hover:bg-muted hover:text-muted-foreground dark:hover:bg-muted/50',
        link: 'text-primary underline-offset-4 hover:underline',
      },
    },
    defaultVariants: {
      variant: 'default',
    },
  },
)

function Badge({
  className,
  variant = 'default',
  asChild = false,
  ...props
}: React.ComponentProps<'span'> &
  VariantProps<typeof badgeVariants> & { asChild?: boolean }) {
  const Comp = asChild ? Slot.Root : 'span'

  return (
    <Comp
      data-slot="badge"
      data-variant={variant}
      className={cn(badgeVariants({ variant }), className)}
      {...props}
    />
  )
}

export { Badge, badgeVariants }

import * as React from 'react'
import { cva } from 'class-variance-authority'
import type { VariantProps } from 'class-variance-authority'
import { cn } from 'cn'
import { Slot } from 'radix-ui'

const buttonVariants = cva(
  "group/button inline-flex shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-xl border border-transparent bg-clip-padding text-sm font-medium whitespace-nowrap transition-all outline-none select-none focus-visible:border-ring focus-visible:ring-3 focus-visible:ring-ring/50 active:not-aria-[haspopup]:translate-y-px disabled:pointer-events-none disabled:opacity-50 aria-invalid:border-destructive aria-invalid:ring-3 aria-invalid:ring-destructive/20 dark:aria-invalid:border-destructive/50 dark:aria-invalid:ring-destructive/40 [&_svg]:pointer-events-none [&_svg]:shrink-0 [&_svg:not([class*='size-'])]:size-4",
  {
    variants: {
      variant: {
        default: 'bg-primary text-primary-foreground hover:bg-primary/80',

        outline:
          'border-border bg-background hover:bg-muted hover:text-foreground aria-expanded:bg-muted aria-expanded:text-foreground dark:border-input dark:bg-input/30 dark:hover:bg-input/50',

        secondary:
          'bg-secondary text-secondary-foreground hover:bg-[color-mix(in_oklch,var(--secondary),var(--foreground)_5%)] aria-expanded:bg-secondary aria-expanded:text-secondary-foreground',

        ghost:
          'hover:bg-muted hover:text-foreground aria-expanded:bg-muted aria-expanded:text-foreground dark:hover:bg-muted/50',

        destructive:
          'bg-destructive/10 text-destructive hover:bg-destructive/20 focus-visible:border-destructive/40 focus-visible:ring-destructive/20 dark:bg-destructive/20 dark:hover:bg-destructive/30 dark:focus-visible:ring-destructive/40',

        link: 'text-primary underline-offset-4 hover:underline',
      },

      size: {
        default:
          'h-8 gap-1.5 px-2.5 has-data-[icon=inline-end]:pr-2 has-data-[icon=inline-start]:pl-2',

        xs: "h-6 gap-1 px-2 text-xs has-data-[icon=inline-end]:pr-1.5 has-data-[icon=inline-start]:pl-1.5 [&_svg:not([class*='size-'])]:size-3",

        sm: "h-7 gap-1 px-2.5 text-[0.8rem] has-data-[icon=inline-end]:pr-1.5 has-data-[icon=inline-start]:pl-1.5 [&_svg:not([class*='size-'])]:size-3.5",

        lg: 'h-9 gap-1.5 px-2.5 has-data-[icon=inline-end]:pr-2 has-data-[icon=inline-start]:pl-2',

        icon: 'size-8',

        'icon-xs': "size-6 [&_svg:not([class*='size-'])]:size-3",

        'icon-sm': 'size-7',

        'icon-lg': 'size-9',
      },
    },

    defaultVariants: {
      variant: 'default',
      size: 'default',
    },
  },
)

function Button({
  className,
  variant = 'default',
  size = 'default',
  asChild = false,
  ...props
}: React.ComponentProps<'button'> &
  VariantProps<typeof buttonVariants> & {
    asChild?: boolean
  }) {
  const Comp = asChild ? Slot.Root : 'button'

  return (
    <Comp
      data-slot="button"
      data-variant={variant}
      data-size={size}
      className={cn(
        buttonVariants({
          variant,
          size,
          className,
        }),
      )}
      {...props}
    />
  )
}

export { Button, buttonVariants }

import * as React from "react"

  className,
  size = "default",
  ...props
}: React.ComponentProps<"div"> & { size?: "default" | "sm" }) {
  return (
    <div
      data-slot="card"
      data-size={size}
      className={cn(
        "group/card flex flex-col gap-(--card-spacing) overflow-hidden rounded-xl bg-card py-(--card-spacing) text-sm text-card-foreground ring-1 ring-foreground/10 [--card-spacing:--spacing(4)] has-data-[slot=card-footer]:pb-0 has-[>img:first-child]:pt-0 data-[size=sm]:[--card-spacing:--spacing(3)] data-[size=sm]:has-data-[slot=card-footer]:pb-0 *:[img:first-child]:rounded-t-xl *:[img:last-child]:rounded-b-xl",
        className
      )}
      {...props}
    />
  )
}

      data-slot="card-content"

      {...props}
    />
  )
}

function CardFooter({ className, ...props }: React.ComponentProps<"div">) {
  return (
    <div
      data-slot="card-footer"
      className={cn(
        "flex items-center rounded-b-xl border-t bg-muted/50 p-(--card-spacing)",
        className
      )}
      {...props}
    />
  )
}

############################################################
FILE: src/components/ui/dialog.tsx
############################################################

import * as React from "react"
import { cn } from "cn"
import { Dialog as DialogPrimitive } from "radix-ui"

import { Button } from "#/components/ui/button.tsx"
import { XIcon } from "lucide-react"

function Dialog({
  ...props
}: React.ComponentProps<typeof DialogPrimitive.Root>) {
  return <DialogPrimitive.Root data-slot="dialog" {...props} />
}

function DialogTrigger({
  ...props
}: React.ComponentProps<typeof DialogPrimitive.Trigger>) {
  return <DialogPrimitive.Trigger data-slot="dialog-trigger" {...props} />
}

function DialogPortal({
  ...props
}: React.ComponentProps<typeof DialogPrimitive.Portal>) {
  return <DialogPrimitive.Portal data-slot="dialog-portal" {...props} />
}

function DialogClose({
  ...props
}: React.ComponentProps<typeof DialogPrimitive.Close>) {
  return <DialogPrimitive.Close data-slot="dialog-close" {...props} />
}

function DialogOverlay({
  className,
  ...props
}: React.ComponentProps<typeof DialogPrimitive.Overlay>) {
  return (
    <DialogPrimitive.Overlay
      data-slot="dialog-overlay"
      className={cn(
        "fixed inset-0 isolate z-50 bg-black/10 duration-100 supports-backdrop-filter:backdrop-blur-xs data-open:animate-in data-open:fade-in-0 data-closed:animate-out data-closed:fade-out-0",
        className
      )}
      {...props}
    />
  )
}

function DialogContent({
  className,
  children,
  showCloseButton = true,
  ...props
}: React.ComponentProps<typeof DialogPrimitive.Content> & {
  showCloseButton?: boolean
}) {
  return (
    <DialogPortal>
      <DialogOverlay />
      <DialogPrimitive.Content
        data-slot="dialog-content"
        className={cn(
          "fixed top-1/2 left-1/2 z-50 grid w-full max-w-[calc(100%-2rem)] -translate-x-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 gap-4 rounded-xl bg-popover p-4 text-sm text-popover-foreground ring-1 ring-foreground/10 duration-100 outline-none sm:max-w-sm data-open:animate-in data-open:fade-in-0 data-open:zoom-in-95 data-closed:animate-out data-closed:fade-out-0 data-closed:zoom-out-95",
          className
        )}
        {...props}
      >
        {children}
        {showCloseButton && (
          <DialogPrimitive.Close data-slot="dialog-close" asChild>
            <Button
              variant="ghost"
              className="absolute top-2 right-2"
              size="icon-sm"
            >
              <XIcon
              />
              <span className="sr-only">Close</span>
            </Button>
          </DialogPrimitive.Close>
        )}
      </DialogPrimitive.Content>
    </DialogPortal>
  )
}

function DialogHeader({ className, ...props }: React.ComponentProps<"div">) {
  return (
    <div
      data-slot="dialog-header"
      className={cn("flex flex-col gap-2", className)}
      {...props}
    />
  )
}

function DialogFooter({
  className,
  showCloseButton = false,
  children,
  ...props
}: React.ComponentProps<"div"> & {
  showCloseButton?: boolean
}) {
  return (
    <div
      data-slot="dialog-footer"
      className={cn(
        "-mx-4 -mb-4 flex flex-col-reverse gap-2 rounded-b-xl border-t bg-muted/50 p-4 sm:flex-row sm:justify-end",
        className
      )}
      {...props}
    >
      {children}
      {showCloseButton && (
        <DialogPrimitive.Close asChild>
          <Button variant="outline">Close</Button>
        </DialogPrimitive.Close>
      )}
    </div>
  )
}

function DialogTitle({
  className,
  ...props
}: React.ComponentProps<typeof DialogPrimitive.Title>) {
  return (
    <DialogPrimitive.Title
      data-slot="dialog-title"
      className={cn(
        "font-heading text-base leading-none font-medium",
        className
      )}
      {...props}
    />
  )
}

function DialogDescription({
  className,
  ...props
}: React.ComponentProps<typeof DialogPrimitive.Description>) {
  return (
    <DialogPrimitive.Description
      data-slot="dialog-description"
      className={cn(
        "text-sm text-muted-foreground *:[a]:underline *:[a]:underline-offset-3 *:[a]:hover:text-foreground",
        className
      )}
      {...props}
    />
  )
}

export {
  Dialog,
  DialogClose,
  DialogContent,
  DialogDescription,
  DialogFooter,
  DialogHeader,
  DialogOverlay,
  DialogPortal,
  DialogTitle,
  DialogTrigger,
}

'use client'

import { useMemo } from 'react'
import { cva } from 'class-variance-authority'
import type { VariantProps } from 'class-variance-authority'
import { cn } from 'cn'

import { Label } from '#/components/ui/label.tsx'
import { Separator } from '#/components/ui/separator.tsx'

function FieldSet({ className, ...props }: React.ComponentProps<'fieldset'>) {
  return (
    <fieldset
      data-slot="field-set"
      className={cn(
        'flex flex-col gap-4 has-[>[data-slot=checkbox-group]]:gap-3 has-[>[data-slot=radio-group]]:gap-3',
        className,
      )}
      {...props}
    />
  )
}

function FieldLegend({
  className,
  variant = 'legend',
  ...props
}: React.ComponentProps<'legend'> & { variant?: 'legend' | 'label' }) {
  return (
    <legend
      data-slot="field-legend"
      data-variant={variant}
      className={cn(
        'mb-1.5 font-medium data-[variant=label]:text-sm data-[variant=legend]:text-base',
        className,
      )}
      {...props}
    />
  )
}

function FieldGroup({ className, ...props }: React.ComponentProps<'div'>) {
  return (
    <div
      data-slot="field-group"
      className={cn(
        'group/field-group @container/field-group flex w-full flex-col gap-5 data-[slot=checkbox-group]:gap-3 *:data-[slot=field-group]:gap-4',
        className,
      )}
      {...props}
    />
  )
}

const fieldVariants = cva(
  'group/field flex w-full gap-2 data-[invalid=true]:text-destructive',
  {
    variants: {
      orientation: {
        vertical: 'flex-col *:w-full [&>.sr-only]:w-auto',
        horizontal:
          'flex-row items-center has-[>[data-slot=field-content]]:items-start *:data-[slot=field-label]:flex-auto has-[>[data-slot=field-content]]:[&>[role=checkbox],[role=radio]]:mt-px',
        responsive:
          'flex-col *:w-full @md/field-group:flex-row @md/field-group:items-center @md/field-group:*:w-auto @md/field-group:has-[>[data-slot=field-content]]:items-start @md/field-group:*:data-[slot=field-label]:flex-auto [&>.sr-only]:w-auto @md/field-group:has-[>[data-slot=field-content]]:[&>[role=checkbox],[role=radio]]:mt-px',
      },
    },
    defaultVariants: {
      orientation: 'vertical',
    },
  },
)

function Field({
  className,
  orientation = 'vertical',
  ...props
}: React.ComponentProps<'div'> & VariantProps<typeof fieldVariants>) {
  return (
    <div
      role="group"
      data-slot="field"
      data-orientation={orientation}
      className={cn(fieldVariants({ orientation }), className)}
      {...props}
    />
  )
}

function FieldContent({ className, ...props }: React.ComponentProps<'div'>) {
  return (
    <div
      data-slot="field-content"
      className={cn(
        'group/field-content flex flex-1 flex-col gap-0.5 leading-snug',
        className,
      )}
      {...props}
    />
  )
}

function FieldLabel({
  className,
  ...props
}: React.ComponentProps<typeof Label>) {
  return (
    <Label
      data-slot="field-label"
      className={cn(
        'group/field-label peer/field-label flex w-fit gap-2 leading-snug group-data-[disabled=true]/field:opacity-50 has-data-checked:border-primary/30 has-data-checked:bg-primary/5 has-[>[data-slot=field]]:rounded-lg has-[>[data-slot=field]]:border has-[>[data-slot=field]]:not-has-[:disabled,[data-disabled]]:hover:bg-muted/50 has-[>[data-slot=field]]:has-focus-visible:border-ring has-[>[data-slot=field]]:has-focus-visible:ring-3 has-[>[data-slot=field]]:has-focus-visible:ring-ring/50 *:data-[slot=field]:p-2.5 dark:has-data-checked:border-primary/20 dark:has-data-checked:bg-primary/10',
        'has-[>[data-slot=field]]:w-full has-[>[data-slot=field]]:flex-col',
        className,
      )}
      {...props}
    />
  )
}

function FieldTitle({ className, ...props }: React.ComponentProps<'div'>) {
  return (
    <div
      data-slot="field-label"
      className={cn(
        'flex w-fit items-center gap-2 text-sm font-medium group-data-[disabled=true]/field:opacity-50',
        className,
      )}
      {...props}
    />
  )
}

function FieldDescription({ className, ...props }: React.ComponentProps<'p'>) {
  return (
    <p
      data-slot="field-description"
      className={cn(
        'text-left text-sm leading-normal font-normal text-muted-foreground group-has-data-horizontal/field:text-balance [[data-variant=legend]+&]:-mt-1.5',
        'last:mt-0 nth-last-2:-mt-1',
        '[&>a]:underline [&>a]:underline-offset-4 [&>a:hover]:text-primary',
        className,
      )}
      {...props}
    />
  )
}

function FieldSeparator({
  children,
  className,
  ...props
}: React.ComponentProps<'div'> & {
  children?: React.ReactNode
}) {
  return (
    <div
      data-slot="field-separator"
      data-content={!!children}
      className={cn(
        'relative -my-2 h-5 text-sm group-data-[variant=outline]/field-group:-mb-2',
        className,
      )}
      {...props}
    >
      <Separator className="absolute inset-0 top-1/2" />
      {children && (
        <span
          className="relative mx-auto block w-fit bg-background px-2 text-muted-foreground"
          data-slot="field-separator-content"
        >
          {children}
        </span>
      )}
    </div>
  )
}

function FieldError({
  className,
  children,
  errors,
  ...props
}: React.ComponentProps<'div'> & {
  errors?: Array<{ message?: string } | undefined>
}) {
  const content = useMemo(() => {
    if (children) {
      return children
    }

    if (!errors?.length) {
      return null
    }

    const uniqueErrors = [
      ...new Map(errors.map((error) => [error?.message, error])).values(),
    ]

    if (uniqueErrors.length === 1) {
      return uniqueErrors[0]?.message
    }

    return (
      <ul className="ml-4 flex list-disc flex-col gap-1">
        {uniqueErrors.map(
          (error, index) =>
            error?.message && <li key={index}>{error.message}</li>,
        )}
      </ul>
    )
  }, [children, errors])

  if (!content) {
    return null
  }

  return (
    <div
      role="alert"
      data-slot="field-error"
      className={cn('text-sm font-normal text-destructive', className)}
      {...props}
    >
      {content}
    </div>
  )
}

export {
  Field,
  FieldLabel,
  FieldDescription,
  FieldError,
  FieldGroup,
  FieldLegend,
  FieldSeparator,
  FieldSet,
  FieldContent,
  FieldTitle,
}

      )}
      {...props}
    >
      {children}
      <SelectPrimitive.Icon asChild>
        <ChevronDownIcon className="pointer-events-none size-4 text-muted-foreground" />
      </SelectPrimitive.Icon>
    </SelectPrimitive.Trigger>
  )
}

        position={position}

############################################################
FILE: src/hooks/use-kk-parser.ts
############################################################

import { useMemo, useState } from 'react'

import type { KKData } from '@/lib/kk-parser/types'
import { createMaskedKK } from '@/lib/kk-parser/mask'
import { parseKK } from '@/lib/kk-parser/parse-kk'
import { kkSchema } from '@/lib/kk-parser/schema'

import { saveKK } from '@/server/kk.functions'
import { toast } from 'sonner'

type ValidationIssue = {
  path: readonly PropertyKey[]
  message: string
}

function formatValidationErrors(issues: readonly ValidationIssue[]): string[] {
  return issues.map((issue) => {
    const field =
      issue.path.length > 0 ? issue.path.map(String).join('.') : 'data'

    return `${field}: ${issue.message}`
  })
}

export function useKKParser() {
  const [text, setTextState] = useState('')

  const [result, setResult] = useState<KKData | null>(null)

  const [parseError, setParseError] = useState<string | null>(null)

  const [validationErrors, setValidationErrors] = useState<string[]>([])

  const [isValid, setIsValid] = useState(false)

  const [showSensitive, setShowSensitive] = useState(false)

  const [isSaving, setIsSaving] = useState(false)

  const [isSaved, setIsSaved] = useState(false)

  const previewData = useMemo(() => {
    if (!result) {
      return null
    }

    return showSensitive ? result : createMaskedKK(result)
  }, [result, showSensitive])

  function resetResultState() {
    setResult(null)
    setParseError(null)
    setValidationErrors([])
    setIsValid(false)
    setShowSensitive(false)
    setIsSaved(false)
  }

  function setText(value: string) {
    setTextState(value)

    // Kalau raw text berubah setelah diparse,
    // hasil lama tidak lagi dianggap valid.
    if (result) {
      resetResultState()
    }
  }

  function handleParse() {
    setParseError(null)
    setResult(null)
    setValidationErrors([])
    setIsValid(false)
    setShowSensitive(false)
    setIsSaved(false)

    if (!text.trim()) {
      setParseError('Text Kartu Keluarga belum diisi.')
      return
    }

    try {
      const parsed = parseKK(text)

      setResult(parsed)

      const validation = kkSchema.safeParse(parsed)

      if (!validation.success) {
        setValidationErrors(formatValidationErrors(validation.error.issues))

        return
      }

      setIsValid(true)
    } catch {
      setParseError('Terjadi kesalahan saat melakukan parsing text.')
    }
  }

  function handleClear() {
    setTextState('')
    resetResultState()
    setIsSaving(false)
  }

  async function handleCopy() {
    if (!previewData) {
      return
    }

    try {
      await navigator.clipboard.writeText(JSON.stringify(previewData, null, 2))

      toast.success('JSON berhasil disalin')
    } catch {
      toast.error('Gagal menyalin JSON')
    }
  }

  async function handleSave() {
    if (!result || isSaving) {
      return
    }

    setIsSaving(true)

    try {
      const validation = kkSchema.safeParse(result)

      if (!validation.success) {
        const errors = formatValidationErrors(validation.error.issues)

        setValidationErrors(errors)
        setIsValid(false)

        toast.error('Data belum dapat disimpan', {
          description:
            'Data tidak lolos validasi. Periksa kembali hasil parsing.',
        })

        return
      }

      const response = await saveKK({
        data: validation.data,
      })

      setIsSaved(true)

      toast.success('Data berhasil disimpan', {
        description: `${response.memberCount} anggota keluarga berhasil disimpan ke database.`,
      })
    } catch (err) {
      const message =
        err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'Terjadi kesalahan pada server.'

      toast.error('Gagal menyimpan data', {
        description: message,
      })
    } finally {
      setIsSaving(false)
    }
  }

  function toggleSensitive() {
    if (!result) {
      return
    }

    setShowSensitive((current) => !current)
  }

  return {
    text,
    result,
    previewData,

    parseError,
    validationErrors,

    isValid,
    showSensitive,

    isSaving,
    isSaved,

    canClear: Boolean(text || result || parseError),

    canSave: Boolean(result) && isValid && !isSaving && !isSaved,

    setText,
    handleParse,
    handleClear,
    handleCopy,
    handleSave,
    toggleSensitive,
  }
}


############################################################
FILE: src/hooks/use-list-controller.ts
############################################################

/* eslint-disable @typescript-eslint/no-unnecessary-type-assertion */
import { useCallback, useMemo } from 'react'

import type { SortingState } from '@tanstack/react-table'

import type { PageSize } from '@/components/data-list/data-pagination'

type SearchState = {
  page?: number
  limit?: PageSize

  q?: string
  by?: string

  sort?: string
  order?: 'asc' | 'desc'
}

type Props<TSearch extends SearchState> = {
  search: TSearch

  defaultSort: string
  defaultOrder?: 'asc' | 'desc'

  allowedSorts: ReadonlySet<string>

  navigate: (options: {
    search: (previous: TSearch) => TSearch
  }) => void | Promise<void>
}

export function useListController<TSearch extends SearchState>({
  search,
  defaultSort,
  defaultOrder = 'asc',
  allowedSorts,
  navigate,
}: Props<TSearch>) {
  const sorting: SortingState = useMemo(
    () => [
      {
        id: search.sort ?? defaultSort,

        desc: (search.order ?? defaultOrder) === 'desc',
      },
    ],
    [search.sort, search.order, defaultSort, defaultOrder],
  )

  const searchChange = useCallback(
    (value: string) => {
      void navigate({
        search: (previous) =>
          ({
            ...previous,

            page: undefined,

            q: value.trim() || undefined,
          }) as TSearch,
      })
    },
    [navigate],
  )

  const searchByChange = useCallback(
    (value: string) => {
      void navigate({
        search: (previous) =>
          ({
            ...previous,

            page: undefined,

            by: value === 'all' ? undefined : value,
          }) as TSearch,
      })
    },
    [navigate],
  )

  const sortingChange = useCallback(
    (updater: SortingState | ((old: SortingState) => SortingState)) => {
      const next = typeof updater === 'function' ? updater(sorting) : updater

      if (next.length === 0) {
        return
      }

      const first = next[0]

      if (!allowedSorts.has(first.id)) {
        return
      }

      void navigate({
        search: (previous) =>
          ({
            ...previous,

            page: undefined,

            sort: first.id,

            order: first.desc ? 'desc' : 'asc',
          }) as TSearch,
      })
    },
    [allowedSorts, navigate, sorting],
  )

  const pageChange = useCallback(
    (page: number) => {
      void navigate({
        search: (previous) =>
          ({
            ...previous,

            page: page <= 1 ? undefined : page,
          }) as TSearch,
      })
    },
    [navigate],
  )

  const pageSizeChange = useCallback(
    (limit: PageSize) => {
      void navigate({
        search: (previous) =>
          ({
            ...previous,

            page: undefined,

            limit: limit === 20 ? undefined : limit,
          }) as TSearch,
      })
    },
    [navigate],
  )

  return {
    sorting,

    searchChange,
    searchByChange,
    sortingChange,

    pageChange,
    pageSizeChange,
  }
}


############################################################
FILE: src/hooks/use-sensitive-kk.ts
############################################################

import { useState } from 'react'

import { getKKPrivateLocal } from '@/server/kk.local.functions'

type PrivateKK = Awaited<ReturnType<typeof getKKPrivateLocal>>

export function useSensitiveKK(id: number) {
  const [data, setData] = useState<PrivateKK | null>(null)

  const [isVisible, setIsVisible] = useState(false)

  const [isLoading, setIsLoading] = useState(false)

  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null)

  async function toggle() {
    if (isVisible) {
      hide()
      return
    }

    setIsLoading(true)
    setError(null)

    try {
      const response = await getKKPrivateLocal({
        data: {
          id,
        },
      })

      setData(response)
      setIsVisible(true)
    } catch (err) {
      setError(
        err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'Gagal membuka data sensitif.',
      )
    } finally {
      setIsLoading(false)
    }
  }

  function hide() {
    setIsVisible(false)

    // Plaintext langsung dibuang dari React state.
    setData(null)
  }

  function getNik(memberNo: number, maskedNik: string) {
    if (!isVisible || !data) {
      return maskedNik
    }

    return (
      data.kk.anggota.find((member) => member.no === memberNo)?.nik ?? maskedNik
    )
  }

  function getNoKK(maskedNoKK: string) {
    if (!isVisible || !data) {
      return maskedNoKK
    }

    return data.kk.noKK
  }

  return {
    data,
    isVisible,
    isLoading,
    error,

    toggle,
    hide,
    getNik,
    getNoKK,
  }
}


############################################################
FILE: src/lib/kk-parser/mask.ts
############################################################

import type { KKData } from './types'

function maskNumber(value: string | null) {
  if (!value) {
    return value
  }

  if (value.length <= 4) {
    return '*'.repeat(value.length)
  }

  return '*'.repeat(value.length - 4) + value.slice(-4)
}

export function createMaskedKK(data: KKData): KKData {
  return {
    ...data,

    noKK: maskNumber(data.noKK),

    anggota: data.anggota.map((member) => ({
      ...member,
      nik: maskNumber(member.nik) ?? '',
    })),
  }
}


############################################################
FILE: src/lib/kk-parser/normalize.ts
############################################################

export function normalizeText(raw: string): string[] {
  return raw
    .replace(/\r/g, '')
    .split('\n')
    .map((line) => line.trim())
    .filter((line) => line.length > 0)
}

export function cleanValue(value?: string | null): string | null {
  if (!value) {
    return null
  }

  return value.replace(/^:\s*/, '').trim()
}

export function cleanSpaces(value: string): string {
  return value.replace(/\s+/g, ' ').trim()
}


############################################################
FILE: src/lib/kk-parser/parse-excel.ts
############################################################

import type { KKData, KKMember } from './types'

import { kkSchema } from './schema'

type ExcelValue = string | number | boolean | Date | null | undefined
type ExcelRow = ExcelValue[]

const REQUIRED_HEADERS = [
  'Keluarga',
  'Nomor',
  'Nama',
  'No KK',
  'NIK',
  'Alamat',

  'RT/RW',
  'Desa/Kelurahan',
  'Kecamatan',
  'Kabupaten/Kota',
  'Provinsi',
  'Kode Pos',

  'L/P',
  'Tempat Lahir',
  'Tanggal Lahir',

  'Agama',
  'Pendidikan',
  'Pekerjaan',
  'Golongan Darah',

  'Ayah',
  'Ibu',
  'Baptis Anak',
  'Sidi/Baptis Dewasa',
  'Menikah',
  'Domisili',
] as const

type HeaderName = (typeof REQUIRED_HEADERS)[number]

type FamilyBuffer = {
  key: string

  noKK: string | null
  alamat: string | null

  rt: string | null
  rw: string | null

  desaKelurahan: string | null
  kecamatan: string | null
  kabupatenKota: string | null
  provinsi: string | null
  kodePos: string | null

  anggota: KKMember[]
}

export type ExcelPreviewRow = {
  rowNumber: number

  keluarga: string
  nomor: string
  nama: string

  noKK: string
  nik: string

  alamat: string
  rtRw: string
  desaKelurahan: string
  kecamatan: string
  kabupatenKota: string
  provinsi: string
  kodePos: string

  jenisKelamin: string
  tempatLahir: string
  tanggalLahir: string

  agama: string
  pendidikan: string
  pekerjaan: string
  golonganDarah: string

  ayah: string
  ibu: string

  baptisAnak: string
  sidiBaptisDewasa: string
  menikah: string

  domisili: string
}

export type ExcelKKImportResult = {
  data: KKData[]
  preview: ExcelPreviewRow[]
  errors: string[]
  totalRows: number
}

export async function parseKKExcel(file: File): Promise<ExcelKKImportResult> {
  const { readSheet } = await import('read-excel-file/browser')

  const rows = (await readSheet(file)) as ExcelRow[]

  if (rows.length === 0) {
    return {
      data: [],
      preview: [],
      errors: ['File Excel tidak memiliki data.'],
      totalRows: 0,
    }
  }

  const headerRow = rows[0]

  const headerIndexes = new Map<string, number>()

  headerRow.forEach((value, index) => {
    const header = textValue(value)

    if (header) {
      headerIndexes.set(header, index)
    }
  })

  const missingHeaders = REQUIRED_HEADERS.filter(
    (header) => !headerIndexes.has(header),
  )

  if (missingHeaders.length > 0) {
    return {
      data: [],
      preview: [],
      errors: [`Kolom Excel tidak lengkap: ${missingHeaders.join(', ')}.`],
      totalRows: Math.max(rows.length - 1, 0),
    }
  }

  function cell(row: ExcelRow, name: HeaderName): ExcelValue {
    const index = headerIndexes.get(name)

    if (index === undefined) {
      return null
    }

    return row[index]
  }

  const preview: ExcelPreviewRow[] = []
  const errors: string[] = []

  const families = new Map<string, FamilyBuffer>()

  let currentFamilyKey: string | null = null

  for (let index = 1; index < rows.length; index += 1) {
    const row = rows[index]
    const excelRowNumber = index + 1

    if (isEmptyRow(row)) {
      continue
    }

    /*
     * Tentukan keluarga aktif.
     *
     * Pada format Excel, kolom Keluarga boleh hanya
     * diisi pada baris pertama keluarga.
     */
    const familyCell = uppercaseText(cell(row, 'Keluarga')) ?? ''

    if (familyCell) {
      currentFamilyKey = familyCell
    }

    /*
     * PREVIEW DIBUAT TERLEBIH DAHULU.
     *
     * Jadi baris tetap terlihat walaupun nantinya
     * gagal validasi.
     */
    preview.push({
      rowNumber: excelRowNumber,

      keluarga: familyCell || currentFamilyKey || '',

      nomor: textValue(cell(row, 'Nomor')),

      nama: requiredUppercaseText(cell(row, 'Nama')),

      noKK: textValue(cell(row, 'No KK')),

      nik: textValue(cell(row, 'NIK')),

      alamat: uppercaseText(cell(row, 'Alamat')) ?? '',

      rtRw: textValue(cell(row, 'RT/RW')),

      desaKelurahan: uppercaseText(cell(row, 'Desa/Kelurahan')) ?? '',

      kecamatan: uppercaseText(cell(row, 'Kecamatan')) ?? '',

      kabupatenKota: uppercaseText(cell(row, 'Kabupaten/Kota')) ?? '',

      provinsi: uppercaseText(cell(row, 'Provinsi')) ?? '',

      kodePos: textValue(cell(row, 'Kode Pos')),

      jenisKelamin:
        genderValue(cell(row, 'L/P')) ??
        textValue(cell(row, 'L/P')).toLocaleUpperCase('id-ID'),

      tempatLahir: uppercaseText(cell(row, 'Tempat Lahir')) ?? '',

      tanggalLahir: dateOrText(cell(row, 'Tanggal Lahir')) ?? '',

      agama: uppercaseText(cell(row, 'Agama')) ?? '',

      pendidikan: uppercaseText(cell(row, 'Pendidikan')) ?? '',

      pekerjaan: uppercaseText(cell(row, 'Pekerjaan')) ?? '',

      golonganDarah: uppercaseText(cell(row, 'Golongan Darah')) ?? '',

      ayah: uppercaseText(cell(row, 'Ayah')) ?? '',

      ibu: uppercaseText(cell(row, 'Ibu')) ?? '',

      baptisAnak: dateOrUppercaseText(cell(row, 'Baptis Anak')) ?? '',

      sidiBaptisDewasa:
        dateOrUppercaseText(cell(row, 'Sidi/Baptis Dewasa')) ?? '',

      menikah: dateOrUppercaseText(cell(row, 'Menikah')) ?? '',

      domisili: uppercaseText(cell(row, 'Domisili')) ?? '',
    })

    /*
     * Setelah preview dibuat baru lakukan validasi.
     */
    if (!currentFamilyKey) {
      errors.push(
        `Baris ${excelRowNumber}: kolom Keluarga belum memiliki nilai.`,
      )

      continue
    }

    let family = families.get(currentFamilyKey)

    if (!family) {
      family = {
        key: currentFamilyKey,

        noKK: null,
        alamat: null,

        rt: null,
        rw: null,

        desaKelurahan: null,
        kecamatan: null,
        kabupatenKota: null,
        provinsi: null,
        kodePos: null,

        anggota: [],
      }

      families.set(currentFamilyKey, family)
    }

    /*
     * NO KK
     */
    try {
      const noKKValue = identityValue(cell(row, 'No KK'), 'No KK')

      if (noKKValue) {
        if (family.noKK && family.noKK !== noKKValue) {
          errors.push(
            `Baris ${excelRowNumber}: No KK berbeda dalam Keluarga ${currentFamilyKey}.`,
          )
        } else {
          family.noKK = noKKValue
        }
      }
    } catch (error) {
      errors.push(
        `Baris ${excelRowNumber}: ${
          error instanceof Error ? error.message : 'No KK tidak dapat dibaca.'
        }`,
      )
    }

    /*
     * ALAMAT
     */
    const alamat = uppercaseText(cell(row, 'Alamat'))

    if (alamat) {
      if (family.alamat && family.alamat !== alamat) {
        errors.push(
          `Baris ${excelRowNumber}: Alamat berbeda dalam Keluarga ${currentFamilyKey}.`,
        )
      } else {
        family.alamat = alamat
      }
    }

    /*
     * ALAMAT ADMINISTRATIF
     */
    const rtRw = parseRtRw(nullableText(cell(row, 'RT/RW')))

    if (rtRw.rt) {
      family.rt = rtRw.rt
    }

    if (rtRw.rw) {
      family.rw = rtRw.rw
    }

    const desaKelurahan = uppercaseText(cell(row, 'Desa/Kelurahan'))

    if (desaKelurahan) {
      family.desaKelurahan = desaKelurahan
    }

    const kecamatan = uppercaseText(cell(row, 'Kecamatan'))

    if (kecamatan) {
      family.kecamatan = kecamatan
    }

    const kabupatenKota = uppercaseText(cell(row, 'Kabupaten/Kota'))

    if (kabupatenKota) {
      family.kabupatenKota = kabupatenKota
    }

    const provinsi = uppercaseText(cell(row, 'Provinsi'))

    if (provinsi) {
      family.provinsi = provinsi
    }

    const kodePos = digitsOrNull(cell(row, 'Kode Pos'))

    if (kodePos) {
      family.kodePos = kodePos
    }

    /*
     * NOMOR ANGGOTA
     */
    const nomor = positiveInteger(cell(row, 'Nomor'))

    if (nomor === null) {
      errors.push(`Baris ${excelRowNumber}: Nomor anggota tidak valid.`)

      continue
    }

    /*
     * NAMA
     */
    const nama = requiredUppercaseText(cell(row, 'Nama'))

    if (!nama) {
      errors.push(`Baris ${excelRowNumber}: Nama anggota tidak boleh kosong.`)

      continue
    }

    /*
     * NIK
     */
    let nik: string | null = null

    try {
      nik = identityValue(cell(row, 'NIK'), 'NIK')
    } catch (error) {
      errors.push(
        `Baris ${excelRowNumber}: ${
          error instanceof Error ? error.message : 'NIK tidak dapat dibaca.'
        }`,
      )

      continue
    }

    if (!nik) {
      errors.push(`Baris ${excelRowNumber}: NIK tidak boleh kosong.`)

      continue
    }

    /*
     * JENIS KELAMIN
     */
    const rawGender = textValue(cell(row, 'L/P'))

    const jenisKelamin = genderValue(cell(row, 'L/P'))

    if (rawGender && jenisKelamin === null) {
      errors.push(`Baris ${excelRowNumber}: L/P harus berisi L atau P.`)
    }

    /*
     * ANGGOTA
     */
    family.anggota.push({
      no: nomor,

      nama,

      nik,

      jenisKelamin,

      tempatLahir: uppercaseText(cell(row, 'Tempat Lahir')),

      tanggalLahir: dateOrText(cell(row, 'Tanggal Lahir')),

      agama: uppercaseText(cell(row, 'Agama')),

      pendidikan: uppercaseText(cell(row, 'Pendidikan')),

      pekerjaan: uppercaseText(cell(row, 'Pekerjaan')),

      golonganDarah: uppercaseText(cell(row, 'Golongan Darah')),

      ayah: uppercaseText(cell(row, 'Ayah')),

      ibu: uppercaseText(cell(row, 'Ibu')),

      baptisAnak: dateOrUppercaseText(cell(row, 'Baptis Anak')),

      sidiBaptisDewasa: dateOrUppercaseText(cell(row, 'Sidi/Baptis Dewasa')),

      menikah: dateOrUppercaseText(cell(row, 'Menikah')),

      domisili: uppercaseText(cell(row, 'Domisili')),
    })
  }

  /*
   * BENTUK DATA KK
   */
  const result: KKData[] = []

  for (const family of families.values()) {
    const anggota = [...family.anggota].sort((a, b) => a.no - b.no)

    const kepala = anggota.find((member) => member.no === 1)

    if (!kepala) {
      errors.push(
        `Keluarga ${family.key}: anggota dengan Nomor 1 tidak ditemukan.`,
      )

      continue
    }

    const candidate: KKData = {
      noKK: family.noKK,

      kepalaKeluarga: kepala.nama,

      alamat: family.alamat,

      rt: family.rt,
      rw: family.rw,

      desaKelurahan: family.desaKelurahan,

      kecamatan: family.kecamatan,

      kabupatenKota: family.kabupatenKota,

      kodePos: family.kodePos,

      provinsi: family.provinsi,

      anggota,
    }

    const validation = kkSchema.safeParse(candidate)

    if (!validation.success) {
      for (const issue of validation.error.issues) {
        const field =
          issue.path.length > 0 ? issue.path.map(String).join('.') : 'data'

        errors.push(`Keluarga ${family.key} — ${field}: ${issue.message}`)
      }

      continue
    }

    result.push(candidate)
  }

  return {
    data: result,
    preview,
    errors,
    totalRows: Math.max(rows.length - 1, 0),
  }
}

function textValue(value: ExcelValue): string {
  if (value === null || value === undefined) {
    return ''
  }

  if (value instanceof Date) {
    return formatDate(value)
  }

  return String(value).trim()
}

function nullableText(value: ExcelValue): string | null {
  const text = textValue(value)

  return text || null
}

function uppercaseText(value: ExcelValue): string | null {
  const text = nullableText(value)

  return text ? text.toLocaleUpperCase('id-ID') : null
}

function requiredUppercaseText(value: ExcelValue): string {
  return textValue(value).toLocaleUpperCase('id-ID')
}

function identityValue(
  value: ExcelValue,
  label: 'NIK' | 'No KK',
): string | null {
  if (value === null || value === undefined || value === '') {
    return null
  }

  /*
   * NIK / No KK harus diperlakukan
   * sebagai identitas, bukan angka hitung.
   *
   * Excel hanya menjamin sekitar 15 digit
   * signifikan sehingga identitas 16 digit
   * wajib disimpan sebagai Text.
   */
  if (typeof value === 'number') {
    if (!Number.isInteger(value) || !Number.isSafeInteger(value)) {
      throw new Error(`${label} 16 digit harus diformat sebagai Text di Excel.`)
    }

    const text = String(value)

    if (text.length >= 16) {
      throw new Error(`${label} 16 digit harus diformat sebagai Text di Excel.`)
    }

    return text
  }

  const digits = String(value).replace(/\D/g, '')

  return digits || null
}

function positiveInteger(value: ExcelValue): number | null {
  if (typeof value === 'number') {
    return Number.isInteger(value) && value > 0 ? value : null
  }

  const parsed = Number(textValue(value))

  return Number.isInteger(parsed) && parsed > 0 ? parsed : null
}

function genderValue(value: ExcelValue): 'LAKI-LAKI' | 'PEREMPUAN' | null {
  const normalized = textValue(value).toLocaleUpperCase('id-ID')

  if (
    normalized === 'L' ||
    normalized === 'LAKI-LAKI' ||
    normalized === 'LAKI LAKI'
  ) {
    return 'LAKI-LAKI'
  }

  if (normalized === 'P' || normalized === 'PEREMPUAN') {
    return 'PEREMPUAN'
  }

  return null
}

/*
 * Field tanggal biasa.
 *
 * Jika Excel menghasilkan objek Date,
 * ubah ke DD-MM-YYYY.
 *
 * Jika teks berbentuk:
 * 1/2/2020
 * 1-2-2020
 * 1.2.2020
 *
 * juga dinormalisasi menjadi:
 * 01-02-2020
 */
function dateOrText(value: ExcelValue): string | null {
  if (value === null || value === undefined || value === '') {
    return null
  }

  if (value instanceof Date) {
    return formatDate(value)
  }

  const text = String(value).trim()

  const match = text.match(/^(\d{1,2})[./-](\d{1,2})[./-](\d{4})$/)

  if (!match) {
    return text || null
  }

  const day = match[1].padStart(2, '0')

  const month = match[2].padStart(2, '0')

  const year = match[3]

  return `${day}-${month}-${year}`
}

/*
 * Khusus field yang bisa berisi tanggal
 * ATAU teks, misalnya:
 *
 * Baptis Anak
 * Sidi/Baptis Dewasa
 * Menikah
 *
 * Tanggal tetap dipertahankan sebagai tanggal,
 * sedangkan teks dibuat CAPITAL.
 */
function dateOrUppercaseText(value: ExcelValue): string | null {
  if (value === null || value === undefined || value === '') {
    return null
  }

  if (value instanceof Date) {
    return formatDate(value)
  }

  const text = String(value).trim()

  const match = text.match(/^(\d{1,2})[./-](\d{1,2})[./-](\d{4})$/)

  if (match) {
    const day = match[1].padStart(2, '0')

    const month = match[2].padStart(2, '0')

    const year = match[3]

    return `${day}-${month}-${year}`
  }

  return text ? text.toLocaleUpperCase('id-ID') : null
}

function formatDate(date: Date): string {
  const day = String(date.getUTCDate()).padStart(2, '0')

  const month = String(date.getUTCMonth() + 1).padStart(2, '0')

  const year = date.getUTCFullYear()

  return `${day}-${month}-${year}`
}

function isEmptyRow(row: ExcelRow) {
  return row.every((value) => {
    if (value === null || value === undefined) {
      return true
    }

    return typeof value === 'string' ? value.trim() === '' : false
  })
}

function parseRtRw(value: string | null) {
  if (!value) {
    return {
      rt: null,
      rw: null,
    }
  }

  const match = value.match(/^(\d{1,3})\s*\/\s*(\d{1,3})$/)

  if (!match) {
    return {
      rt: null,
      rw: null,
    }
  }

  return {
    rt: match[1],
    rw: match[2],
  }
}

function digitsOrNull(value: ExcelValue): string | null {
  if (value === null || value === undefined || value === '') {
    return null
  }

  const digits = String(value).replace(/\D/g, '')

  return digits || null
}


############################################################
FILE: src/lib/kk-parser/parse-header.ts
############################################################

import type { KKData } from './types'

type HeaderData = Omit<KKData, 'anggota'>

export function parseHeader(rawText: string): HeaderData {
  const lines = normalizeLines(rawText)

  /*
   * Hanya ambil bagian header.
   * Berhenti ketika menemukan
   * anggota pertama.
   */
  const headerLines = getHeaderLines(lines)

  const noKK = findNoKK(headerLines)

  const kepalaKeluarga = findFieldValue(headerLines, [
    'NAMA KEPALA KELUARGA',

    /*
     * OCR sering salah:
     * KEPALA → KEPAIA
     */
    'NAMA KEPAIA KELUARGA',
  ])

  const alamat = findFieldValue(headerLines, ['ALAMAT'])

  const rtRw = findFieldValue(headerLines, ['RT/RW', 'RT / RW'])

  const { rt, rw } = parseRtRw(rtRw)

  const desaKelurahan = findFieldValue(headerLines, [
    'DESA/KELURAHAN',
    'DESA / KELURAHAN',
  ])

  const kecamatan = findFieldValue(headerLines, ['KECAMATAN'])

  const kodePos = findFieldValue(headerLines, ['KODE POS'])

  const kabupatenKota = findFieldValue(headerLines, [
    'KABUPATEN/KOTA',
    'KABUPATEN / KOTA',
  ])

  const provinsi = findFieldValue(headerLines, ['PROVINSI'])

  return {
    noKK,

    kepalaKeluarga,

    alamat: nullable(alamat),

    rt,

    rw,

    desaKelurahan: nullable(desaKelurahan),

    kecamatan: nullable(kecamatan),

    kabupatenKota: nullable(kabupatenKota),

    kodePos: nullable(cleanDigits(kodePos ?? '')),

    provinsi: nullable(provinsi),
  }
}

/* ======================================================
 * HEADER BOUNDARY
 * ====================================================== */

function getHeaderLines(lines: string[]) {
  /*
   * Cari:
   *
   * 1
   * TES1
   * 331220...
   *
   * Jangan menganggap nomor lain
   * sebagai awal anggota.
   */
  for (let index = 0; index < lines.length; index += 1) {
    if (!isRowNumber(lines[index])) {
      continue
    }

    const nearby = lines.slice(index + 1, index + 6)

    const hasNik = nearby.some(isIdentityNumber)

    if (hasNik) {
      return lines.slice(0, index)
    }
  }

  return lines
}

/* ======================================================
 * NO KK
 * ====================================================== */

function findNoKK(lines: string[]): string | null {
  for (let index = 0; index < lines.length; index += 1) {
    const line = lines[index]

    /*
     * No. 3312202506090002
     */
    const inline = line.match(/^NO\s*\.?\s*:?\s*(\d{10,20})$/i)

    if (inline?.[1]) {
      return inline[1]
    }

    /*
     * No.
     * 3312202506090002
     */
    if (/^NO\s*\.?\s*:?\s*$/i.test(line)) {
      const next = cleanDigits(lines[index + 1] ?? '')

      if (isIdentityNumber(next)) {
        return next
      }
    }
  }

  return null
}

/* ======================================================
 * GENERIC HEADER FIELD
 * ====================================================== */

function findFieldValue(lines: string[], labels: string[]): string | null {
  for (let index = 0; index < lines.length; index += 1) {
    const current = normalizeSemantic(lines[index])

    for (const label of labels) {
      const normalizedLabel = normalizeSemantic(label)

      /*
       * Format:
       *
       * Alamat : JOHO
       */
      if (current.startsWith(`${normalizedLabel}:`)) {
        const separator = lines[index].indexOf(':')

        if (separator >= 0) {
          const value = cleanValue(lines[index].slice(separator + 1))

          if (value) {
            return value
          }
        }
      }

      /*
       * Format:
       *
       * Alamat
       * : JOHO
       */
      if (current === normalizedLabel) {
        for (
          let nextIndex = index + 1;
          nextIndex < Math.min(lines.length, index + 4);
          nextIndex += 1
        ) {
          const next = cleanValue(lines[nextIndex])

          if (!next) {
            continue
          }

          /*
           * Kalau baris selanjutnya
           * ternyata label baru,
           * jangan dipakai sebagai value.
           */
          if (isKnownHeaderLabel(next)) {
            break
          }

          return next
        }
      }
    }
  }

  return null
}

/* ======================================================
 * RT / RW
 * ====================================================== */

function parseRtRw(value: string | null) {
  if (!value) {
    return {
      rt: null,
      rw: null,
    }
  }

  const match = value.match(/(\d{1,3})\s*\/\s*(\d{1,3})/)

  if (!match) {
    return {
      rt: null,
      rw: null,
    }
  }

  return {
    rt: match[1],
    rw: match[2],
  }
}

/* ======================================================
 * HEADER LABEL
 * ====================================================== */

function isKnownHeaderLabel(value: string) {
  const normalized = normalizeSemantic(value)

  const labels = [
    'KARTU KELUARGA',

    'NAMA KEPALA KELUARGA',
    'NAMA KEPAIA KELUARGA',

    'ALAMAT',

    'RT/RW',
    'RT / RW',

    'DESA/KELURAHAN',
    'DESA / KELURAHAN',

    'KECAMATAN',

    'KODE POS',

    'KABUPATEN/KOTA',
    'KABUPATEN / KOTA',

    'PROVINSI',
  ]

  return labels.some((label) => normalized === normalizeSemantic(label))
}

/* ======================================================
 * UTILITY
 * ====================================================== */

function normalizeLines(value: string) {
  return value.replace(/\r/g, '').split('\n').map(cleanValue).filter(Boolean)
}

function cleanValue(value: string) {
  return value
    .replace(/^[\s:;|]+/, '')
    .replace(/\s+/g, ' ')
    .trim()
}

function normalizeSemantic(value: string) {
  return cleanValue(value)
    .toUpperCase()
    .replace(/–|—/g, '-')
    .replace(/\s*\/\s*/g, '/')
    .replace(/\s+/g, ' ')
    .trim()
}

function cleanDigits(value: string) {
  return value.replace(/\D/g, '')
}

function isIdentityNumber(value: string) {
  const digits = cleanDigits(value)

  return digits === value.trim() && digits.length >= 10 && digits.length <= 20
}

function isRowNumber(value: string) {
  return /^\d{1,2}$/.test(value.trim())
}

function nullable(value: string | null) {
  if (!value) {
    return null
  }

  const result = value.trim()

  return result ? result : null
}


############################################################
FILE: src/lib/kk-parser/parse-kk.ts
############################################################

import type { KKData } from './types'

import { parseHeader } from './parse-header'

import { parseMembers } from './parse-members'

export function parseKK(rawText: string): KKData {
  const header = parseHeader(rawText)

  const anggota = parseMembers(rawText)

  return {
    ...header,
    anggota,
  }
}


############################################################
FILE: src/lib/kk-parser/parse-members.ts
############################################################

import type { KKMember } from './types'

const RELIGIONS = new Set([
  'ISLAM',
  'KRISTEN',
  'KRISTEN PROTESTAN',
  'PROTESTAN',
  'KATOLIK',
  'KATHOLIK',
  'HINDU',
  'BUDDHA',
  'BUDHA',
  'KONGHUCU',
  'KEPERCAYAAN',
])

const BLOOD_TYPES = new Set([
  'A',
  'B',
  'AB',
  'O',
  'A+',
  'A-',
  'B+',
  'B-',
  'AB+',
  'AB-',
  'O+',
  'O-',
  'TIDAK TAHU',
  'TIDAK DIKETAHUI',
])

const EDUCATION_PATTERNS = [
  'TIDAK/BELUM SEKOLAH',
  'BELUM TAMAT SD/SEDERAJAT',
  'TAMAT SD/SEDERAJAT',
  'SD/SEDERAJAT',

  'SLTP/SEDERAJAT',
  'SMP/SEDERAJAT',

  'SLTA/SEDERAJAT',
  'SMA/SEDERAJAT',
  'SMK/SEDERAJAT',

  'DIPLOMA I',
  'DIPLOMA II',
  'DIPLOMA III',
  'DIPLOMA IV',

  'AKADEMI',

  'STRATA I',
  'STRATA II',
  'STRATA III',

  'S1',
  'S2',
  'S3',
]

const OCCUPATIONS = new Set([
  'BELUM/TIDAK BEKERJA',
  'MENGURUS RUMAH TANGGA',

  'PELAJAR/MAHASISWA',
  'PELAJAR',
  'MAHASISWA',

  'PENSIUNAN',

  'PEGAWAI NEGERI SIPIL',
  'PNS',

  'TNI',
  'POLRI',

  'PETANI',
  'PETANI/PEKEBUN',
  'PEKEBUN',

  'PETERNAK',

  'NELAYAN',
  'NELAYAN/PERIKANAN',

  'KARYAWAN SWASTA',
  'KARYAWAN BUMN',
  'KARYAWAN BUMD',
  'KARYAWAN HONORER',

  'BURUH HARIAN LEPAS',

  'PEDAGANG',

  'GURU',
  'DOSEN',

  'DOKTER',
  'PERAWAT',
  'BIDAN',

  'SOPIR',

  'PERANGKAT DESA',

  'KEPALA DESA',

  'WIRASWASTA',

  'LAINNYA',
])

export function parseMembers(rawText: string): KKMember[] {
  const lines = normalizeLines(rawText)

  const numbered = parseNumberedMembers(lines)
  const nikAnchored = parseNikAnchoredMembers(lines)

  if (numbered.length === 0) {
    return nikAnchored
  }

  if (nikAnchored.length > numbered.length) {
    return nikAnchored
  }

  return numbered
}

/* ======================================================
 * NUMBERED MEMBER MODE
 * ====================================================== */

function parseNumberedMembers(lines: string[]): KKMember[] {
  const starts: number[] = []

  for (let index = 0; index < lines.length; index += 1) {
    if (!isRowNumber(lines[index])) {
      continue
    }

    /*
     * Dalam beberapa baris berikutnya
     * harus ada NIK.
     */
    const nearby = lines.slice(index + 1, index + 7)

    if (nearby.some(isIdentityNumber)) {
      starts.push(index)
    }
  }

  if (starts.length === 0) {
    return []
  }

  const members: KKMember[] = []

  for (let index = 0; index < starts.length; index += 1) {
    const start = starts[index]

    const end = starts[index + 1] ?? lines.length

    const block = lines.slice(start + 1, end)

    const member = parseMemberBlock(block, members.length + 1)

    if (member) {
      members.push(member)
    }
  }

  return members
}

function parseMemberBlock(block: string[], no: number): KKMember | null {
  if (block.length === 0) {
    return null
  }

  const nikIndex = block.findIndex(isIdentityNumber)

  if (nikIndex < 0) {
    return null
  }

  const nik = cleanDigits(block[nikIndex])

  const genderIndex = block.findIndex(
    (value) => normalizeGender(value) !== null,
  )

  const gender = genderIndex >= 0 ? normalizeGender(block[genderIndex]) : null

  const dateIndex = block.findIndex((value) => normalizeDate(value) !== null)

  const tanggalLahir = dateIndex >= 0 ? normalizeDate(block[dateIndex]) : null

  const religionIndex = block.findIndex(
    (value) => normalizeReligion(value) !== null,
  )

  const agama =
    religionIndex >= 0 ? normalizeReligion(block[religionIndex]) : null

  const educationIndex = block.findIndex(
    (value) => normalizeEducation(value) !== null,
  )

  const pendidikan =
    educationIndex >= 0 ? normalizeEducation(block[educationIndex]) : null

  const occupationIndex = block.findIndex(
    (value) => normalizeOccupation(value) !== null,
  )

  const pekerjaan =
    occupationIndex >= 0 ? normalizeOccupation(block[occupationIndex]) : null

  const bloodIndex = block.findIndex(
    (value) => normalizeBloodType(value) !== null,
  )

  const golonganDarah =
    bloodIndex >= 0 ? normalizeBloodType(block[bloodIndex]) : null

  /*
   * Nama tidak boleh bergantung
   * apakah NIK berada sebelum/atau
   * sesudah jenis kelamin.
   *
   * Contoh anggota 3:
   *
   * TES3
   * PEREMPUAN
   * 331220...
   */
  const namaIndex = findNameIndex(block, {
    nikIndex,
    genderIndex,
    dateIndex,
    religionIndex,
    educationIndex,
    occupationIndex,
    bloodIndex,
  })

  const nama = namaIndex >= 0 ? cleanText(block[namaIndex]) : ''

  /*
   * Tempat lahir adalah field text
   * bebas yang tersisa setelah
   * field semantic dibuang.
   */
  const tempatLahir = findBirthPlace(
    block,
    new Set([
      nikIndex,
      genderIndex,
      dateIndex,
      religionIndex,
      educationIndex,
      occupationIndex,
      bloodIndex,
      namaIndex,
    ]),
  )

  return {
    no,

    nama,

    nik,

    jenisKelamin: gender,

    tempatLahir,

    tanggalLahir,

    agama,

    pendidikan,

    pekerjaan,

    golonganDarah,

    ayah: null,
    ibu: null,
    baptisAnak: null,
    sidiBaptisDewasa: null,
    menikah: null,
    domisili: null,
  }
}

/* ======================================================
 * NAME
 * ====================================================== */

function findNameIndex(
  block: string[],
  used: {
    nikIndex: number
    genderIndex: number
    dateIndex: number
    religionIndex: number
    educationIndex: number
    occupationIndex: number
    bloodIndex: number
  },
) {
  const excluded = new Set([
    used.nikIndex,
    used.genderIndex,
    used.dateIndex,
    used.religionIndex,
    used.educationIndex,
    used.occupationIndex,
    used.bloodIndex,
  ])

  /*
   * Nama biasanya field text
   * pertama setelah nomor anggota.
   */
  for (let index = 0; index < block.length; index += 1) {
    if (excluded.has(index)) {
      continue
    }

    const value = cleanText(block[index])

    if (!isFreeText(value)) {
      continue
    }

    return index
  }

  return -1
}

/* ======================================================
 * BIRTH PLACE
 * ====================================================== */

function findBirthPlace(block: string[], excluded: Set<number>): string | null {
  for (let index = 0; index < block.length; index += 1) {
    if (excluded.has(index)) {
      continue
    }

    const value = cleanText(block[index])

    if (!isFreeText(value)) {
      continue
    }

    /*
     * Remaining free text pertama
     * setelah nama dianggap
     * tempat lahir.
     */
    return value
  }

  return null
}

/* ======================================================
 * FALLBACK: NIK ANCHOR MODE
 * ====================================================== */

function parseNikAnchoredMembers(lines: string[]): KKMember[] {
  const nikIndexes: number[] = []

  for (let index = 0; index < lines.length; index += 1) {
    if (isIdentityNumber(lines[index])) {
      /*
       * Jangan masukkan No KK.
       *
       * No KK biasanya berada
       * sebelum area anggota.
       */
      if (looksLikeNoKKContext(lines, index)) {
        continue
      }

      nikIndexes.push(index)
    }
  }

  if (nikIndexes.length === 0) {
    return []
  }

  return nikIndexes.map((nikIndex, memberIndex) => {
    const previousNik = nikIndexes[memberIndex - 1] ?? -1

    const nextNik = nikIndexes[memberIndex + 1] ?? lines.length

    const start = Math.max(previousNik + 1, nikIndex - 4)

    const end = Math.min(nextNik, nikIndex + 12)

    const block = lines.slice(start, end)

    /*
     * Tambahkan nomor dummy
     * lalu gunakan parser block.
     */
    const parsed = parseMemberBlock(block, memberIndex + 1)

    if (parsed) {
      return parsed
    }

    return {
      no: memberIndex + 1,

      nama: '',

      nik: cleanDigits(lines[nikIndex]),

      jenisKelamin: null,

      tempatLahir: null,

      tanggalLahir: null,

      agama: null,

      pendidikan: null,

      pekerjaan: null,

      golonganDarah: null,

      ayah: null,
      ibu: null,
      baptisAnak: null,
      sidiBaptisDewasa: null,
      menikah: null,
      domisili: null,
    }
  })
}

/* ======================================================
 * GENDER
 * ====================================================== */

function normalizeGender(value: string): 'LAKI-LAKI' | 'PEREMPUAN' | null {
  const normalized = normalizeSemantic(value)

  if (normalized === 'PEREMPUAN') {
    return 'PEREMPUAN'
  }

  if (normalized === 'LAKI-LAKI' || normalized === 'LAKI LAKI') {
    return 'LAKI-LAKI'
  }

  return null
}

/* ======================================================
 * DATE
 * ====================================================== */

function normalizeDate(value: string): string | null {
  const normalized = value.trim().replace(/[./]/g, '-').replace(/\s+/g, '')

  const match = normalized.match(/^(\d{1,2})-(\d{1,2})-(\d{4})$/)

  if (!match) {
    return null
  }

  const day = Number(match[1])

  const month = Number(match[2])

  const year = Number(match[3])

  if (
    day < 1 ||
    day > 31 ||
    month < 1 ||
    month > 12 ||
    year < 1900 ||
    year > 2100
  ) {
    return null
  }

  return [
    String(day).padStart(2, '0'),

    String(month).padStart(2, '0'),

    String(year),
  ].join('-')
}

/* ======================================================
 * RELIGION
 * ====================================================== */

function normalizeReligion(value: string): string | null {
  const normalized = normalizeSemantic(value)

  if (!RELIGIONS.has(normalized)) {
    return null
  }

  if (normalized === 'KATHOLIK') {
    return 'KATOLIK'
  }

  if (normalized === 'BUDHA') {
    return 'BUDDHA'
  }

  return normalized
}

/* ======================================================
 * EDUCATION
 * ====================================================== */

function normalizeEducation(value: string): string | null {
  const normalized = normalizeSemantic(value)

  for (const education of EDUCATION_PATTERNS) {
    if (normalized === education) {
      return normalized
    }
  }

  return null
}

/* ======================================================
 * OCCUPATION
 * ====================================================== */

function normalizeOccupation(value: string): string | null {
  const normalized = normalizeSemantic(value)

  if (OCCUPATIONS.has(normalized)) {
    return normalized
  }

  return null
}

/* ======================================================
 * BLOOD TYPE
 * ====================================================== */

function normalizeBloodType(value: string): string | null {
  const normalized = normalizeSemantic(value)

  if (BLOOD_TYPES.has(normalized)) {
    return normalized
  }

  return null
}

/* ======================================================
 * FREE TEXT
 * ====================================================== */

function isFreeText(value: string) {
  if (!value) {
    return false
  }

  if (
    isIdentityNumber(value) ||
    isRowNumber(value) ||
    normalizeGender(value) ||
    normalizeDate(value) ||
    normalizeReligion(value) ||
    normalizeEducation(value) ||
    normalizeOccupation(value) ||
    normalizeBloodType(value)
  ) {
    return false
  }

  if (isKnownHeaderOrTableLabel(value)) {
    return false
  }

  return /[A-Za-z]/.test(value)
}

/* ======================================================
 * LABELS / JUNK
 * ====================================================== */

function isKnownHeaderOrTableLabel(value: string) {
  const normalized = normalizeSemantic(value)

  const labels = [
    'KARTU KELUARGA',
    'REPUBLIK INDONESIA',

    'NAMA KEPALA KELUARGA',
    'NAMA KEPAIA KELUARGA',

    'ALAMAT',
    'RT/RW',

    'DESA/KELURAHAN',
    'KECAMATAN',
    'KODE POS',

    'KABUPATEN/KOTA',
    'PROVINSI',

    'NO',
    'NAMA LENGKAP',
    'NIK',

    'JENIS KELAMIN',
    'TEMPAT LAHIR',
    'TANGGAL LAHIR',

    'AGAMA',
    'PENDIDIKAN',

    'JENIS PEKERJAAN',

    'GOLONGAN DARAH',
  ]

  return labels.includes(normalized)
}

/* ======================================================
 * NIK
 * ====================================================== */

function isIdentityNumber(value: string) {
  const trimmed = value.trim()

  /*
   * Development:
   * dummy NIK masih boleh
   * 10 sampai 20 digit.
   */
  return /^\d{10,20}$/.test(trimmed)
}

function looksLikeNoKKContext(lines: string[], index: number) {
  const previous = normalizeSemantic(lines[index - 1] ?? '')

  const twoBefore = normalizeSemantic(lines[index - 2] ?? '')

  return (
    previous === 'NO' ||
    previous === 'KARTU KELUARGA' ||
    twoBefore === 'KARTU KELUARGA'
  )
}

function cleanDigits(value: string) {
  return value.replace(/\D/g, '')
}

/* ======================================================
 * GENERAL
 * ====================================================== */

function normalizeLines(value: string) {
  return value.replace(/\r/g, '').split('\n').map(cleanText).filter(Boolean)
}

function cleanText(value: string) {
  return value
    .replace(/^[\s:;|]+/, '')
    .replace(/\s+/g, ' ')
    .trim()
}

function normalizeSemantic(value: string) {
  return cleanText(value)
    .toUpperCase()
    .replace(/–|—/g, '-')
    .replace(/\s*\/\s*/g, '/')
    .replace(/\s*-\s*/g, '-')
    .replace(/\s+/g, ' ')
    .trim()
}

function isRowNumber(value: string) {
  return /^[1-9]\d?$/.test(value.trim())
}
