import { z } from 'zod'

const nullableText = z.string().trim().nullable()

export const kkMemberSchema = z.object({
  id: z.number().int().positive().optional(),
  no: z.number().int().positive(),

  nama: z.string().trim().min(1, 'Nama tidak boleh kosong'),

  nik: z
    .string()
    .trim()
    .regex(/^\d{16}$/, 'NIK harus 16 digit'),

  jenisKelamin: z.enum(['LAKI-LAKI', 'PEREMPUAN']).nullable(),

  tempatLahir: nullableText,

  tanggalLahir: z
    .string()
    .regex(/^\d{2}-\d{2}-\d{4}$/, 'Tanggal lahir harus DD-MM-YYYY')
    .nullable(),

  agama: nullableText,
  pendidikan: nullableText,
  pekerjaan: nullableText,
  golonganDarah: nullableText,
  ayah: nullableText,
  ibu: nullableText,
  baptisAnak: nullableText,
  sidiBaptisDewasa: nullableText,
  menikah: nullableText,
  domisili: nullableText,
})

export const kkSchema = z.object({
  noKK: z
    .string()
    .trim()
    .regex(/^\d{16}$/, 'Nomor KK harus 16 digit'),

  kepalaKeluarga: z
    .string()
    .trim()
    .min(1, 'Kepala keluarga tidak boleh kosong'),

  alamat: nullableText,

  rt: z
    .string()
    .regex(/^\d{1,3}$/)
    .nullable(),

  rw: z
    .string()
    .regex(/^\d{1,3}$/)
    .nullable(),

  desaKelurahan: nullableText,
  kecamatan: nullableText,
  kabupatenKota: nullableText,

  kodePos: z
    .string()
    .regex(/^\d{5}$/, 'Kode pos harus 5 digit')
    .nullable(),

  provinsi: nullableText,

  anggota: z.array(kkMemberSchema).min(1, 'Tidak ada anggota keluarga'),
})

export type ValidatedKK = z.infer<typeof kkSchema>

  alamat: string | null

  rt: string | null
  rw: string | null

  desaKelurahan: string | null

############################################################
FILE: src/lib/list-search.ts
############################################################

export type PageSize = 10 | 20 | 50 | 100

export type SortOrder = 'asc' | 'desc'

export type ListSearch<TSearchBy extends string, TSortBy extends string> = {
  page?: number
  limit?: PageSize

  q?: string
  by?: TSearchBy

  sort?: TSortBy
  order?: SortOrder
}

type Options<TSearchBy extends string, TSortBy extends string> = {
  searchBy: readonly TSearchBy[]
  sortBy: readonly TSortBy[]
}

const allowedPageSizes = new Set<number>([10, 20, 50, 100])

export function createListSearchValidator<
  TSearchBy extends string,
  TSortBy extends string,
>({ searchBy, sortBy }: Options<TSearchBy, TSortBy>) {
  const allowedSearchBy = new Set<string>(searchBy)

  const allowedSortBy = new Set<string>(sortBy)

  return (search: Record<string, unknown>): ListSearch<TSearchBy, TSortBy> => {
    const result: ListSearch<TSearchBy, TSortBy> = {}

    const page = Number(search.page)

    if (Number.isInteger(page) && page > 1) {
      result.page = page
    }

    const limit = Number(search.limit)

    if (allowedPageSizes.has(limit)) {
      result.limit = limit as PageSize
    }

    if (typeof search.q === 'string') {
      const q = search.q.trim()

      if (q) {
        result.q = q
      }
    }

    if (typeof search.by === 'string' && allowedSearchBy.has(search.by)) {
      result.by = search.by as TSearchBy
    }

    if (typeof search.sort === 'string' && allowedSortBy.has(search.sort)) {
      result.sort = search.sort as TSortBy
    }

    if (search.order === 'asc' || search.order === 'desc') {
      result.order = search.order
    }

    return result
  }
}

  DataKkIdRoute: typeof DataKkIdRoute
  EventKesehatanIdRoute: typeof EventKesehatanIdRoute
  FiturImportExcelRoute: typeof FiturImportExcelRoute
  FiturParserKkRoute: typeof FiturParserKkRoute
}

declare module '@tanstack/react-router' {
  interface FileRoutesByPath {
    '/': {
      id: '/'
      path: '/'
      fullPath: '/'
      preLoaderRoute: typeof IndexRouteImport
      parentRoute: typeof rootRouteImport
    }
    '/akun': {
      id: '/akun'
      path: '/akun'
      fullPath: '/akun'
      preLoaderRoute: typeof AkunRouteImport
      parentRoute: typeof rootRouteImport
    }
    '/data-anggota': {
      id: '/data-anggota'
      path: '/data-anggota'
      fullPath: '/data-anggota'
      preLoaderRoute: typeof DataAnggotaRouteImport
      parentRoute: typeof rootRouteImport
    }
    '/data-kk': {
      id: '/data-kk'
      path: '/data-kk'
      fullPath: '/data-kk'
      preLoaderRoute: typeof DataKkRouteImport
      parentRoute: typeof rootRouteImport
    }
    '/event-kesehatan': {
      id: '/event-kesehatan'
      path: '/event-kesehatan'
      fullPath: '/event-kesehatan'
      preLoaderRoute: typeof EventKesehatanRouteImport
      parentRoute: typeof rootRouteImport
    }
    '/login': {
      id: '/login'
      path: '/login'
      fullPath: '/login'
      preLoaderRoute: typeof LoginRouteImport
      parentRoute: typeof rootRouteImport
    }
    '/setup-admin': {
      id: '/setup-admin'
      path: '/setup-admin'
      fullPath: '/setup-admin'
      preLoaderRoute: typeof SetupAdminRouteImport
      parentRoute: typeof rootRouteImport
    }
    '/admin/users': {
      id: '/admin/users'
      path: '/admin/users'
      fullPath: '/admin/users'
      preLoaderRoute: typeof AdminUsersRouteImport
      parentRoute: typeof rootRouteImport
    }
    '/data-anggota_/$id': {
      id: '/data-anggota_/$id'
      path: '/data-anggota/$id'
      fullPath: '/data-anggota/$id'
      preLoaderRoute: typeof DataAnggotaIdRouteImport
      parentRoute: typeof rootRouteImport
    }
    '/data-kk_/$id': {
      id: '/data-kk_/$id'
      path: '/data-kk/$id'
      fullPath: '/data-kk/$id'
      preLoaderRoute: typeof DataKkIdRouteImport
      parentRoute: typeof rootRouteImport
    }
    '/event-kesehatan_/$id': {
      id: '/event-kesehatan_/$id'
      path: '/event-kesehatan/$id'
      fullPath: '/event-kesehatan/$id'
      preLoaderRoute: typeof EventKesehatanIdRouteImport
      parentRoute: typeof rootRouteImport
    }
    '/fitur/import-excel': {
      id: '/fitur/import-excel'
      path: '/fitur/import-excel'
      fullPath: '/fitur/import-excel'
      preLoaderRoute: typeof FiturImportExcelRouteImport
      parentRoute: typeof rootRouteImport
    }
    '/fitur/parser-kk': {
      id: '/fitur/parser-kk'
      path: '/fitur/parser-kk'
      fullPath: '/fitur/parser-kk'
      preLoaderRoute: typeof FiturParserKkRouteImport
      parentRoute: typeof rootRouteImport
    }
  }
}

const rootRouteChildren: RootRouteChildren = {
  IndexRoute: IndexRoute,
  AkunRoute: AkunRoute,
  DataAnggotaRoute: DataAnggotaRoute,
  DataKkRoute: DataKkRoute,
  EventKesehatanRoute: EventKesehatanRoute,
  LoginRoute: LoginRoute,
  SetupAdminRoute: SetupAdminRoute,
  AdminUsersRoute: AdminUsersRoute,
  DataAnggotaIdRoute: DataAnggotaIdRoute,
  DataKkIdRoute: DataKkIdRoute,
  EventKesehatanIdRoute: EventKesehatanIdRoute,
  FiturImportExcelRoute: FiturImportExcelRoute,
  FiturParserKkRoute: FiturParserKkRoute,
}
export const routeTree = rootRouteImport
  ._addFileChildren(rootRouteChildren)
  ._addFileTypes<FileRouteTypes>()

import type { getRouter } from './router.tsx'
import type { createStart } from '@tanstack/react-start'
declare module '@tanstack/react-start' {
  interface Register {
    ssr: true
    router: Awaited<ReturnType<typeof getRouter>>
  }
}

import { createRouter as createTanStackRouter } from '@tanstack/react-router'
import { routeTree } from './routeTree.gen'

export function getRouter() {
  const router = createTanStackRouter({
    routeTree,
    scrollRestoration: true,
    defaultPreload: 'intent',
    defaultPreloadStaleTime: 0,
  })

  return router
}

declare module '@tanstack/react-router' {
  interface Register {
    router: ReturnType<typeof getRouter>
  }
}


############################################################
FILE: src/routes/__root.tsx

import {
  HeadContent,
  Scripts,
  createRootRoute,
  redirect,
} from '@tanstack/react-router'

import { TanStackRouterDevtoolsPanel } from '@tanstack/react-router-devtools'
import { TanStackDevtools } from '@tanstack/react-devtools'

import { SiteFooter } from '@/components/layout/site-footer'
import { SiteNavbar } from '@/components/layout/site-navbar'
import { Toaster } from '@/components/ui/sonner'

import appCss from '../styles.css?url'
import { getCurrentUser } from '#/server/auth/actions.functions'

export const Route = createRootRoute({
  beforeLoad: async ({ location }) => {
    const user = await getCurrentUser()

    const isPublicRoute =
      location.pathname === '/login' || location.pathname === '/setup-admin'

    if (isPublicRoute) {
      return {
        user,
      }
    }

    if (!user) {
      throw redirect({
        to: '/login',
      })
    }

    if (user.role === 'PETUGAS') {
      const isHealthRoute =
        location.pathname === '/event-kesehatan' ||
        location.pathname.startsWith('/event-kesehatan/')

      const isAccountRoute = location.pathname === '/akun'

      if (!isHealthRoute && !isAccountRoute) {
        throw redirect({
          to: '/event-kesehatan',
        })
      }
    }

    return {
      user,
    }
  },

  head: () => ({
    meta: [
      {
        charSet: 'utf-8',
      },
      {
        name: 'viewport',
        content: 'width=device-width, initial-scale=1, viewport-fit=cover',
      },
      {
        title: 'Sistem Data Jemaat | GKJ Slogohimo',
      },
      {
        name: 'description',
        content:
          'Sistem pengelolaan data jemaat GKJ Slogohimo untuk data keluarga, anggota, dan pelayanan kesehatan.',
      },
      {
        name: 'application-name',
        content: 'GKJ Slogohimo',
      },
      {
        name: 'theme-color',
        content: '#2563eb',
      },
      {
        name: 'apple-mobile-web-app-capable',
        content: 'yes',
      },
      {
        name: 'apple-mobile-web-app-status-bar-style',
        content: 'default',
      },
      {
        name: 'apple-mobile-web-app-title',
        content: 'GKJ Slogohimo',
      },
      {
        name: 'mobile-web-app-capable',
        content: 'yes',
      },
      {
        property: 'og:title',
        content: 'Sistem Data Jemaat | GKJ Slogohimo',
      },
      {
        property: 'og:description',
        content:
          'Sistem pengelolaan data jemaat GKJ Slogohimo untuk data keluarga, anggota, dan pelayanan kesehatan.',
      },
      {
        property: 'og:type',
        content: 'website',
      },
      {
        property: 'og:site_name',
        content: 'GKJ Slogohimo',
      },
      {
        property: 'og:image',
        content: '/icons/pwa-512x512.png',
      },
    ],

    links: [
      {
        rel: 'stylesheet',
        href: appCss,
      },
      {
        rel: 'manifest',
        href: '/manifest.webmanifest',
      },
      {
        rel: 'icon',
        type: 'image/png',
        sizes: '16x16',
        href: '/icons/favicon-16x16.png',
      },
      {
        rel: 'icon',
        type: 'image/png',
        sizes: '32x32',
        href: '/icons/favicon-32x32.png',
      },
      {
        rel: 'icon',
        type: 'image/png',
        sizes: '48x48',
        href: '/icons/favicon-48x48.png',
      },
      {
        rel: 'apple-touch-icon',
        sizes: '180x180',
        href: '/icons/apple-touch-icon.png',
      },
    ],
  }),

  shellComponent: RootDocument,
})

function RootDocument({ children }: { children: React.ReactNode }) {
  return (
    <html lang="id">
      <head>
        <HeadContent />
      </head>

      <body>
        <div className="flex min-h-dvh flex-col bg-blue-50/30">
          <SiteNavbar />

          <div className="flex-1">{children}</div>

          <SiteFooter />
        </div>

        <Toaster richColors position="top-right" />

        <TanStackDevtools
          config={{
            position: 'bottom-right',
          }}
          plugins={[
            {
              name: 'Tanstack Router',
              render: <TanStackRouterDevtoolsPanel />,
            },
          ]}
        />

        <Scripts />
      </body>
    </html>
  )
}


############################################################
FILE: src/routes/admin.users.tsx
############################################################

import {
  createFileRoute,
  getRouteApi,
  redirect,
  useRouter,
} from '@tanstack/react-router'
import { KeyRound, ShieldCheck, UserPlus, UsersRound } from 'lucide-react'
import { useState } from 'react'

import type { FormEvent } from 'react'

import { Button } from '@/components/ui/button'
import { Input } from '@/components/ui/input'
import { Label } from '@/components/ui/label'

import {
  createUser,
  listUsers,
  resetUserPassword,
  setUserActive,
} from '@/server/users/users.functions'
import {
  Dialog,
  DialogContent,
  DialogDescription,
  DialogFooter,
  DialogHeader,
  DialogTitle,
} from '#/components/ui/dialog'

const rootRoute = getRouteApi('__root__')

export const Route = createFileRoute('/admin/users')({
  beforeLoad: ({ context }) => {
    if (context.user?.role !== 'ADMIN') {
      throw redirect({
        to: '/',
      })
    }
  },

  loader: () => listUsers(),

  component: UsersPage,
})

function UsersPage() {
  const { user: currentUser } = rootRoute.useRouteContext()
  const users = Route.useLoaderData()
  const router = useRouter()

  const [nama, setNama] = useState('')
  const [username, setUsername] = useState('')
  const [password, setPassword] = useState('')
  const [role, setRole] = useState<'ADMIN' | 'PETUGAS'>('PETUGAS')

  const [resetTarget, setResetTarget] = useState<{
    id: number
    nama: string
  } | null>(null)

  const [resetPassword, setResetPassword] = useState('')
  const [resetPasswordConfirm, setResetPasswordConfirm] = useState('')

  const [resetError, setResetError] = useState<string | null>(null)

  const [isResetting, setIsResetting] = useState(false)

  const [message, setMessage] = useState<string | null>(null)
  const [errorMessage, setErrorMessage] = useState<string | null>(null)

  const [isSubmitting, setIsSubmitting] = useState(false)
  const [processingUserId, setProcessingUserId] = useState<number | null>(null)

  async function handleCreateUser(event: FormEvent<HTMLFormElement>) {
    event.preventDefault()

    if (isSubmitting) {
      return
    }

    setMessage(null)
    setErrorMessage(null)
    setIsSubmitting(true)

    try {
      await createUser({
        data: {
          nama,
          username,
          password,
          role,
        },
      })

      setNama('')
      setUsername('')
      setPassword('')
      setRole('PETUGAS')

      setMessage('User berhasil dibuat.')

      await router.invalidate()
    } catch (error) {
      setErrorMessage(
        error instanceof Error ? error.message : 'User tidak dapat dibuat.',
      )
    } finally {
      setIsSubmitting(false)
    }
  }

  async function handleResetPassword(event: FormEvent<HTMLFormElement>) {
    event.preventDefault()

    if (!resetTarget || isResetting) {
      return
    }

    setResetError(null)

    if (resetPassword !== resetPasswordConfirm) {
      setResetError('Konfirmasi password tidak sama.')
      return
    }

    setIsResetting(true)

    try {
      await resetUserPassword({
        data: {
          id: resetTarget.id,
          newPassword: resetPassword,
        },
      })

      setMessage(`Password ${resetTarget.nama} berhasil direset.`)

      setResetTarget(null)
      setResetPassword('')
      setResetPasswordConfirm('')
    } catch (error) {
      setResetError(
        error instanceof Error
          ? error.message
          : 'Password tidak dapat direset.',
      )
    } finally {
      setIsResetting(false)
    }
  }

  async function handleToggleUser(id: number, isActive: boolean) {
    if (processingUserId !== null) {
      return
    }

    setMessage(null)
    setErrorMessage(null)
    setProcessingUserId(id)

    try {
      await setUserActive({
        data: {
          id,
          isActive: !isActive,
        },
      })

      await router.invalidate()
    } catch (error) {
      setErrorMessage(
        error instanceof Error
          ? error.message
          : 'Status user tidak dapat diubah.',
      )
    } finally {
      setProcessingUserId(null)
    }
  }

  return (
    <main className="mx-auto w-full max-w-7xl px-4 py-8 sm:px-6 lg:px-8">
      <section className="mb-6 rounded-2xl border border-slate-200 bg-white p-5 shadow-sm sm:p-6">
        <div className="flex items-start gap-4">
          <div className="flex size-12 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-xl bg-blue-50 text-blue-600">
            <UsersRound className="size-5" />
          </div>

          <div>
            <div className="text-xs font-bold uppercase tracking-[0.16em] text-blue-600">
              Administrator
            </div>

            <h1 className="mt-2 text-2xl font-bold tracking-tight text-slate-900">
              Kelola User
            </h1>

            <p className="mt-2 text-sm leading-6 text-slate-500">
              Tambah dan atur akses pengguna internal Sistem Data Jemaat GKJ
              Slogohimo.
            </p>
          </div>
        </div>
      </section>

      <div className="grid gap-6 lg:grid-cols-[360px_1fr]">
        <section className="rounded-2xl border border-slate-200 bg-white p-5 shadow-sm">
          <div className="mb-5 flex items-center gap-3">
            <UserPlus className="size-5 text-blue-600" />

            <h2 className="font-semibold text-slate-900">Tambah User</h2>
          </div>

          <form className="space-y-4" onSubmit={handleCreateUser}>
            <div className="space-y-2">
              <Label htmlFor="nama">Nama</Label>

              <Input
                id="nama"
                value={nama}
                onChange={(event) => setNama(event.target.value)}
                disabled={isSubmitting}
                required
              />
            </div>

            <div className="space-y-2">
              <Label htmlFor="username">Username</Label>

              <Input
                id="username"
                autoComplete="off"
                value={username}
                onChange={(event) => setUsername(event.target.value)}
                disabled={isSubmitting}
                required
              />
            </div>

            <div className="space-y-2">
              <Label htmlFor="password">Password Awal</Label>

              <Input
                id="password"
                type="password"
                autoComplete="new-password"
                value={password}
                onChange={(event) => setPassword(event.target.value)}
                disabled={isSubmitting}
                required
              />

              <p className="text-xs text-slate-400">Minimal 8 karakter.</p>
            </div>

            <div className="space-y-2">
              <Label htmlFor="role">Role</Label>

              <select
                id="role"
                value={role}
                onChange={(event) =>
                  setRole(event.target.value as 'ADMIN' | 'PETUGAS')
                }
                disabled={isSubmitting}
                className="flex h-10 w-full rounded-xl border border-slate-200 bg-white px-3 text-sm text-slate-700 outline-none transition focus:border-blue-400 focus:ring-2 focus:ring-blue-100"
              >
                <option value="PETUGAS">Petugas</option>
                <option value="ADMIN">Administrator</option>
              </select>
            </div>

            <Button type="submit" className="w-full" disabled={isSubmitting}>
              <UserPlus className="size-4" />

              {isSubmitting ? 'Menyimpan...' : 'Tambah User'}
            </Button>
          </form>
        </section>

        <section className="rounded-2xl border border-slate-200 bg-white p-5 shadow-sm">
          <div className="mb-5 flex items-center gap-3">
            <ShieldCheck className="size-5 text-blue-600" />

            <h2 className="font-semibold text-slate-900">User Terdaftar</h2>
          </div>

          {message ? (
            <div className="mb-4 rounded-xl border border-green-200 bg-green-50 px-4 py-3 text-sm text-green-700">
              {message}
            </div>
          ) : null}

          {errorMessage ? (
            <div className="mb-4 rounded-xl border border-red-200 bg-red-50 px-4 py-3 text-sm text-red-700">
              {errorMessage}
            </div>
          ) : null}

          <div className="space-y-3">
            {users.map((user) => (
              <div
                key={user.id}
                className="flex flex-col gap-4 rounded-2xl border border-slate-200 p-4 sm:flex-row sm:items-center sm:justify-between"
              >
                <div className="min-w-0">
                  <div className="font-semibold text-slate-900">
                    {user.nama}
                  </div>

                  <div className="mt-1 text-sm text-slate-500">
                    @{user.username}
                  </div>

                  <div className="mt-2 flex flex-wrap gap-2">
                    <span className="rounded-lg bg-blue-50 px-2 py-1 text-xs font-medium text-blue-700">
                      {user.role === 'ADMIN' ? 'Administrator' : 'Petugas'}
                    </span>

                    <span
                      className={
                        user.isActive
                          ? 'rounded-lg bg-green-50 px-2 py-1 text-xs font-medium text-green-700'
                          : 'rounded-lg bg-slate-100 px-2 py-1 text-xs font-medium text-slate-500'
                      }
                    >
                      {user.isActive ? 'Aktif' : 'Nonaktif'}
                    </span>
                  </div>
                </div>

                <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2">
                  {currentUser?.id !== user.id ? (
                    <Button
                      type="button"
                      variant="outline"
                      onClick={() => {
                        setResetError(null)
                        setResetPassword('')
                        setResetPasswordConfirm('')

                        setResetTarget({
                          id: user.id,
                          nama: user.nama,
                        })
                      }}
                    >
                      <KeyRound className="size-4" />
                      Reset Password
                    </Button>
                  ) : null}

                  <Button
                    type="button"
                    variant={user.isActive ? 'outline' : 'default'}
                    disabled={processingUserId !== null}
                    onClick={() =>
                      void handleToggleUser(user.id, user.isActive)
                    }
                  >
                    {processingUserId === user.id
                      ? 'Memproses...'
                      : user.isActive
                        ? 'Nonaktifkan'
                        : 'Aktifkan'}
                  </Button>
                </div>
              </div>
            ))}
          </div>
        </section>
      </div>

      <Dialog
        open={resetTarget !== null}
        onOpenChange={(open) => {
          if (!open && !isResetting) {
            setResetTarget(null)
            setResetPassword('')
            setResetPasswordConfirm('')
            setResetError(null)
          }
        }}
      >
        <DialogContent className="sm:max-w-md">
          <DialogHeader>
            <DialogTitle>Reset Password</DialogTitle>

            <DialogDescription>
              Buat password baru untuk{' '}
              <span className="font-medium text-slate-700">
                {resetTarget?.nama}
              </span>
              . Semua session user tersebut akan otomatis dikeluarkan.
            </DialogDescription>
          </DialogHeader>

          <form className="space-y-5" onSubmit={handleResetPassword}>
            <div className="space-y-2">
              <Label htmlFor="resetPassword">Password Baru</Label>

              <Input
                id="resetPassword"
                type="password"
                autoComplete="new-password"
                value={resetPassword}
                onChange={(event) => setResetPassword(event.target.value)}
                disabled={isResetting}
                required
                autoFocus
              />

              <p className="text-xs text-slate-400">Minimal 8 karakter.</p>
            </div>

            <div className="space-y-2">
              <Label htmlFor="resetPasswordConfirm">Konfirmasi Password</Label>

              <Input
                id="resetPasswordConfirm"
                type="password"
                autoComplete="new-password"
                value={resetPasswordConfirm}
                onChange={(event) =>
                  setResetPasswordConfirm(event.target.value)
                }
                disabled={isResetting}
                required
              />
            </div>

            {resetError ? (
              <div className="rounded-xl border border-red-200 bg-red-50 px-4 py-3 text-sm text-red-700">
                {resetError}
              </div>
            ) : null}

            <DialogFooter>
              <Button
                type="button"
                variant="outline"
                disabled={isResetting}
                onClick={() => setResetTarget(null)}
              >
                Batal
              </Button>

              <Button type="submit" disabled={isResetting}>
                <KeyRound className="size-4" />

                {isResetting ? 'Mereset...' : 'Reset Password'}
              </Button>
            </DialogFooter>
          </form>
        </DialogContent>
      </Dialog>
    </main>
  )
}


############################################################
FILE: src/routes/akun.tsx
############################################################

import { createFileRoute, useRouter } from '@tanstack/react-router'
import { KeyRound, ShieldCheck } from 'lucide-react'
import { useState } from 'react'

import type { FormEvent } from 'react'

import { Button } from '@/components/ui/button'
import { Input } from '@/components/ui/input'
import { Label } from '@/components/ui/label'

import { changeOwnPassword } from '@/server/users/users.functions'

export const Route = createFileRoute('/akun')({
  component: AccountPage,
})

function AccountPage() {
  const router = useRouter()

  const [currentPassword, setCurrentPassword] = useState('')
  const [newPassword, setNewPassword] = useState('')
  const [confirmPassword, setConfirmPassword] = useState('')

  const [errorMessage, setErrorMessage] = useState<string | null>(null)
  const [isSubmitting, setIsSubmitting] = useState(false)

  async function handleSubmit(event: FormEvent<HTMLFormElement>) {
    event.preventDefault()

    if (isSubmitting) {
      return
    }

    setErrorMessage(null)

    if (newPassword !== confirmPassword) {
      setErrorMessage('Konfirmasi password baru tidak sama.')
      return
    }

    setIsSubmitting(true)

    try {
      await changeOwnPassword({
        data: {
          currentPassword,
          newPassword,
        },
      })

      await router.navigate({
        to: '/login',
        replace: true,
      })
    } catch (error) {
      setErrorMessage(
        error instanceof Error ? error.message : 'Password tidak dapat diubah.',
      )
    } finally {
      setIsSubmitting(false)
    }
  }

  return (
    <main className="mx-auto w-full max-w-7xl px-4 py-8 sm:px-6 lg:px-8">
      <div className="mx-auto max-w-lg">
        <section className="rounded-2xl border border-slate-200 bg-white p-6 shadow-sm sm:p-8">
          <div className="mb-7">
            <div className="mb-5 flex size-12 items-center justify-center rounded-xl bg-blue-50 text-blue-600">
              <KeyRound className="size-5" />
            </div>

            <h1 className="text-2xl font-bold tracking-tight text-slate-900">
              Ganti Password
            </h1>

            <p className="mt-2 text-sm leading-6 text-slate-500">
              Ubah password akun anda. Setelah password berhasil diubah, anda
              perlu login kembali.
            </p>
          </div>

          <form className="space-y-5" onSubmit={handleSubmit}>
            <div className="space-y-2">
              <Label htmlFor="currentPassword">Password Saat Ini</Label>

              <Input
                id="currentPassword"
                type="password"
                autoComplete="current-password"
                value={currentPassword}
                onChange={(event) => setCurrentPassword(event.target.value)}
                disabled={isSubmitting}
                required
              />
            </div>

            <div className="space-y-2">
              <Label htmlFor="newPassword">Password Baru</Label>

              <Input
                id="newPassword"
                type="password"
                autoComplete="new-password"
                value={newPassword}
                onChange={(event) => setNewPassword(event.target.value)}
                disabled={isSubmitting}
                required
              />

              <p className="text-xs text-slate-400">Minimal 8 karakter.</p>
            </div>

            <div className="space-y-2">
              <Label htmlFor="confirmPassword">Konfirmasi Password Baru</Label>

              <Input
                id="confirmPassword"
                type="password"
                autoComplete="new-password"
                value={confirmPassword}
                onChange={(event) => setConfirmPassword(event.target.value)}
                disabled={isSubmitting}
                required
              />
            </div>

            {errorMessage ? (
              <div className="rounded-xl border border-red-200 bg-red-50 px-4 py-3 text-sm text-red-700">
                {errorMessage}
              </div>
            ) : null}

            <Button type="submit" className="w-full" disabled={isSubmitting}>
              <ShieldCheck className="size-4" />

              {isSubmitting ? 'Mengubah password...' : 'Ganti Password'}
            </Button>
          </form>
        </section>
      </div>
    </main>
  )
}


############################################################

############################################################

import { createFileRoute, Link } from '@tanstack/react-router'
import {
  ContactRound,
  Database,
  FileJson2,
  Files,
  LayoutList,
  UserRound,
} from 'lucide-react'

import { anggotaColumns } from '@/components/anggota/list/anggota-columns'
import {
  anggotaColumnLabels,
  anggotaSearchOptions,
} from '@/components/anggota/list/config'

import { DataCountBadge } from '@/components/data-list/data-count-badge'
import { DataListCard } from '@/components/data-list/data-list-card'
import { DataListEmpty } from '@/components/data-list/data-list-empty'
import { DataListHero } from '@/components/data-list/data-list-hero'
import { DataListLoading } from '@/components/data-list/data-list-loading'
import { DataListPage } from '@/components/data-list/data-list-page'
import { DataListStats } from '@/components/data-list/data-list-stats'

import { DataTable } from '@/components/data-table/data-table'
import { Button } from '@/components/ui/button'

import { useListController } from '@/hooks/use-list-controller'

import { createListSearchValidator } from '@/lib/list-search'

import type { ListSearch } from '@/lib/list-search'

import { listAnggota } from '@/server/anggota'

const searchBy = ['all', 'nama', 'nik', 'alamat'] as const

const sortBy = ['nama', 'kepalaKeluarga', 'alamat', 'createdAt'] as const

type SearchBy = (typeof searchBy)[number]

type SortBy = (typeof sortBy)[number]

type DataAnggotaSearch = ListSearch<SearchBy, SortBy>

const allowedSorts = new Set<string>(sortBy)

const validateSearch = createListSearchValidator({
  searchBy,
  sortBy,
})

export const Route = createFileRoute('/data-anggota')({
  validateSearch,

  loaderDeps: ({ search }) => ({
    page: search.page ?? 1,

    limit: search.limit ?? 20,

    q: search.q ?? '',

    searchBy: search.by ?? 'all',

    sortBy: search.sort ?? 'nama',

    sortOrder: search.order ?? 'asc',
  }),

  loader: ({ deps }) =>
    listAnggota({
      data: {
        page: deps.page,

        limit: deps.limit,

        q: deps.q,

        searchBy: deps.searchBy,

        sortBy: deps.sortBy,

        sortOrder: deps.sortOrder,
      },
    }),

  pendingComponent: DataAnggotaPending,

  component: DataAnggotaPage,
})

function DataAnggotaPage() {
  const result = Route.useLoaderData()

  const search = Route.useSearch()

  const navigate = Route.useNavigate()

  const list = useListController<DataAnggotaSearch>({
    search,

    defaultSort: 'nama',

    defaultOrder: 'asc',

    allowedSorts,

    navigate,
  })

  const hasData = result.data.length > 0

  const isEmpty = !hasData && !search.q

  return (
    <DataListPage>
      <DataListHero
        icon={<ContactRound className="size-5" />}
        eyebrow="Database Jemaat"
        title="Data Anggota"
        description="Lihat dan kelola seluruh anggota jemaat beserta identitas, keluarga, alamat, dan informasi terkait."
        badge={
          <DataCountBadge>
            {result.pagination.total > 0
              ? `${result.pagination.total.toLocaleString(
                  'id-ID',
                )} anggota tersimpan`
              : 'Belum ada anggota tersimpan'}
          </DataCountBadge>
        }
        actions={
          <>
            <Button
              variant="outline"
              className="h-10 w-full rounded-lg border-slate-200 bg-white px-4 text-slate-700 shadow-sm hover:bg-slate-50 sm:w-auto"
              asChild
            >
              <Link to="/data-kk">
                <Database className="size-4 text-blue-600" />
                Data KK
              </Link>
            </Button>

            <Button
              className="h-10 w-full rounded-lg px-4 shadow-sm sm:w-auto"
              asChild
            >
              <Link to="/fitur/parser-kk">
                <FileJson2 className="size-4" />
                Tambah Data
              </Link>
            </Button>
          </>
        }
      />

      {hasData && (
        <DataListStats
          items={[
            {
              label: 'Total Anggota',

              value: result.pagination.total.toLocaleString('id-ID'),

              icon: <UserRound className="size-4" />,
            },

            {
              label: 'Ditampilkan',

              value: result.data.length.toLocaleString('id-ID'),

              icon: <LayoutList className="size-4" />,
            },

            {
              label: 'Halaman',

              value: `${result.pagination.page} / ${Math.max(
                result.pagination.totalPages,
                1,
              )}`,

              icon: <Files className="size-4" />,
            },
          ]}
        />
      )}

      {isEmpty ? (
        <DataListEmpty
          icon={<ContactRound className="size-7" />}
          title="Belum ada data anggota"
          description="Data anggota akan tersedia setelah Kartu Keluarga ditambahkan ke database."
          action={
            <Button asChild>
              <Link to="/fitur/parser-kk">
                <FileJson2 className="size-4" />
                Tambah Data KK
              </Link>
            </Button>
          }
        />
      ) : (
        <DataListCard
          pagination={result.pagination}
          onPageChange={list.pageChange}
          onPageSizeChange={list.pageSizeChange}
        >
          <DataTable
            columns={anggotaColumns}
            data={result.data}
            searchValue={search.q ?? ''}
            searchMode={search.by ?? 'all'}
            searchOptions={anggotaSearchOptions}
            columnLabels={anggotaColumnLabels}
            sorting={list.sorting}
            onSearchChange={list.searchChange}
            onSearchModeChange={list.searchByChange}
            onSortingChange={list.sortingChange}
          />
        </DataListCard>
      )}
    </DataListPage>
  )
}

function DataAnggotaPending() {
  return (
    <DataListPage>
      <DataListLoading />
    </DataListPage>
  )
}

import { createFileRoute, Link } from '@tanstack/react-router'
import {
  ArrowLeft,
  BriefcaseBusiness,
  CalendarDays,
  Droplets,
  IdCard,
  MapPin,
  UserRound,
  UsersRound,
} from 'lucide-react'

import type { ReactNode } from 'react'

import { RiwayatPemeriksaan } from '@/components/kesehatan/riwayat-pemeriksaan'
import { RingkasanKesehatan } from '@/components/kesehatan/ringkasan-kesehatan'

import { Button } from '@/components/ui/button'
import { Card, CardContent, CardHeader, CardTitle } from '@/components/ui/card'

import { getAnggotaDetail } from '@/server/anggota'

export const Route = createFileRoute('/data-anggota_/$id')({
  loader: async ({ params }) => {
    const id = Number(params.id)

    if (!Number.isInteger(id) || id <= 0) {
      throw new Error('ID anggota tidak valid.')
    }

    return getAnggotaDetail({
      data: {
        id,
      },
    })
  },

  component: DataAnggotaDetailPage,
})

function DataAnggotaDetailPage() {
  const anggota = Route.useLoaderData()

  const lokasi = [anggota.desaKelurahan, anggota.kecamatan]
    .filter(Boolean)
    .join(', ')

  return (
    <main className="bg-slate-50/70">
      <div className="mx-auto w-full max-w-7xl px-4 py-7 sm:px-6 sm:py-8 lg:px-8 lg:py-10">
        {/* BACK */}
        <div className="mb-4">
          <Button
            variant="ghost"
            size="sm"
            className="-ml-2 h-9 rounded-lg px-2 text-slate-500 hover:bg-white hover:text-slate-900"
            asChild
          >
            <Link to="/data-anggota">

              Kembali ke Data Anggota

        </div>

          <div className="flex flex-col gap-5 p-5 sm:p-6 lg:flex-row lg:items-start lg:justify-between">
            <div className="min-w-0 flex-1">
              <div className="flex items-start gap-4">
                <div className="mt-0.5 flex size-12 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-xl bg-blue-50 text-blue-600 ring-1 ring-inset ring-blue-100">
                  <UserRound className="size-5" />
                </div>

                <div className="min-w-0 flex-1">
                  <div className="text-xs font-bold uppercase tracking-[0.16em] text-blue-600">
                    Data Anggota
                  </div>

                  <h1 className="mt-2 text-2xl font-bold tracking-tight text-slate-900 sm:text-[28px]">
                    {anggota.nama}
                  </h1>

                  <div className="mt-3 flex flex-wrap gap-x-5 gap-y-2 text-sm text-slate-500">
                    <span className="flex items-center gap-1.5">
                      <IdCard className="size-4 text-slate-400" />

                        NIK {anggota.nik}

                asChild
              >
                <Link
                  to="/data-kk/$id"
                  params={{
                    id: String(anggota.kkId),
                  }}
                  preload="intent"
                >
                  <UsersRound className="size-4" />
                  Buka Kartu Keluarga

                    Identitas

                    Informasi pribadi anggota
                  </p>
                </div>
              </div>

              <div className="grid gap-x-6 gap-y-5 sm:grid-cols-2">
                <Info

                  value={String(anggota.noUrut)}

              <div className="flex items-start gap-3">
                <div className="flex size-10 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-xl bg-blue-50 text-blue-600">
                  <MapPin className="size-4" />
                </div>

                <div>
                  <CardTitle className="text-base font-semibold text-slate-900">
                    Keluarga & Alamat
                  </CardTitle>

                  <p className="mt-0.5 text-xs leading-5 text-slate-500">
                    Informasi keluarga dan domisili anggota
                  </p>
                </div>
              </div>
            </CardHeader>

            <CardContent className="p-5 sm:p-6">
              <div className="grid gap-x-6 gap-y-5 sm:grid-cols-2">
                <Info
                  label="Kepala Keluarga"
                  value={anggota.kepalaKeluarga}
                  className="sm:col-span-2"
                />

                  value={anggota.alamat}
                  className="sm:col-span-2"
                />

                  value={formatRtRw(anggota.rt, anggota.rw)}
                />

                <Info label="Desa / Kelurahan" value={anggota.desaKelurahan} />

                <Info label="Kecamatan" value={anggota.kecamatan} />

                <Info label="Kabupaten / Kota" value={anggota.kabupatenKota} />

                <Info label="Provinsi" value={anggota.provinsi} />
              </div>

              <div className="flex size-10 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-xl bg-blue-50 text-blue-600">
                <UsersRound className="size-4" />
              </div>

              <div>
                <CardTitle className="text-base font-semibold text-slate-900">
                  Keluarga & Data Gerejawi
                </CardTitle>

                <p className="mt-0.5 text-xs leading-5 text-slate-500">
                  Informasi keluarga, domisili, dan riwayat gerejawi anggota
                </p>
              </div>
            </div>
          </CardHeader>

          <CardContent className="p-5 sm:p-6">
            <div className="grid gap-x-8 gap-y-5 sm:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-3">
              <Info label="Ayah" value={anggota.ayah} />

              <Info label="Ibu" value={anggota.ibu} />

              <Info label="Domisili" value={anggota.domisili} />

              <Info label="Baptis Anak" value={anggota.baptisAnak} />

              <Info
                label="Sidi / Baptis Dewasa"
                value={anggota.sidiBaptisDewasa}
              />

              <Info label="Menikah" value={anggota.menikah} />
            </div>
          </CardContent>
        </Card>

        {/* KESEHATAN */}
        <section className="mt-8">
          <div className="mb-4">
            <div className="text-xs font-bold uppercase tracking-[0.14em] text-blue-600">
              Kesehatan
            </div>

            <h2 className="mt-1 text-xl font-semibold text-slate-900">
              Informasi Kesehatan
            </h2>

            <p className="mt-1 text-sm text-slate-500">
              Ringkasan, perkembangan, dan riwayat pemeriksaan kesehatan
              anggota.
            </p>
          </div>

          <div className="space-y-6">
            <RingkasanKesehatan anggotaId={anggota.id} />

            <TrenKesehatan anggotaId={anggota.id} />

            <section className="rounded-2xl border border-slate-200 bg-white p-5 shadow-sm sm:p-6">
              <RiwayatPemeriksaan anggotaId={anggota.id} refreshKey={0} />
            </section>
          </div>
        </section>

        {/* META */}
        <div className="mt-6 flex flex-col gap-1 border-t border-slate-200 pt-4 text-xs text-slate-400 sm:flex-row sm:items-center sm:justify-between">
          <span>Data Anggota #{anggota.id}</span>

          <span>Terdaftar pada KK #{anggota.kkId}</span>
        </div>
      </div>
    </main>
  )
}

type InfoProps = {
  label: string
  value: string | null
  icon?: ReactNode
  mono?: boolean
  className?: string
}

function Info({ label, value, icon, mono = false, className = '' }: InfoProps) {
  return (
    <div className={className}>
      <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5 text-xs font-medium text-slate-400">
        {icon}
        {label}
      </div>

      <div
        className={[
          'mt-1 text-sm font-medium leading-6 text-slate-800',
          mono ? 'font-mono tabular-nums' : '',
        ].join(' ')}
      >
        {value || '-'}
      </div>
    </div>
  )
}

function formatRtRw(rt: string | null, rw: string | null) {
  if (!rt && !rw) {
    return '-'
  }

  return `${rt ?? '-'} / ${rw ?? '-'}`
}

import { createFileRoute, Link } from '@tanstack/react-router'
import {
  Database,
  FileJson2,
  Files,

  Users,
  UsersRound,
} from 'lucide-react'

import { DataListCard } from '@/components/data-list/data-list-card'
import { DataListEmpty } from '@/components/data-list/data-list-empty'
import { DataListHero } from '@/components/data-list/data-list-hero'
import { DataListLoading } from '@/components/data-list/data-list-loading'
import { DataListPage } from '@/components/data-list/data-list-page'
import { DataListStats } from '@/components/data-list/data-list-stats'

import { DataTable } from '@/components/data-table/data-table'
import { Button } from '@/components/ui/button'

import { kkColumns } from '@/components/kk/list/kk-columns'
import { kkColumnLabels, kkSearchOptions } from '@/components/kk/list/config'

import { useListController } from '@/hooks/use-list-controller'

import { createListSearchValidator } from '@/lib/list-search'

import type { ListSearch } from '@/lib/list-search'

import { listKK } from '@/server/kk.functions'

const searchBy = ['all', 'nama', 'nik', 'alamat'] as const

const sortBy = [
  'kepalaKeluarga',
  'alamat',
  'jumlahAnggota',
  'createdAt',
] as const

type SearchBy = (typeof searchBy)[number]

type SortBy = (typeof sortBy)[number]

type DataKKSearch = ListSearch<SearchBy, SortBy>

const allowedSorts = new Set<string>(sortBy)

const validateSearch = createListSearchValidator({
  searchBy,
  sortBy,
})

export const Route = createFileRoute('/data-kk')({
  validateSearch,

  loaderDeps: ({ search }) => ({
    page: search.page ?? 1,

    limit: search.limit ?? 20,

    q: search.q ?? '',

    searchBy: search.by ?? 'all',

    sortBy: search.sort ?? 'createdAt',

    sortOrder: search.order ?? 'desc',
  }),

  loader: ({ deps }) =>
    listKK({
      data: {
        page: deps.page,

        limit: deps.limit,

        q: deps.q,

        searchBy: deps.searchBy,

        sortBy: deps.sortBy,

        sortOrder: deps.sortOrder,
      },
    }),

  pendingComponent: DataKKPending,

  component: DataKKPage,
})

function DataKKPage() {
  const result = Route.useLoaderData()

  const search = Route.useSearch()

  const navigate = Route.useNavigate()

  const list = useListController<DataKKSearch>({
    search,

    defaultSort: 'createdAt',

    defaultOrder: 'desc',

    allowedSorts,

    navigate,
  })

  const hasData = result.data.length > 0

  const isEmpty = !hasData && !search.q

  return (
    <DataListPage>
      <DataListHero
        icon={<Database className="size-5" />}
        eyebrow="Database Jemaat"
        title="Data Kartu Keluarga"
        description="Kelola data Kartu Keluarga, anggota keluarga, alamat, dan informasi jemaat dalam satu database."
        badge={
          <DataCountBadge>
            {result.pagination.total > 0
              ? `${result.pagination.total.toLocaleString(
                  'id-ID',
                )} Kartu Keluarga tersimpan`
              : 'Belum ada data tersimpan'}
          </DataCountBadge>
        }
        actions={
          <>
            <Button
              variant="outline"
              className="h-10 w-full rounded-lg border-slate-200 bg-white px-4 text-slate-700 shadow-sm hover:bg-slate-50 sm:w-auto"
              asChild
            >
              <Link to="/data-anggota">
                <UsersRound className="size-4 text-blue-600" />
                Data Anggota
              </Link>
            </Button>

            <Button
              className="h-10 w-full rounded-lg px-4 shadow-sm sm:w-auto"
              asChild
            >
              <Link to="/fitur/parser-kk">
                <FileJson2 className="size-4" />
                Tambah Data
              </Link>
            </Button>
          </>
        }
      />

      {hasData && (
        <DataListStats
          items={[
            {
              label: 'Total KK',

              value: result.pagination.total.toLocaleString('id-ID'),

              icon: <Database className="size-4" />,
            },

            {
              label: 'Ditampilkan',

              value: result.data.length.toLocaleString('id-ID'),

              icon: <LayoutList className="size-4" />,
            },

            {
              label: 'Halaman',

              value: `${result.pagination.page} / ${Math.max(
                result.pagination.totalPages,
                1,
              )}`,

              icon: <Files className="size-4" />,
            },
          ]}
        />
      )}

      {isEmpty ? (
        <DataListEmpty
          icon={<Users className="size-7" />}
          title="Belum ada data Kartu Keluarga"
          description="Tambahkan data Kartu Keluarga melalui Parser KK untuk mulai membangun database jemaat."
          action={
            <Button asChild>
              <Link to="/fitur/parser-kk">
                <FileJson2 className="size-4" />
                Tambah Data KK
              </Link>
            </Button>
          }
        />
      ) : (
        <DataListCard
          pagination={result.pagination}
          onPageChange={list.pageChange}
          onPageSizeChange={list.pageSizeChange}
        >
          <DataTable
            columns={kkColumns}
            data={result.data}
            searchValue={search.q ?? ''}
            searchMode={search.by ?? 'all'}
            searchOptions={kkSearchOptions}
            columnLabels={kkColumnLabels}
            sorting={list.sorting}
            onSearchChange={list.searchChange}
            onSearchModeChange={list.searchByChange}
            onSortingChange={list.sortingChange}
          />
        </DataListCard>
      )}
    </DataListPage>
  )
}

function DataKKPending() {
  return (
    <DataListPage>
      <DataListLoading />
    </DataListPage>
  )
}

import { createFileRoute, useRouter } from '@tanstack/react-router'

import { ShieldCheck, TriangleAlert } from 'lucide-react'

import { useState } from 'react'

import { EditKKDialog } from '@/components/kk/edit-kk-dialog'
import { KKDetailHeader } from '@/components/kk/detail/kk-detail-header'
import { KKInfoCard } from '@/components/kk/detail/kk-info-card'
import { KKMembersCard } from '@/components/kk/detail/kk-members-card'

import { useSensitiveKK } from '@/hooks/use-sensitive-kk'

import { getKKDetail } from '@/server/kk.functions'

export const Route = createFileRoute('/data-kk_/$id')({
  loader: async ({ params }) => {
    const id = Number(params.id)

    if (!Number.isInteger(id) || id <= 0) {
      throw new Error('ID tidak valid.')
    }

    return getKKDetail({
      data: {
        id,
      },
    })
  },

  component: KKDetailPage,
})

function KKDetailPage() {
  const data = Route.useLoaderData()

  const router = useRouter()

  const [editOpen, setEditOpen] = useState(false)

  const sensitive = useSensitiveKK(data.id)

  async function handleSaved() {
    sensitive.hide()

    await router.invalidate()
  }

  return (
    <main className="bg-slate-50/70">
      <div className="mx-auto w-full max-w-7xl px-4 py-7 sm:px-6 sm:py-8 lg:px-8 lg:py-10">
        <KKDetailHeader
          kepalaKeluarga={data.kepalaKeluarga}
          desaKelurahan={data.desaKelurahan}
          kecamatan={data.kecamatan}
          jumlahAnggota={data.anggota.length}
          sensitiveVisible={sensitive.isVisible}
          sensitiveLoading={sensitive.isLoading}
          onEdit={() => setEditOpen(true)}
          onToggleSensitive={sensitive.toggle}
        />

        {sensitive.error && (
          <div className="mb-6 flex items-start gap-3 rounded-xl border border-red-200 bg-red-50/60 px-4 py-3.5 text-sm text-red-700">
            <TriangleAlert className="mt-0.5 size-4 shrink-0" />

            <div className="min-w-0">
              <div className="font-medium">
                Data sensitif tidak dapat dibuka
              </div>

              <p className="mt-0.5 text-xs leading-5 text-red-600">
                {sensitive.error}
              </p>
            </div>
          </div>
        )}

        <div className="space-y-6">
          <KKInfoCard
            noKK={sensitive.getNoKK(data.noKK)}
            kepalaKeluarga={data.kepalaKeluarga}
            alamat={data.alamat}
            rt={data.rt}
            rw={data.rw}
            desaKelurahan={data.desaKelurahan}
            kecamatan={data.kecamatan}
            kabupatenKota={data.kabupatenKota}
            kodePos={data.kodePos}
            provinsi={data.provinsi}
          />

          <KKMembersCard
            members={data.anggota}
            sensitiveVisible={sensitive.isVisible}
            getNik={sensitive.getNik}
          />
        </div>

        <div className="mt-6 flex flex-col gap-2 border-t border-slate-200 pt-4 text-xs text-slate-400 sm:flex-row sm:items-center sm:justify-between">
          <span>Data ID #{data.id}</span>

          <span className="flex items-center gap-1.5">
            <ShieldCheck className="size-3.5" />
            Data sensitif disimpan dalam keadaan terenkripsi
          </span>
        </div>
      </div>

      <EditKKDialog
        id={data.id}
        open={editOpen}
        onOpenChange={setEditOpen}
        onSaved={handleSaved}
      />
    </main>
  )
}

FILE: src/routes/event-kesehatan.tsx

import { createFileRoute, getRouteApi, useRouter } from '@tanstack/react-router'
import { CalendarHeart, ClipboardList, HeartPulse, SearchX } from 'lucide-react'

import { CreateEventForm } from '@/components/kesehatan/create-event-form'
import { EventListToolbar } from '@/components/kesehatan/event-list-toolbar'
import { EventList } from '@/components/kesehatan/event-list'

import type { EventStatus } from '@/components/kesehatan/event-list-toolbar'

import { DataCountBadge } from '@/components/data-list/data-count-badge'
import { DataListHero } from '@/components/data-list/data-list-hero'
import { DataListPage } from '@/components/data-list/data-list-page'
import { DataPagination } from '@/components/data-list/data-pagination'

import type { PageSize } from '@/components/data-list/data-pagination'

import { listEventKesehatan } from '@/server/kesehatan'

type EventKesehatanSearch = {
  page?: number

  limit?: PageSize

  q?: string

  status?: Exclude<EventStatus, 'all'>

  tahun?: number
}

const rootRoute = getRouteApi('__root__')

function validateSearch(search: Record<string, unknown>): EventKesehatanSearch {
  const result: EventKesehatanSearch = {}

  const page = Number(search.page)

  if (Number.isInteger(page) && page > 1) {
    result.page = page
  }

  const limit = Number(search.limit)

  if (limit === 10 || limit === 20 || limit === 50 || limit === 100) {
    result.limit = limit
  }

  if (typeof search.q === 'string') {
    const q = search.q.trim()

    if (q) {
      result.q = q
    }
  }

  if (search.status === 'aktif' || search.status === 'selesai') {
    result.status = search.status
  }

  const tahun = Number(search.tahun)

  if (Number.isInteger(tahun) && tahun >= 2000 && tahun <= 2100) {
    result.tahun = tahun
  }

  return result
}

export const Route = createFileRoute('/event-kesehatan')({
  validateSearch,

  loaderDeps: ({
    search,
  }): {
    page: number
    limit: PageSize
    q: string
    status: EventStatus
    tahun?: number
  } => ({
    page: search.page ?? 1,

    limit: search.limit ?? 10,

    q: search.q ?? '',

    status: search.status ?? 'all',

    tahun: search.tahun,
  }),

  loader: ({ deps }) =>
    listEventKesehatan({
      data: {
        page: deps.page,

        limit: deps.limit,

        q: deps.q,

        status: deps.status,

        tahun: deps.tahun,
      },
    }),

  component: EventKesehatanPage,
})

function EventKesehatanPage() {
  const { user } = rootRoute.useRouteContext()

  const result = Route.useLoaderData()

  const search = Route.useSearch()

  const navigate = Route.useNavigate()

  const router = useRouter()

  const events = result.data

  const isPetugas = user?.role === 'PETUGAS'

  const activeEvents = events.filter((event) => !event.isClosed)

  const status: EventStatus = search.status ?? 'all'

  const hasFilter = Boolean(search.q || search.status || search.tahun)

  function handleSearchChange(q: string) {
    void navigate({
      search: (previous) => ({
        ...previous,

        page: undefined,

        q: q.trim() || undefined,
      }),
    })
  }

  function handleStatusChange(nextStatus: EventStatus) {
    void navigate({
      search: (previous) => ({
        ...previous,

        page: undefined,

        status: nextStatus === 'all' ? undefined : nextStatus,
      }),
    })
  }

  function handleYearChange(tahun: number | undefined) {
    void navigate({
      search: (previous) => ({
        ...previous,

        page: undefined,

        tahun,
      }),
    })
  }

  function handleReset() {
    void navigate({
      search: (previous) => ({
        ...previous,

        page: undefined,

        q: undefined,

        status: undefined,

        tahun: undefined,
      }),
    })
  }

  function handlePageChange(page: number) {
    void navigate({
      search: (previous) => ({
        ...previous,

        page: page <= 1 ? undefined : page,
      }),
    })
  }

  function handlePageSizeChange(limit: PageSize) {
    void navigate({
      search: (previous) => ({
        ...previous,

        page: undefined,

        limit: limit === 10 ? undefined : limit,
      }),
    })
  }

  return (
    <DataListPage>
      <DataListHero
        icon={
          isPetugas ? (
            <HeartPulse className="size-5" />
          ) : (
            <CalendarHeart className="size-5" />
          )
        }
        eyebrow={isPetugas ? 'Pelayanan Kesehatan' : 'Kesehatan Jemaat'}
        title={isPetugas ? 'Pelayanan Event Kesehatan' : 'Event Kesehatan'}
        description={
          isPetugas
            ? 'Kelola kegiatan pemeriksaan kesehatan jemaat, cari warga, dan catat hasil pemeriksaan melalui event yang sedang berjalan.'
            : 'Kelola kegiatan pemeriksaan kesehatan warga, mulai dari pembuatan event hingga pencatatan hasil pemeriksaan.'
        }
        badge={
          <DataCountBadge>
            {hasFilter
              ? `${result.pagination.total.toLocaleString(
                  'id-ID',
                )} event ditemukan`
              : activeEvents.length > 0
                ? `${activeEvents.length.toLocaleString('id-ID')} event aktif`
                : result.pagination.total > 0
                  ? `${result.pagination.total.toLocaleString(
                      'id-ID',
                    )} event tersimpan`
                  : 'Belum ada event'}
          </DataCountBadge>
        }
      />

      {isPetugas && activeEvents.length > 0 ? (
        <section className="mb-6 overflow-hidden rounded-2xl border border-blue-200 bg-blue-50/40 shadow-sm">
          <div className="border-b border-blue-100 bg-white/70 px-5 py-4">
            <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
              <div className="flex size-10 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-xl bg-blue-100 text-blue-700">
                <HeartPulse className="size-5" />
              </div>

              <div>
                <h2 className="font-semibold text-slate-900">
                  Event Sedang Aktif
                </h2>

                <p className="mt-0.5 text-xs text-slate-500">
                  Pilih event untuk mulai pelayanan dan pencatatan pemeriksaan.
                </p>
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>

          <div className="p-4 sm:p-5">
            <EventList events={activeEvents} />
          </div>
        </section>
      ) : null}

      <div className="grid gap-6 lg:grid-cols-[360px_minmax(0,1fr)]">
        <aside className="min-w-0 lg:sticky lg:top-28 lg:self-start">
          <CreateEventForm
            onCreated={async () => {
              await router.invalidate()
            }}
          />
        </aside>

        <section className="min-w-0 overflow-hidden rounded-2xl border border-slate-200 bg-white shadow-sm">
          <div className="border-b border-slate-200 px-4 py-4 sm:px-5">
            <div className="mb-4 flex items-center justify-between gap-4">
              <div className="flex min-w-0 items-center gap-3">
                <div className="flex size-10 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-xl bg-blue-50 text-blue-600">
                  <ClipboardList className="size-4" />
                </div>

                <div className="min-w-0">
                  <h2 className="font-semibold text-slate-900">
                    {isPetugas ? 'Seluruh Event' : 'Daftar Event'}
                  </h2>

                  <p className="mt-0.5 text-xs text-slate-500">
                    {result.pagination.total > 0
                      ? `${result.pagination.total.toLocaleString(
                          'id-ID',
                        )} event sesuai filter`
                      : 'Tidak ada event yang sesuai'}
                  </p>
                </div>
              </div>
            </div>

            <EventListToolbar
              searchValue={search.q ?? ''}
              status={status}
              tahun={search.tahun}
              onSearchChange={handleSearchChange}
              onStatusChange={handleStatusChange}
              onYearChange={handleYearChange}
              onReset={handleReset}
            />
          </div>

          <div className="p-4 sm:p-5">
            {events.length > 0 ? (
              <EventList events={events} />
            ) : (
              <div className="flex min-h-64 flex-col items-center justify-center px-6 text-center">
                <div className="flex size-12 items-center justify-center rounded-xl bg-slate-100 text-slate-400">
                  <SearchX className="size-5" />
                </div>

                <h3 className="mt-4 font-semibold text-slate-800">
                  {hasFilter ? 'Event tidak ditemukan' : 'Belum ada event'}
                </h3>

                <p className="mt-1 max-w-sm text-sm leading-6 text-slate-500">
                  {hasFilter
                    ? 'Coba ubah kata pencarian, status, atau tahun event.'
                    : 'Event kesehatan yang dibuat akan muncul di sini.'}
                </p>
              </div>
            )}
          </div>

          {result.pagination.total > 0 ? (
            <div className="border-t border-slate-200 bg-slate-50/50 px-4 py-4 sm:px-5">
              <DataPagination
                pagination={result.pagination}
                onPageChange={handlePageChange}
                onPageSizeChange={handlePageSizeChange}
              />
            </div>
          ) : null}
        </section>
      </div>
    </DataListPage>
  )
}
