import {
  createFileRoute,
  getRouteApi,
  Link,
  useRouter,
} from '@tanstack/react-router'
import { ArrowLeft, HeartPulse, LockKeyhole, ShieldCheck } from 'lucide-react'
import { useState } from 'react'

import { DaftarPemeriksaanEvent } from '@/components/kesehatan/daftar-pemeriksaan-event'
import { EventKesehatanHeader } from '@/components/kesehatan/event-kesehatan-header'
import { PetugasEvent } from '@/components/kesehatan/petugas-event'
import { WargaKesehatanSection } from '@/components/kesehatan/warga-kesehatan-section'

import { Button } from '@/components/ui/button'

import { getEventKesehatan } from '@/server/kesehatan'

const rootRoute = getRouteApi('__root__')

export const Route = createFileRoute('/event-kesehatan_/$id')({
  loader: async ({ params }) => {
    const id = Number(params.id)

    if (!Number.isInteger(id) || id <= 0) {
      throw new Error('ID event tidak valid.')
    }

    return getEventKesehatan({
      data: {
        id,
      },
    })
  },

  component: EventKesehatanDetailPage,
})

function EventKesehatanDetailPage() {
  const { user } = rootRoute.useRouteContext()

  const event = Route.useLoaderData()

  const router = useRouter()

  const [refreshKey, setRefreshKey] = useState(0)

  const isPetugas = user?.role === 'PETUGAS'

  async function handleChanged() {
    setRefreshKey((value) => value + 1)

    await router.invalidate()
  }

  return (
    <main className="bg-slate-50/70">
      <div className="mx-auto w-full max-w-6xl px-4 py-6 sm:px-6 sm:py-8 lg:px-8 lg:py-10">
        {/* BACK */}
        <div className="mb-4">
          <Button
            variant="ghost"
            size="sm"
            className="-ml-2 h-9 rounded-lg px-2 text-slate-500 hover:bg-white hover:text-slate-900"
            asChild
          >
            <Link to="/event-kesehatan">
              <ArrowLeft className="size-4" />
              Kembali ke Event
            </Link>
          </Button>
        </div>

        {/* HEADER */}
        <EventKesehatanHeader event={event} onChanged={handleChanged} />

        {/* MODE PETUGAS */}
        {isPetugas && !event.isClosed ? (
          <div className="mb-6 flex items-start gap-3 rounded-2xl border border-blue-200 bg-blue-50/70 px-4 py-4 shadow-sm sm:px-5">
            <div className="flex size-10 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-xl bg-blue-100 text-blue-700">
              <HeartPulse className="size-5" />
            </div>

            <div className="min-w-0">
              <div className="text-sm font-semibold text-blue-900">
                Mode Pelayanan Kesehatan
              </div>

              <p className="mt-1 text-sm leading-6 text-blue-700/80">
                Cari warga yang akan diperiksa, masukkan hasil pemeriksaan, lalu
                simpan data ke event ini.
              </p>
            </div>
          </div>
        ) : null}

        {/* MODE ADMIN */}
        {!isPetugas && !event.isClosed ? (
          <div className="mb-6 flex items-start gap-3 rounded-2xl border border-slate-200 bg-white px-4 py-4 shadow-sm sm:px-5">
            <div className="flex size-10 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-xl bg-slate-100 text-slate-600">
              <ShieldCheck className="size-4" />
            </div>

            <div className="min-w-0">
              <div className="text-sm font-semibold text-slate-800">
                Pengelolaan Event
              </div>

              <p className="mt-1 text-sm leading-6 text-slate-500">
                Event masih aktif. Data petugas dan hasil pemeriksaan masih
                dapat dikelola.
              </p>
            </div>
          </div>
        ) : null}

        {/* CLOSED NOTICE */}
        {event.isClosed ? (
          <div className="mb-6 flex items-start gap-3 rounded-2xl border border-slate-200 bg-white px-4 py-4 shadow-sm sm:px-5">
            <div className="flex size-10 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-xl bg-slate-100 text-slate-500">
              <LockKeyhole className="size-4" />
            </div>

            <div className="min-w-0">
              <div className="text-sm font-semibold text-slate-800">
                Event telah selesai
              </div>

              <p className="mt-1 text-sm leading-6 text-slate-500">
                Data pemeriksaan tetap dapat dilihat, tetapi event yang sudah
                ditutup tidak dapat ditambah atau diubah.
              </p>
            </div>
          </div>
        ) : null}

        {/* EVENT AKTIF */}
        {!event.isClosed ? (
          <>
            {isPetugas ? (
              /*
               * PETUGAS:
               * pekerjaan utama langsung di atas.
               */
              <div className="space-y-6">
                <WargaKesehatanSection
                  eventId={event.id}
                  petugas={event.petugas}
                  refreshKey={refreshKey}
                  onChanged={handleChanged}
                />

                <PetugasEvent
                  eventId={event.id}
                  petugas={event.petugas}
                  onChanged={handleChanged}
                />
              </div>
            ) : (
              /*
               * ADMIN:
               * tetap menggunakan urutan pengelolaan.
               */
              <div className="space-y-6">
                <PetugasEvent
                  eventId={event.id}
                  petugas={event.petugas}
                  onChanged={handleChanged}
                />

                <WargaKesehatanSection
                  eventId={event.id}
                  petugas={event.petugas}
                  refreshKey={refreshKey}
                  onChanged={handleChanged}
                />
              </div>
            )}
          </>
        ) : null}

        {/* DAFTAR PEMERIKSAAN */}
        <div className={event.isClosed ? '' : 'mt-6'}>
          <DaftarPemeriksaanEvent
            eventId={event.id}
            refreshKey={refreshKey}
            readOnly={event.isClosed}
            onChanged={handleChanged}
          />
        </div>
      </div>
    </main>
  )
}


############################################################
FILE: src/routes/fitur.import-excel.tsx
############################################################

import { createFileRoute, Link, useRouter } from '@tanstack/react-router'
import {
  ArrowLeft,
  CheckCircle2,
  Database,
  FileSpreadsheet,
  Loader2,
  Upload,
} from 'lucide-react'
import { useState } from 'react'
import type { ReactNode } from 'react'
import { toast } from 'sonner'

import { Button } from '@/components/ui/button'

import { parseKKExcel } from '@/lib/kk-parser/parse-excel'
import type { ExcelKKImportResult } from '@/lib/kk-parser/parse-excel'

import { kkSchema } from '@/lib/kk-parser/schema'

import { saveKK } from '@/server/kk.functions'

export const Route = createFileRoute('/fitur/import-excel')({
  component: ImportExcelPage,
})

function ImportExcelPage() {
  const router = useRouter()

  const [fileName, setFileName] = useState<string | null>(null)

  const [result, setResult] = useState<ExcelKKImportResult | null>(null)

  const [isReading, setIsReading] = useState(false)
  const [isImporting, setIsImporting] = useState(false)

  const [showSensitive, setShowSensitive] = useState(false)

  const totalAnggota =
    result?.data.reduce((total, kk) => total + kk.anggota.length, 0) ?? 0

  async function handleFile(file: File) {
    setFileName(file.name)
    setResult(null)
    setShowSensitive(false)
    setIsReading(true)

    try {
      const parsed = await parseKKExcel(file)

      setResult(parsed)

      if (parsed.errors.length > 0) {
        toast.error('Excel perlu diperiksa', {
          description: `${parsed.errors.length} masalah ditemukan.`,
        })

        return
      }

      toast.success('Excel berhasil dibaca', {
        description: `${parsed.data.length} KK siap diimpor.`,
      })
    } catch (error) {
      const message =
        error instanceof Error
          ? error.message
          : 'File Excel tidak dapat dibaca.'

      toast.error('Gagal membaca Excel', {
        description: message,
      })
    } finally {
      setIsReading(false)
    }
  }

  async function handleImport() {
    const importResult = result

    if (
      !importResult ||
      importResult.errors.length > 0 ||
      importResult.data.length === 0 ||
      isImporting
    ) {
      return
    }

    setIsImporting(true)

    let success = 0
    const importErrors: string[] = []

    try {
      for (const kk of importResult.data) {
        const validation = kkSchema.safeParse(kk)

        if (!validation.success) {
          importErrors.push(`${kk.kepalaKeluarga ?? 'KK'}: data tidak valid.`)

          continue
        }

        try {
          await saveKK({
            data: validation.data,
          })

          success += 1
        } catch (error) {
          importErrors.push(
            `${kk.kepalaKeluarga ?? 'KK'}: ${
              error instanceof Error ? error.message : 'gagal disimpan'
            }`,
          )
        }
      }

      if (importErrors.length > 0) {
        setResult({
          ...importResult,
          errors: importErrors,
        })

        toast.error('Import selesai dengan beberapa masalah', {
          description: `${success} KK berhasil, ${importErrors.length} gagal.`,
        })

        return
      }

      toast.success('Import Excel selesai', {
        description: `${success} Kartu Keluarga berhasil disimpan.`,
      })

      await router.invalidate()
    } finally {
      setIsImporting(false)
    }
  }

  function handleClear() {
    setFileName(null)
    setResult(null)
    setShowSensitive(false)
  }

  function hasRowError(rowNumber: number) {
    if (!result) {
      return false
    }

    return result.errors.some((error) =>
      error.startsWith(`Baris ${rowNumber}:`),
    )
  }

  return (
    <main className="min-h-screen bg-slate-50/70">
      <div className="mx-auto w-full max-w-7xl px-4 py-7 sm:px-6 sm:py-8 lg:px-8 lg:py-10">
        <section className="overflow-hidden rounded-2xl border border-slate-200 bg-white shadow-sm">
          <div className="h-1 bg-linear-to-r from-emerald-600 via-emerald-500 to-teal-400" />

          <div className="flex flex-col gap-5 px-5 py-5 sm:px-6 lg:flex-row lg:items-center lg:justify-between">
            <div className="flex min-w-0 items-start gap-4">
              <div className="flex size-12 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-xl bg-emerald-50 text-emerald-600">
                <FileSpreadsheet className="size-5" />
              </div>

              <div>
                <div className="text-xs font-bold uppercase tracking-[0.16em] text-emerald-600">
                  Import Data
                </div>

                <h1 className="mt-2 text-2xl font-bold tracking-tight text-slate-900">
                  Import Data Jemaat dari Excel
                </h1>

                <p className="mt-2 max-w-3xl text-sm leading-6 text-slate-500">
                  Baca data Excel, periksa seluruh isi dan hasil validasi,
                  kemudian simpan data yang valid ke database.
                </p>
              </div>
            </div>

            <div className="flex flex-col gap-2 sm:flex-row">
              <Button variant="outline" asChild>
                <Link to="/fitur/parser-kk">
                  <ArrowLeft className="size-4" />
                  Parser KK
                </Link>
              </Button>

              <Button variant="outline" asChild>
                <Link to="/data-kk">
                  <Database className="size-4" />
                  Data KK
                </Link>
              </Button>
            </div>
          </div>
        </section>

        <section className="mt-6 rounded-2xl border border-slate-200 bg-white p-5 shadow-sm sm:p-6">
          <label className="flex cursor-pointer flex-col items-center justify-center rounded-xl border border-dashed border-slate-300 bg-slate-50 px-6 py-10 text-center hover:bg-slate-100">
            {isReading ? (
              <Loader2 className="size-7 animate-spin text-emerald-600" />
            ) : (
              <Upload className="size-7 text-slate-400" />
            )}

            <div className="mt-3 text-sm font-semibold text-slate-800">
              {fileName ?? 'Pilih file Excel'}
            </div>

            <div className="mt-1 text-xs text-slate-500">Format file .xlsx</div>

            <input
              type="file"
              accept=".xlsx,application/vnd.openxmlformats-officedocument.spreadsheetml.sheet"
              className="sr-only"
              disabled={isReading || isImporting}
              onChange={(event) => {
                const file = event.target.files?.[0]

                if (file) {
                  void handleFile(file)
                }

                event.currentTarget.value = ''
              }}
            />
          </label>
        </section>

        {result && (
          <>
            <section className="mt-6 grid gap-4 sm:grid-cols-2 xl:grid-cols-4">
              <Summary label="Baris Excel" value={result.totalRows} />

              <Summary label="KK Valid" value={result.data.length} />

              <Summary label="Anggota Valid" value={totalAnggota} />

              <Summary label="Masalah" value={result.errors.length} />
            </section>

            <section className="mt-6 overflow-hidden rounded-2xl border border-slate-200 bg-white shadow-sm">
              <div className="flex flex-col gap-3 border-b border-slate-200 px-5 py-4 sm:flex-row sm:items-center sm:justify-between">
                <div>
                  <h2 className="text-base font-semibold text-slate-900">
                    Preview Data Excel
                  </h2>

                  <p className="mt-1 text-xs text-slate-500">
                    Seluruh baris ditampilkan sebelum masuk database.
                  </p>
                </div>

                <Button
                  type="button"
                  variant="outline"
                  size="sm"
                  onClick={() => setShowSensitive((current) => !current)}
                >
                  {showSensitive
                    ? 'Sembunyikan NIK / No KK'
                    : 'Tampilkan NIK / No KK'}
                </Button>
              </div>

              <div className="max-h-[60vh] overflow-auto">
                <table className="min-w-max text-left text-xs">
                  <thead className="sticky top-0 z-10 bg-slate-50 text-slate-500">
                    <tr className="border-b border-slate-200">
                      <Head>Status</Head>
                      <Head>Baris</Head>
                      <Head>Keluarga</Head>
                      <Head>No</Head>
                      <Head>Nama</Head>
                      <Head>No KK</Head>
                      <Head>NIK</Head>

                      <Head>Alamat</Head>
                      <Head>RT/RW</Head>
                      <Head>Desa/Kelurahan</Head>
                      <Head>Kecamatan</Head>
                      <Head>Kabupaten/Kota</Head>
                      <Head>Provinsi</Head>
                      <Head>Kode Pos</Head>

                      <Head>L/P</Head>
                      <Head>Tempat Lahir</Head>
                      <Head>Tanggal Lahir</Head>
                      <Head>Agama</Head>
                      <Head>Pendidikan</Head>
                      <Head>Pekerjaan</Head>
                      <Head>Golongan Darah</Head>

                      <Head>Ayah</Head>
                      <Head>Ibu</Head>
                      <Head>Baptis Anak</Head>
                      <Head>Sidi / Baptis Dewasa</Head>
                      <Head>Menikah</Head>
                      <Head>Domisili</Head>
                    </tr>
                  </thead>

                  <tbody>
                    {result.preview.map((row) => (
                      <tr
                        key={row.rowNumber}
                        className={[
                          'border-b border-slate-100',
                          hasRowError(row.rowNumber)
                            ? 'bg-red-50/60'
                            : 'bg-white',
                        ].join(' ')}
                      >
                        <Cell>
                          {hasRowError(row.rowNumber) ? (
                            <span className="inline-flex rounded-full bg-red-100 px-2 py-1 text-[10px] font-semibold text-red-700">
                              Error
                            </span>
                          ) : (
                            <span className="inline-flex rounded-full bg-emerald-100 px-2 py-1 text-[10px] font-semibold text-emerald-700">
                              OK
                            </span>
                          )}
                        </Cell>
                        <Cell>{row.rowNumber}</Cell>
                        <Cell>{row.keluarga || '-'}</Cell>
                        <Cell>{row.nomor || '-'}</Cell>

                        <Cell strong>{row.nama || '-'}</Cell>

                        <Cell mono>
                          {showSensitive
                            ? row.noKK || '-'
                            : maskIdentity(row.noKK)}
                        </Cell>

                        <Cell mono>
                          {showSensitive
                            ? row.nik || '-'
                            : maskIdentity(row.nik)}
                        </Cell>

                        <Cell>{row.alamat || '-'}</Cell>
                        <Cell>{row.rtRw || '-'}</Cell>
                        <Cell>{row.desaKelurahan || '-'}</Cell>
                        <Cell>{row.kecamatan || '-'}</Cell>
                        <Cell>{row.kabupatenKota || '-'}</Cell>
                        <Cell>{row.provinsi || '-'}</Cell>
                        <Cell>{row.kodePos || '-'}</Cell>

                        <Cell>{row.jenisKelamin || '-'}</Cell>
                        <Cell>{row.tempatLahir || '-'}</Cell>
                        <Cell>{row.tanggalLahir || '-'}</Cell>
                        <Cell>{row.agama || '-'}</Cell>
                        <Cell>{row.pendidikan || '-'}</Cell>
                        <Cell>{row.pekerjaan || '-'}</Cell>
                        <Cell>{row.golonganDarah || '-'}</Cell>

                        <Cell>{row.ayah || '-'}</Cell>
                        <Cell>{row.ibu || '-'}</Cell>
                        <Cell>{row.baptisAnak || '-'}</Cell>
                        <Cell>{row.sidiBaptisDewasa || '-'}</Cell>
                        <Cell>{row.menikah || '-'}</Cell>
                        <Cell>{row.domisili || '-'}</Cell>
                      </tr>
                    ))}
                  </tbody>
                </table>
              </div>
            </section>

            {result.errors.length > 0 && (
              <section className="mt-6 rounded-2xl border border-red-200 bg-red-50 p-5">
                <div className="font-semibold text-red-700">
                  Perlu diperbaiki
                </div>

                <ul className="mt-3 space-y-1 text-sm text-red-600">
                  {result.errors.map((error, index) => (
                    <li key={`${index}-${error}`}>• {error}</li>
                  ))}
                </ul>
              </section>
            )}

            <section className="mt-6 flex flex-col-reverse gap-2 rounded-2xl border border-slate-200 bg-white p-5 sm:flex-row sm:justify-end">
              <Button
                type="button"
                variant="outline"
                disabled={isImporting}
                onClick={handleClear}
              >
                Pilih File Lain
              </Button>

              <Button
                type="button"
                disabled={
                  result.data.length === 0 ||
                  result.errors.length > 0 ||
                  isImporting
                }
                onClick={() => void handleImport()}
              >
                {isImporting ? (
                  <>
                    <Loader2 className="size-4 animate-spin" />
                    Mengimpor...
                  </>
                ) : (
                  <>
                    <CheckCircle2 className="size-4" />
                    Import ke Database
                  </>
                )}
              </Button>
            </section>
          </>
        )}
      </div>
    </main>
  )
}

function maskIdentity(value: string) {
  const digits = value.replace(/\D/g, '')

  if (!digits) {
    return '-'
  }

  if (digits.length <= 4) {
    return '••••'
  }

  return `${'•'.repeat(Math.max(digits.length - 4, 4))}${digits.slice(-4)}`
}

function Summary({ label, value }: { label: string; value: number }) {
  return (
    <div className="rounded-2xl border border-slate-200 bg-white p-5 shadow-sm">
      <div className="text-xs font-medium text-slate-500">{label}</div>

      <div className="mt-2 text-2xl font-semibold tabular-nums text-slate-900">
        {value.toLocaleString('id-ID')}
      </div>
    </div>
  )
}

function Head({ children }: { children: ReactNode }) {
  return (
    <th className="whitespace-nowrap px-3 py-3 font-semibold">{children}</th>
  )
}

function Cell({
  children,
  mono = false,
  strong = false,
}: {
  children: ReactNode
  mono?: boolean
  strong?: boolean
}) {
  return (
    <td
      className={[
        'whitespace-nowrap px-3 py-2.5 text-slate-700',
        mono ? 'font-mono tabular-nums' : '',
        strong ? 'font-semibold text-slate-900' : '',
      ].join(' ')}
    >
      {children}
    </td>
  )
}

import { createFileRoute } from '@tanstack/react-router'
import { useState } from 'react'

import { CreateKKDialog } from '@/components/kk/create/create-kk-dialog'
import { KKParserHeader } from '@/components/kk/parser/kk-parser-header'
import { ParserInputCard } from '@/components/kk/parser/parser-input-card'
import { ParserPreviewCard } from '@/components/kk/parser/parser-preview-card'
import { ParserSaveCard } from '@/components/kk/parser/parser-save-card'
import { ParserValidationCard } from '@/components/kk/parser/parser-validation-card'
import { PrivacyNotice } from '@/components/kk/parser/privacy-notice'

import { useKKParser } from '@/hooks/use-kk-parser'

export const Route = createFileRoute('/fitur/parser-kk')({
  component: ParserKKPage,
})

function ParserKKPage() {
  const parser = useKKParser()

  const [createOpen, setCreateOpen] = useState(false)

  return (
    <main className="bg-slate-50/70">
      <div className="mx-auto w-full max-w-7xl px-4 py-7 sm:px-6 sm:py-8 lg:px-8 lg:py-10">
        {/* HEADER */}
        <KKParserHeader onAddManual={() => setCreateOpen(true)} />
        {/* PRIVACY */}
        <div className="mb-6">
          <PrivacyNotice />
        </div>

        {/* PARSER WORKSPACE */}
        <section className="grid min-w-0 gap-6 lg:grid-cols-2 lg:items-stretch">
          <div className="min-w-0">
            <ParserInputCard
              text={parser.text}
              error={parser.parseError}
              canClear={parser.canClear}
              onTextChange={parser.setText}
              onParse={parser.handleParse}
              onClear={parser.handleClear}
            />
          </div>

          <div className="min-w-0">
            <ParserPreviewCard
              data={parser.previewData}
              isValid={parser.isValid}
              showSensitive={parser.showSensitive}
              onToggleSensitive={parser.toggleSensitive}
              onCopy={parser.handleCopy}
            />
          </div>
        </section>

        {/* RESULT */}
        {parser.result && (
          <section className="mt-6 space-y-6">
            <ParserValidationCard
              isValid={parser.isValid}
              errors={parser.validationErrors}
            />

            <ParserSaveCard
              canSave={parser.canSave}
              isSaving={parser.isSaving}
              isSaved={parser.isSaved}
              onSave={parser.handleSave}
            />
          </section>
        )}
      </div>

      <CreateKKDialog
        open={createOpen}
        onOpenChange={setCreateOpen}
        onCreated={() => {
          setCreateOpen(false)
        }}
      />
    </main>
  )
}

############################################################
FILE: src/routes/login.tsx
############################################################

import { createFileRoute, redirect } from '@tanstack/react-router'
import { LogIn, ShieldCheck } from 'lucide-react'
import { useState } from 'react'

import type { FormEvent } from 'react'

import { Button } from '@/components/ui/button'
import { Input } from '@/components/ui/input'
import { Label } from '@/components/ui/label'
import {
  getCurrentUser,
  getInitialSetupStatus,
  login,
} from '@/server/auth/actions.functions'

export const Route = createFileRoute('/login')({
  loader: async () => {
    const [user, setup] = await Promise.all([
      getCurrentUser(),
      getInitialSetupStatus(),
    ])

    if (setup.requiresSetup) {
      throw redirect({
        to: '/setup-admin',
      })
    }

    if (user) {
      throw redirect({
        to: user.role === 'PETUGAS' ? '/event-kesehatan' : '/',
      })
    }

    return null
  },

  component: LoginPage,
})

function LoginPage() {
  const navigate = Route.useNavigate()

  const [username, setUsername] = useState('')
  const [password, setPassword] = useState('')

  const [errorMessage, setErrorMessage] = useState<string | null>(null)

  const [isSubmitting, setIsSubmitting] = useState(false)

  async function handleSubmit(event: FormEvent<HTMLFormElement>) {
    event.preventDefault()

    if (isSubmitting) {
      return
    }

    setErrorMessage(null)
    setIsSubmitting(true)

    try {
      const result = await login({
        data: {
          username,
          password,
        },
      })

      if (!result.success) {
        setErrorMessage(result.message)
        return
      }

      await navigate({
        to: result.user.role === 'PETUGAS' ? '/event-kesehatan' : '/',
        replace: true,
      })
    } catch {
      setErrorMessage('Login tidak dapat diproses. Silakan coba kembali.')
    } finally {
      setIsSubmitting(false)
    }
  }

  return (
    <main className="mx-auto flex w-full max-w-7xl flex-1 items-center justify-center px-4 py-12 sm:px-6">
      <div className="w-full max-w-md">
        <div className="rounded-2xl border border-slate-200 bg-white p-6 shadow-sm sm:p-8">
          <div className="mb-7">
            <div className="mb-5 flex size-12 items-center justify-center rounded-xl bg-blue-50 text-blue-600">
              <ShieldCheck className="size-6" />
            </div>

            <h1 className="text-2xl font-bold tracking-tight text-slate-900">
              Masuk
            </h1>

            <p className="mt-2 text-sm leading-6 text-slate-500">
              Masuk ke Sistem Data Jemaat GKJ Slogohimo.
            </p>
          </div>

          <form className="space-y-5" onSubmit={handleSubmit}>
            <div className="space-y-2">
              <Label htmlFor="username">Username</Label>

              <Input
                id="username"
                name="username"
                autoComplete="username"
                value={username}
                onChange={(event) => setUsername(event.target.value)}
                placeholder="Masukkan username"
                disabled={isSubmitting}
                autoFocus
                required
              />
            </div>

            <div className="space-y-2">
              <Label htmlFor="password">Password</Label>

              <Input
                id="password"
                name="password"
                type="password"
                autoComplete="current-password"
                value={password}
                onChange={(event) => setPassword(event.target.value)}
                placeholder="Masukkan password"
                disabled={isSubmitting}
                required
              />
            </div>

            {errorMessage ? (
              <div className="rounded-xl border border-red-200 bg-red-50 px-4 py-3 text-sm text-red-700">
                {errorMessage}
              </div>
            ) : null}

            <Button type="submit" className="w-full" disabled={isSubmitting}>
              <LogIn className="size-4" />

              {isSubmitting ? 'Memeriksa...' : 'Masuk'}
            </Button>
          </form>
        </div>

        <p className="mt-5 text-center text-xs leading-5 text-slate-400">
          Akses hanya untuk pengguna internal yang telah terdaftar.
        </p>
      </div>
    </main>
  )
}


############################################################
FILE: src/routes/setup-admin.tsx
############################################################

import { createFileRoute, redirect } from '@tanstack/react-router'
import { ShieldCheck, UserRoundPlus } from 'lucide-react'
import { useState } from 'react'

import type { FormEvent } from 'react'

import { Button } from '@/components/ui/button'
import { Input } from '@/components/ui/input'
import { Label } from '@/components/ui/label'
import {
  createInitialAdmin,
  getInitialSetupStatus,
} from '@/server/auth/actions.functions'

export const Route = createFileRoute('/setup-admin')({
  loader: async () => {
    const status = await getInitialSetupStatus()

    if (!status.requiresSetup) {
      throw redirect({
        to: '/login',
      })
    }

    return status
  },

  component: SetupAdminPage,
})

function SetupAdminPage() {
  const navigate = Route.useNavigate()

  const [nama, setNama] = useState('')
  const [username, setUsername] = useState('')
  const [password, setPassword] = useState('')
  const [passwordConfirm, setPasswordConfirm] = useState('')

  const [errorMessage, setErrorMessage] = useState<string | null>(null)
  const [isSubmitting, setIsSubmitting] = useState(false)

  async function handleSubmit(event: FormEvent<HTMLFormElement>) {
    event.preventDefault()

    if (isSubmitting) {
      return
    }

    setErrorMessage(null)

    if (password !== passwordConfirm) {
      setErrorMessage('Konfirmasi password tidak sama.')
      return
    }

    setIsSubmitting(true)

    try {
      await createInitialAdmin({
        data: {
          nama,
          username,
          password,
        },
      })

      await navigate({
        to: '/',
        replace: true,
      })
    } catch (error) {
      setErrorMessage(
        error instanceof Error
          ? error.message
          : 'Administrator tidak dapat dibuat.',
      )
    } finally {
      setIsSubmitting(false)
    }
  }

  return (
    <main className="mx-auto flex w-full max-w-7xl flex-1 items-center justify-center px-4 py-12 sm:px-6">
      <div className="w-full max-w-md">
        <div className="rounded-2xl border border-slate-200 bg-white p-6 shadow-sm sm:p-8">
          <div className="mb-7">
            <div className="mb-5 flex size-12 items-center justify-center rounded-xl bg-blue-50 text-blue-600">
              <UserRoundPlus className="size-6" />
            </div>

            <h1 className="text-2xl font-bold tracking-tight text-slate-900">
              Buat Administrator
            </h1>

            <p className="mt-2 text-sm leading-6 text-slate-500">
              Buat akun administrator pertama untuk Sistem Data Jemaat GKJ
              Slogohimo.
            </p>
          </div>

          <form className="space-y-5" onSubmit={handleSubmit}>
            <div className="space-y-2">
              <Label htmlFor="nama">Nama</Label>

              <Input
                id="nama"
                name="nama"
                autoComplete="name"
                value={nama}
                onChange={(event) => setNama(event.target.value)}
                placeholder="Nama administrator"
                disabled={isSubmitting}
                required
              />
            </div>

            <div className="space-y-2">
              <Label htmlFor="username">Username</Label>

              <Input
                id="username"
                name="username"
                autoComplete="username"
                value={username}
                onChange={(event) => setUsername(event.target.value)}
                placeholder="Username"
                disabled={isSubmitting}
                required
              />

              <p className="text-xs text-slate-400">
                Gunakan minimal 3 karakter.
              </p>
            </div>

            <div className="space-y-2">
              <Label htmlFor="password">Password</Label>

              <Input
                id="password"
                name="password"
                type="password"
                autoComplete="new-password"
                value={password}
                onChange={(event) => setPassword(event.target.value)}
                placeholder="Minimal 8 karakter"
                disabled={isSubmitting}
                required
              />
            </div>

            <div className="space-y-2">
              <Label htmlFor="passwordConfirm">Konfirmasi Password</Label>

              <Input
                id="passwordConfirm"
                name="passwordConfirm"
                type="password"
                autoComplete="new-password"
                value={passwordConfirm}
                onChange={(event) => setPasswordConfirm(event.target.value)}
                placeholder="Ulangi password"
                disabled={isSubmitting}
                required
              />
            </div>

            {errorMessage ? (
              <div className="rounded-xl border border-red-200 bg-red-50 px-4 py-3 text-sm text-red-700">
                {errorMessage}
              </div>
            ) : null}

            <Button type="submit" className="w-full" disabled={isSubmitting}>
              <ShieldCheck className="size-4" />

              {isSubmitting ? 'Membuat administrator...' : 'Buat Administrator'}
            </Button>
          </form>
        </div>

        <p className="mt-5 text-center text-xs leading-5 text-slate-400">
          Halaman ini hanya tersedia selama belum ada user di sistem.
        </p>
      </div>
    </main>
  )
}


############################################################
FILE: src/server/anggota/get-anggota-detail.ts
############################################################

import { createServerFn } from '@tanstack/react-start'

import { db } from '../db.server'
import { maskIdentityNumber } from '../kk/utils.server'
import { decryptValue } from '../security/encrypt.server'

import { anggotaIdSchema } from './schemas'
import { requireAdmin } from '../auth/session.server'

export const getAnggotaDetail = createServerFn({
  method: 'GET',
})
  .validator(anggotaIdSchema)
  .handler(async ({ data }) => {
    await requireAdmin()
    const anggota = await db.anggotaKeluarga.findUnique({
      where: {
        id: data.id,
      },

      select: {
        id: true,
        kkId: true,
        noUrut: true,

        nama: true,

        nikCiphertext: true,
        nikIv: true,
        nikAuthTag: true,

        jenisKelamin: true,
        tempatLahir: true,
        tanggalLahir: true,

        agama: true,
        pendidikan: true,
        pekerjaan: true,
        golonganDarah: true,

        ayah: true,
        ibu: true,
        baptisAnak: true,
        sidiBaptisDewasa: true,
        menikah: true,
        domisili: true,

        kk: {
          select: {
            kepalaKeluarga: true,

            alamat: true,
            rt: true,
            rw: true,

            desaKelurahan: true,
            kecamatan: true,
            kabupatenKota: true,
            kodePos: true,
            provinsi: true,
          },
        },
      },
    })

    if (!anggota) {
      throw new Error('Data anggota tidak ditemukan.')
    }

    const nik = decryptValue({
      ciphertext: anggota.nikCiphertext,
      iv: anggota.nikIv,
      authTag: anggota.nikAuthTag,
    })

    return {
      id: anggota.id,
      kkId: anggota.kkId,
      noUrut: anggota.noUrut,

      nama: anggota.nama,
      nik: maskIdentityNumber(nik),

      jenisKelamin: anggota.jenisKelamin,

      tempatLahir: anggota.tempatLahir,
      tanggalLahir: anggota.tanggalLahir,

      agama: anggota.agama,
      pendidikan: anggota.pendidikan,
      pekerjaan: anggota.pekerjaan,
      golonganDarah: anggota.golonganDarah,

      kepalaKeluarga: anggota.kk.kepalaKeluarga,

      alamat: anggota.kk.alamat,
      rt: anggota.kk.rt,
      rw: anggota.kk.rw,

      desaKelurahan: anggota.kk.desaKelurahan,
      kecamatan: anggota.kk.kecamatan,
      kabupatenKota: anggota.kk.kabupatenKota,
      kodePos: anggota.kk.kodePos,
      provinsi: anggota.kk.provinsi,

      ayah: anggota.ayah,
      ibu: anggota.ibu,
      baptisAnak: anggota.baptisAnak,
      sidiBaptisDewasa: anggota.sidiBaptisDewasa,
      menikah: anggota.menikah,
      domisili: anggota.domisili,
    }
  })

############################################################
FILE: src/server/anggota/list-anggota.ts
############################################################

import { createServerFn } from '@tanstack/react-start'

import { db } from '../db.server'

import { decryptValue } from '../security/encrypt.server'

import { maskIdentityNumber } from '../kk/utils.server'

import { buildAnggotaOrderBy, buildAnggotaWhere } from './list-query.server'

import { listAnggotaSchema } from './schemas'
import { requireAdmin } from '../auth/session.server'

export const listAnggota = createServerFn({
  method: 'GET',
})
  .validator(listAnggotaSchema)
  .handler(async ({ data }) => {
    await requireAdmin()
    const { page, limit, q, searchBy, sortBy, sortOrder } = data

    const where = buildAnggotaWhere(q, searchBy)

    const orderBy = buildAnggotaOrderBy(sortBy, sortOrder)

    const total = await db.anggotaKeluarga.count({
      where,
    })

    const totalPages = Math.max(Math.ceil(total / limit), 1)

    const safePage = Math.min(page, totalPages)

    const rows = await db.anggotaKeluarga.findMany({
      where,

      orderBy,

      skip: (safePage - 1) * limit,

      take: limit,

      select: {
        id: true,
        kkId: true,
        noUrut: true,
        nama: true,

        nikCiphertext: true,

        nikIv: true,

        nikAuthTag: true,

        jenisKelamin: true,

        tempatLahir: true,

        tanggalLahir: true,

        agama: true,

        pekerjaan: true,

        createdAt: true,

        kk: {
          select: {
            kepalaKeluarga: true,

            alamat: true,

            rt: true,

            rw: true,

            desaKelurahan: true,

            kecamatan: true,

            kabupatenKota: true,

            provinsi: true,
          },
        },
      },
    })

    return {
      data: rows.map((row) => {
        const nik = decryptValue({
          ciphertext: row.nikCiphertext,

          iv: row.nikIv,

          authTag: row.nikAuthTag,
        })

        return {
          id: row.id,

          kkId: row.kkId,

          no: row.noUrut,

          nama: row.nama,

          nik: maskIdentityNumber(nik),

          jenisKelamin: row.jenisKelamin,

          tempatLahir: row.tempatLahir,

          tanggalLahir: row.tanggalLahir,

          agama: row.agama,

          pekerjaan: row.pekerjaan,

          kepalaKeluarga: row.kk.kepalaKeluarga,

          alamat: row.kk.alamat,

          rt: row.kk.rt,

          rw: row.kk.rw,

          desaKelurahan: row.kk.desaKelurahan,

          kecamatan: row.kk.kecamatan,

          kabupatenKota: row.kk.kabupatenKota,

          provinsi: row.kk.provinsi,

          createdAt: row.createdAt.toISOString(),
        }
      }),

      pagination: {
        page: safePage,

        limit,

        total,

        totalPages,
      },
    }
  })


############################################################
FILE: src/server/anggota/list-query.server.ts
############################################################

import type { Prisma } from '@/generated/prisma/client'

import { hashIdentityNumber } from '../security/hash.server'

import type { AnggotaSearchBy, ListAnggotaInput } from './schemas'

type SortBy = ListAnggotaInput['sortBy']

type SortOrder = ListAnggotaInput['sortOrder']

function buildNamaWhere(q: string): Prisma.AnggotaKeluargaWhereInput {
  return {
    OR: [
      {
        nama: {
          contains: q,
          mode: 'insensitive',
        },
      },

      {
        kk: {
          is: {
            kepalaKeluarga: {
              contains: q,
              mode: 'insensitive',
            },
          },
        },
      },
    ],
  }
}

function buildAlamatWhere(q: string): Prisma.AnggotaKeluargaWhereInput {
  return {
    kk: {
      is: {
        OR: [
          {
            alamat: {
              contains: q,
              mode: 'insensitive',
            },
          },

          {
            desaKelurahan: {
              contains: q,
              mode: 'insensitive',
            },
          },

          {
            kecamatan: {
              contains: q,
              mode: 'insensitive',
            },
          },

          {
            kabupatenKota: {
              contains: q,
              mode: 'insensitive',
            },
          },

          {
            provinsi: {
              contains: q,
              mode: 'insensitive',
            },
          },
        ],
      },
    },
  }
}

function buildNikWhere(q: string): Prisma.AnggotaKeluargaWhereInput {
  const nik = q.replace(/\D/g, '')

  if (!nik) {
    return {
      id: -1,
    }
  }

  return {
    nikHash: hashIdentityNumber(nik),
  }
}

export function buildAnggotaWhere(
  query: string,
  searchBy: AnggotaSearchBy,
): Prisma.AnggotaKeluargaWhereInput {
  const q = query.trim()

  if (!q) {
    return {}
  }

  switch (searchBy) {
    case 'nama':
      return buildNamaWhere(q)

    case 'nik':
      return buildNikWhere(q)

    case 'alamat':
      return buildAlamatWhere(q)

    case 'all':
    default: {
      const conditions: Prisma.AnggotaKeluargaWhereInput[] = [
        buildNamaWhere(q),
        buildAlamatWhere(q),
      ]

      const numericQuery = q.replace(/\D/g, '')

      /*
       * Jangan menganggap angka pendek
       * seperti RT/RW sebagai NIK.
       */
      if (numericQuery.length >= 10 && numericQuery === q.replace(/\s/g, '')) {
        conditions.push(buildNikWhere(q))
      }

      return {
        OR: conditions,
      }
    }
  }
}

export function buildAnggotaOrderBy(
  sortBy: SortBy,
  sortOrder: SortOrder,
): Prisma.AnggotaKeluargaOrderByWithRelationInput {
  switch (sortBy) {
    case 'kepalaKeluarga':
      return {
        kk: {
          kepalaKeluarga: sortOrder,
        },
      }

    case 'alamat':
      return {
        kk: {
          alamat: sortOrder,
        },
      }

    case 'createdAt':
      return {
        createdAt: sortOrder,
      }

    case 'nama':
    default:
      return {
        nama: sortOrder,
      }
  }
}


############################################################
FILE: src/server/anggota/schemas.ts
############################################################

import { z } from 'zod'

export const anggotaSearchBySchema = z.enum(['all', 'nama', 'nik', 'alamat'])

export const anggotaIdSchema = z.object({
  id: z.number().int().positive(),
})

export const listAnggotaSchema = z.object({
  page: z.number().int().positive().default(1),

  limit: z
    .union([z.literal(10), z.literal(20), z.literal(50), z.literal(100)])
    .default(20),

  q: z.string().trim().max(100).default(''),

  searchBy: anggotaSearchBySchema.default('all'),

  sortBy: z
    .enum(['nama', 'kepalaKeluarga', 'alamat', 'createdAt'])
    .default('nama'),

  sortOrder: z.enum(['asc', 'desc']).default('asc'),
})

export type AnggotaSearchBy = z.infer<typeof anggotaSearchBySchema>

export type ListAnggotaInput = z.infer<typeof listAnggotaSchema>

import { createServerFn } from '@tanstack/react-start'

import { db } from '../db.server'

import { hashPassword, verifyPassword } from './password.server'
import {
  createUserSession,
  deleteCurrentSession,
  getCurrentSession,
} from './session.server'
import { initialAdminSchema, loginSchema } from './schemas'

export const login = createServerFn({
  method: 'POST',
})
  .validator(loginSchema)
  .handler(async ({ data }) => {
    const user = await db.user.findUnique({
      where: {
        username: data.username,
      },

      select: {
        id: true,
        nama: true,
        username: true,
        passwordHash: true,
        role: true,
        isActive: true,
      },
    })

    if (!user || !user.isActive) {
      return {
        success: false as const,
        message: 'Username atau password tidak benar.',
      }
    }

    const passwordValid = await verifyPassword(data.password, user.passwordHash)

    if (!passwordValid) {
      return {
        success: false as const,
        message: 'Username atau password tidak benar.',
      }
    }

    await createUserSession(user.id)

    return {
      success: true as const,

      user: {
        id: user.id,
        nama: user.nama,
        username: user.username,
        role: user.role,
      },
    }
  })

export const logout = createServerFn({
  method: 'POST',
}).handler(async () => {
  await deleteCurrentSession()

  return {
    success: true,
  }
})

export const getCurrentUser = createServerFn({
  method: 'GET',
}).handler(async () => {
  const session = await getCurrentSession()

  if (!session) {
    return null
  }

  return session.user
})

export const getInitialSetupStatus = createServerFn({
  method: 'GET',
}).handler(async () => {
  const userCount = await db.user.count()

  return {
    requiresSetup: userCount === 0,
  }
})

export const createInitialAdmin = createServerFn({
  method: 'POST',
})
  .validator(initialAdminSchema)
  .handler(async ({ data }) => {
    const userCount = await db.user.count()

    if (userCount > 0) {
      throw new Error(
        'Setup awal sudah selesai. Administrator baru harus dibuat oleh administrator.',
      )
    }

    const passwordHash = await hashPassword(data.password)

    const user = await db.user.create({
      data: {
        nama: data.nama,
        username: data.username,
        passwordHash,
        role: 'ADMIN',
        isActive: true,
      },

      select: {
        id: true,
        nama: true,
        username: true,
        role: true,
      },
    })

    await createUserSession(user.id)

    return {
      success: true,
      user,
    }
  })

import { getCurrentSession } from './session.server'

export const authMiddleware = createMiddleware({
  type: 'function',
}).server(async ({ next }) => {
  const session = await getCurrentSession()

  if (!session) {
    throw new Error('Unauthorized')
  }

  return next({
    context: {
      session,
    },
  })
})


############################################################
FILE: src/server/auth/password.server.ts
############################################################

import { randomBytes, scrypt, timingSafeEqual } from 'node:crypto'

const KEY_LENGTH = 64
const SALT_BYTES = 16

function deriveKey(password: string, salt: string): Promise<Buffer> {
  return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
    scrypt(password, salt, KEY_LENGTH, (error, derivedKey) => {
      if (error) {
        reject(error)
        return
      }

      resolve(derivedKey)
    })
  })
}

export async function hashPassword(password: string) {
  if (password.length < 8) {
    throw new Error('Password minimal 8 karakter.')
  }

  const salt = randomBytes(SALT_BYTES).toString('hex')
  const hash = await deriveKey(password, salt)

  return `scrypt$${salt}$${hash.toString('hex')}`
}

export async function verifyPassword(
  password: string,
  storedPasswordHash: string,
) {
  const [algorithm, salt, hashHex] = storedPasswordHash.split('$')

  if (algorithm !== 'scrypt' || !salt || !hashHex) {
    return false
  }

  const storedHash = Buffer.from(hashHex, 'hex')

  if (storedHash.length !== KEY_LENGTH) {
    return false
  }

  const candidateHash = await deriveKey(password, salt)

  return timingSafeEqual(storedHash, candidateHash)
}

import { z } from 'zod'

  .string()

  .max(50, 'Username maksimal 50 karakter.')
  .regex(
    /^[a-zA-Z0-9._-]+$/,
    'Username hanya boleh berisi huruf, angka, titik, garis bawah, atau tanda minus.',
  )
  .transform((value) => value.toLowerCase())

export const loginSchema = z.object({
  username: usernameSchema,

  password: z
    .string()
    .min(1, 'Password wajib diisi.')
    .max(128, 'Password terlalu panjang.'),
})

  nama: z
    .string()
    .trim()
    .min(2, 'Nama minimal 2 karakter.')
    .max(100, 'Nama maksimal 100 karakter.'),

  username: usernameSchema,

  password: z
    .string()
    .min(8, 'Password minimal 8 karakter.')
    .max(128, 'Password maksimal 128 karakter.'),
})


############################################################
FILE: src/server/auth/session.server.ts
############################################################

import { createHash, randomBytes } from 'node:crypto'

import {
  getRequestHeader,
  setResponseHeader,
} from '@tanstack/react-start/server'

import { db } from '../db.server'

const SESSION_TTL_MS = 12 * 60 * 60 * 1000

function getSessionCookieName() {
  return process.env.NODE_ENV === 'production'
    ? '__Host-gkjs_session'
    : 'gkjs_session'
}

function hashSessionToken(token: string) {
  return createHash('sha256').update(token).digest('hex')
}

function setSessionCookie(token: string, expiresAt: Date) {
  const parts = [
    `${getSessionCookieName()}=${encodeURIComponent(token)}`,
    'HttpOnly',
    'SameSite=Lax',
    'Path=/',
    `Expires=${expiresAt.toUTCString()}`,
  ]

  if (process.env.NODE_ENV === 'production') {
    parts.push('Secure')
  }

  setResponseHeader('Set-Cookie', parts.join('; '))
}

export function clearSessionCookie() {
  const parts = [
    `${getSessionCookieName()}=`,
    'HttpOnly',
    'SameSite=Lax',
    'Path=/',
    'Max-Age=0',
  ]

  if (process.env.NODE_ENV === 'production') {
    parts.push('Secure')
  }

  setResponseHeader('Set-Cookie', parts.join('; '))
}

export function readSessionToken() {
  const cookieHeader = getRequestHeader('cookie')

  if (!cookieHeader) {
    return null
  }

  const cookieName = getSessionCookieName()

  for (const cookie of cookieHeader.split(/;\s*/)) {
    const separatorIndex = cookie.indexOf('=')

    if (separatorIndex === -1) {
      continue
    }

    const name = cookie.slice(0, separatorIndex)
    const value = cookie.slice(separatorIndex + 1)

    if (name !== cookieName) {
      continue
    }

    try {
      return decodeURIComponent(value)
    } catch {
      return null
    }
  }

  return null
}

export async function createUserSession(userId: number) {
  const token = randomBytes(32).toString('base64url')
  const tokenHash = hashSessionToken(token)

  const expiresAt = new Date(Date.now() + SESSION_TTL_MS)

  const session = await db.session.create({
    data: {
      userId,
      tokenHash,
      expiresAt,
    },

    select: {
      id: true,
      expiresAt: true,
    },
  })

  setSessionCookie(token, expiresAt)

  return session
}

export async function getCurrentSession() {
  const token = readSessionToken()

  if (!token) {
    return null
  }

  const tokenHash = hashSessionToken(token)

  const session = await db.session.findUnique({
    where: {
      tokenHash,
    },

    select: {
      id: true,
      expiresAt: true,

      user: {
        select: {
          id: true,
          nama: true,
          username: true,
          role: true,
          isActive: true,
        },
      },
    },
  })

  if (!session) {
    return null
  }

  if (session.expiresAt <= new Date() || !session.user.isActive) {
    await db.session.deleteMany({
      where: {
        id: session.id,
      },
    })

    clearSessionCookie()

    return null
  }

  return session
}

export async function deleteCurrentSession() {
  const token = readSessionToken()

  if (token) {
    await db.session.deleteMany({
      where: {
        tokenHash: hashSessionToken(token),
      },
    })
  }

  clearSessionCookie()
}

export async function deleteAllUserSessions(userId: number) {
  await db.session.deleteMany({
    where: {
      userId,
    },
  })
}

export async function requireUser() {
  const session = await getCurrentSession()

  if (!session) {
    throw new Error('Unauthorized')
  }

  return session.user
}

export async function requireRole(...allowedRoles: Array<'ADMIN' | 'PETUGAS'>) {
  const user = await requireUser()

  const allowed = allowedRoles.some((role) => role === user.role)

  if (!allowed) {
    throw new Error('Forbidden')
  }

  return user
}

export async function requireAdmin() {
  return requireRole('ADMIN')
}

export async function requireHealthAccess() {
  return requireRole('ADMIN', 'PETUGAS')
}

import 'dotenv/config'

import { PrismaPg } from '@prisma/adapter-pg'

import { PrismaClient } from '@/generated/prisma/client'

const globalForPrisma = globalThis as unknown as {
  prisma: PrismaClient | undefined
}

const adapter = new PrismaPg({
  connectionString: process.env.DATABASE_URL,

  max: 10,

  connectionTimeoutMillis: 10_000,

  idleTimeoutMillis: 30_000,
})

export const db =
  globalForPrisma.prisma ??
  new PrismaClient({
    adapter,
  })

if (process.env.NODE_ENV !== 'production') {
  globalForPrisma.prisma = db
}


############################################################
FILE: src/server/home/get-home-stats.ts
############################################################

import { createServerFn } from '@tanstack/react-start'

import { db } from '../db.server'
import { requireAdmin } from '../auth/session.server'

export const getHomeStats = createServerFn({
  method: 'GET',
}).handler(async () => {
  await requireAdmin()
  const [totalKK, totalAnggota, events, latestEvent] = await Promise.all([
    db.kartuKeluarga.count(),

    db.anggotaKeluarga.count(),

    db.eventKesehatan.findMany({
      select: {
        isClosed: true,

        _count: {
          select: {
            pemeriksaan: true,
          },
        },
      },
    }),

    db.eventKesehatan.findFirst({
      orderBy: [
        {
          tanggal: 'desc',
        },
        {
          id: 'desc',
        },
      ],

      select: {
        id: true,
        nama: true,
        tanggal: true,
        lokasi: true,
        isClosed: true,

        _count: {
          select: {
            pemeriksaan: true,
          },
        },
      },
    }),
  ])

  const totalEvent = events.length

  const eventAktif = events.filter((event) => !event.isClosed).length

  const eventSelesai = totalEvent - eventAktif

  const totalPemeriksaan = events.reduce(
    (total, event) => total + event._count.pemeriksaan,
    0,
  )

  return {
    totalKK,
    totalAnggota,

    totalEvent,
    eventAktif,
    eventSelesai,

    totalPemeriksaan,

    latestEvent: latestEvent
      ? {
          id: latestEvent.id,

          nama: latestEvent.nama,

          tanggal: latestEvent.tanggal.toISOString().slice(0, 10),

          lokasi: latestEvent.lokasi,

          isClosed: latestEvent.isClosed,

          jumlahPemeriksaan: latestEvent._count.pemeriksaan,
        }
      : null,
  }
})


############################################################
FILE: src/server/kesehatan/detail.ts

import { createServerFn } from '@tanstack/react-start'

import { db } from '../db.server'
import { decryptValue } from '../security/encrypt.server'
import { hashIdentityNumber } from '../security/hash.server'
import { maskIdentityNumber } from '../kk/utils.server'

import { eventKesehatanIdSchema, searchWargaKesehatanSchema } from './schemas'
import { requireHealthAccess } from '../auth/session.server'

export const getEventKesehatan = createServerFn({
  method: 'GET',
})
  .validator(eventKesehatanIdSchema)
  .handler(async ({ data }) => {
    await requireHealthAccess()
    const event = await db.eventKesehatan.findUnique({
      where: {
        id: data.id,
      },

      select: {
        id: true,
        nama: true,
        tanggal: true,
        lokasi: true,

        isClosed: true,
        closedAt: true,

        petugas: {
          orderBy: {
            nama: 'asc',
          },
          select: {
            id: true,
            nama: true,
          },
        },

        _count: {
          select: {
            pemeriksaan: true,
          },
        },
      },
    })

    if (!event) {
      throw new Error('Event kesehatan tidak ditemukan.')
    }

    return {
      id: event.id,
      nama: event.nama,
      tanggal: event.tanggal.toISOString().slice(0, 10),
      lokasi: event.lokasi,
      isClosed: event.isClosed,
      closedAt: event.closedAt?.toISOString() ?? null,
      jumlahPemeriksaan: event._count.pemeriksaan,
      petugas: event.petugas,
    }
  })

export const searchWargaKesehatan = createServerFn({
  method: 'GET',
})
  .validator(searchWargaKesehatanSchema)
  .handler(async ({ data }) => {
    await requireHealthAccess()

    const event = await db.eventKesehatan.findUnique({
      where: {
        id: data.eventId,
      },
      select: {
        id: true,
      },
    })

    if (!event) {
      throw new Error('Event kesehatan tidak ditemukan.')
    }

    const q = data.q.trim()

    const digits = q.replace(/\D/g, '')

    const orConditions = [
      {
        nama: {
          contains: q,
          mode: 'insensitive' as const,
        },
      },
    ]

    if (digits.length === 16) {
      orConditions.push({
        nikHash: hashIdentityNumber(digits),
      } as never)
    }

    const rows = await db.anggotaKeluarga.findMany({
      where: {
        OR: orConditions,
      },

      take: 20,

      orderBy: {
        nama: 'asc',
      },

      select: {
        id: true,
        nama: true,

        nikCiphertext: true,
        nikIv: true,
        nikAuthTag: true,

        jenisKelamin: true,
        tanggalLahir: true,

        kk: {
          select: {
            kepalaKeluarga: true,
            alamat: true,
            desaKelurahan: true,
          },
        },

        pemeriksaanKesehatan: {
          where: {
            eventId: data.eventId,
          },
          select: {
            id: true,
          },
          take: 1,
        },
      },
    })

    return rows.map((row) => {
      const nik = decryptValue({
        ciphertext: row.nikCiphertext,
        iv: row.nikIv,
        authTag: row.nikAuthTag,
      })

      return {
        id: row.id,
        nama: row.nama,
        nik: maskIdentityNumber(nik),
        jenisKelamin: row.jenisKelamin,
        tanggalLahir: row.tanggalLahir,
        kepalaKeluarga: row.kk.kepalaKeluarga,
        alamat: row.kk.alamat,
        desaKelurahan: row.kk.desaKelurahan,
        sudahDiperiksa: row.pemeriksaanKesehatan.length > 0,
      }
    })
  })


############################################################
FILE: src/server/kesehatan/event.ts
############################################################

import { createServerFn } from '@tanstack/react-start'

import { db } from '../db.server'

import {
  createEventKesehatanSchema,
  updateEventKesehatanSchema,
  eventKesehatanIdSchema,
  listEventKesehatanSchema,
} from './schemas'
import type { Prisma } from '#/generated/prisma/client'
import { requireHealthAccess } from '../auth/session.server'

export const listEventKesehatan = createServerFn({
  method: 'GET',
})
  .validator(listEventKesehatanSchema)
  .handler(async ({ data }) => {
    await requireHealthAccess()
    const { page, limit, q, status, tahun } = data

    const where: Prisma.EventKesehatanWhereInput = {
      ...(q
        ? {
            OR: [
              {
                nama: {
                  contains: q,
                  mode: 'insensitive',
                },
              },
              {
                lokasi: {
                  contains: q,
                  mode: 'insensitive',
                },
              },
            ],
          }
        : {}),

      ...(status !== 'all'
        ? {
            isClosed: status === 'selesai',
          }
        : {}),

      ...(tahun
        ? {
            tanggal: {
              gte: new Date(Date.UTC(tahun, 0, 1)),

              lt: new Date(Date.UTC(tahun + 1, 0, 1)),
            },
          }
        : {}),
    }

    const total = await db.eventKesehatan.count({
      where,
    })

    const totalPages = Math.max(Math.ceil(total / limit), 1)

    const safePage = Math.min(page, totalPages)

    const rows = await db.eventKesehatan.findMany({
      where,

      orderBy: [
        {
          tanggal: 'desc',
        },
        {
          id: 'desc',
        },
      ],

      skip: (safePage - 1) * limit,

      take: limit,

      select: {
        id: true,
        nama: true,
        tanggal: true,
        lokasi: true,
        isClosed: true,
        closedAt: true,

        petugas: {
          orderBy: {
            nama: 'asc',
          },

          select: {
            id: true,
            nama: true,
          },
        },

        _count: {
          select: {
            pemeriksaan: true,
          },
        },
      },
    })

    return {
      data: rows.map((row) => ({
        id: row.id,

        nama: row.nama,

        tanggal: row.tanggal.toISOString().slice(0, 10),

        lokasi: row.lokasi,

        isClosed: row.isClosed,

        closedAt: row.closedAt?.toISOString() ?? null,

        petugas: row.petugas,

        jumlahPemeriksaan: row._count.pemeriksaan,
      })),

      pagination: {
        page: safePage,

        limit,

        total,

        totalPages,
      },
    }
  })

export const createEventKesehatan = createServerFn({
  method: 'POST',
})
  .validator(createEventKesehatanSchema)
  .handler(async ({ data }) => {
    await requireHealthAccess()
    const uniquePetugas = [
      ...new Map(
        data.petugas.map((nama) => [nama.trim().toLowerCase(), nama.trim()]),
      ).values(),
    ]

    const event = await db.eventKesehatan.create({
      data: {
        nama: data.nama,
        tanggal: new Date(`${data.tanggal}T00:00:00.000Z`),
        lokasi: data.lokasi || null,

        petugas: {
          create: uniquePetugas.map((nama) => ({
            nama,
          })),
        },
      },

      select: {
        id: true,
        nama: true,
      },
    })

    return event
  })

export const updateEventKesehatan = createServerFn({
  method: 'POST',
})
  .validator(updateEventKesehatanSchema)
  .handler(async ({ data }) => {
    await requireHealthAccess()
    const current = await db.eventKesehatan.findUnique({
      where: {
        id: data.id,
      },
      select: {
        id: true,
        isClosed: true,
      },
    })

    if (!current) {
      throw new Error('Event kesehatan tidak ditemukan.')
    }

    if (current.isClosed) {
      throw new Error('Event sudah ditutup dan tidak dapat diedit.')
    }

    return db.eventKesehatan.update({
      where: {
        id: data.id,
      },

      data: {
        nama: data.nama,
        tanggal: new Date(`${data.tanggal}T00:00:00.000Z`),
        lokasi: data.lokasi || null,
      },
      select: {
        id: true,
      },
    })
  })

export const closeEventKesehatan = createServerFn({
  method: 'POST',
})
  .validator(eventKesehatanIdSchema)
  .handler(async ({ data }) => {
    await requireHealthAccess()
    const event = await db.eventKesehatan.findUnique({
      where: {
        id: data.id,
      },

      select: {
        id: true,
        isClosed: true,
      },
    })

    if (!event) {
      throw new Error('Event kesehatan tidak ditemukan.')
    }

    if (event.isClosed) {
      throw new Error('Event ini sudah ditutup.')
    }

    await db.eventKesehatan.update({
      where: {
        id: data.id,
      },

      data: {
        isClosed: true,
        closedAt: new Date(),
      },
    })

    return {
      success: true,
    }
  })

export const deleteEventKesehatan = createServerFn({
  method: 'POST',
})
  .validator(eventKesehatanIdSchema)
  .handler(async ({ data }) => {
    await requireHealthAccess()

    const event = await db.eventKesehatan.findUnique({
      where: {
        id: data.id,
      },

      select: {
        id: true,

        _count: {
          select: {
            pemeriksaan: true,
          },
        },
      },
    })

    if (!event) {
      throw new Error('Event kesehatan tidak ditemukan.')
    }

    if (event._count.pemeriksaan > 0) {
      throw new Error(
        'Event yang sudah memiliki pemeriksaan tidak boleh dihapus karena data pemeriksaan harus tetap disimpan.',
      )
    }

    await db.eventKesehatan.delete({
      where: {
        id: data.id,
      },
    })

    return {
      success: true,
    }
  })

import { createServerFn } from '@tanstack/react-start'

import { db } from '../db.server'

import {
  listPemeriksaanEventSchema,
  riwayatPemeriksaanSchema,
  savePemeriksaanKesehatanSchema,
  updatePemeriksaanKesehatanSchema,
} from './schemas'
import { decryptValue } from '../security/encrypt.server'
import { maskIdentityNumber } from '../kk/utils.server'
import { requireHealthAccess } from '../auth/session.server'

export const savePemeriksaanKesehatan = createServerFn({
  method: 'POST',
})
  .validator(savePemeriksaanKesehatanSchema)
  .handler(async ({ data }) => {
    const currentUser = await requireHealthAccess()

    const event = await db.eventKesehatan.findUnique({
      where: {
        id: data.eventId,
      },
      select: {
        id: true,
        isClosed: true,
      },
    })

    if (!event) {
      throw new Error('Event kesehatan tidak ditemukan.')
    }

    if (event.isClosed) {
      throw new Error(
        'Event ini sudah ditutup. Pemeriksaan baru tidak dapat ditambahkan.',
      )
    }

    const existing = await db.pemeriksaanKesehatan.findUnique({
      where: {
        eventId_anggotaId: {
          eventId: data.eventId,
          anggotaId: data.anggotaId,
        },
      },

      select: {
        id: true,
      },
    })

    if (existing) {
      throw new Error(
        'Warga ini sudah memiliki hasil pemeriksaan pada event ini.',
      )
    }

    const pemeriksaan = await db.pemeriksaanKesehatan.create({
      data: {
        eventId: data.eventId,
        anggotaId: data.anggotaId,

        createdByUserId: currentUser.id,
        updatedByUserId: currentUser.id,

        tensiSistolik: data.tensiSistolik,
        tensiDiastolik: data.tensiDiastolik,

        gulaDarah: data.gulaDarah,
        kolesterol: data.kolesterol,
        asamUrat: data.asamUrat,

        beratBadan: data.beratBadan,
        tinggiBadan: data.tinggiBadan,

        keluhan: data.keluhan || null,
        hasilRingkas: data.hasilRingkas || null,
      },

      select: {
        id: true,
      },
    })

    return pemeriksaan
  })

export const getRiwayatPemeriksaan = createServerFn({
  method: 'GET',
})
  .validator(riwayatPemeriksaanSchema)
  .handler(async ({ data }) => {
    await requireHealthAccess()
    const rows = await db.pemeriksaanKesehatan.findMany({
      where: {
        anggotaId: data.anggotaId,
      },

      orderBy: [
        {
          event: {
            tanggal: 'desc',
          },
        },
        {
          createdAt: 'desc',
        },
      ],

      select: {
        id: true,

        tensiSistolik: true,
        tensiDiastolik: true,

        gulaDarah: true,
        kolesterol: true,
        asamUrat: true,

        beratBadan: true,
        tinggiBadan: true,

        keluhan: true,
        hasilRingkas: true,

        createdAt: true,
        updatedAt: true,

        createdBy: {
          select: {
            id: true,
            nama: true,
            username: true,
            role: true,
          },
        },

        updatedBy: {
          select: {
            id: true,
            nama: true,
            username: true,
            role: true,
          },
        },

        event: {
          select: {
            id: true,
            nama: true,
            tanggal: true,
            lokasi: true,

            petugas: {
              orderBy: {
                nama: 'asc',
              },
              select: {
                id: true,
                nama: true,
              },
            },
          },
        },
      },
    })

    return rows.map((row) => ({
      id: row.id,

      createdAt: row.createdAt.toISOString(),
      updatedAt: row.updatedAt.toISOString(),

      createdBy: row.createdBy,
      updatedBy: row.updatedBy,

      tensiSistolik: row.tensiSistolik,
      tensiDiastolik: row.tensiDiastolik,

      gulaDarah: row.gulaDarah,
      kolesterol: row.kolesterol,
      asamUrat: row.asamUrat,

      beratBadan: row.beratBadan,
      tinggiBadan: row.tinggiBadan,

      keluhan: row.keluhan,
      hasilRingkas: row.hasilRingkas,

      event: {
        id: row.event.id,
        nama: row.event.nama,
        tanggal: row.event.tanggal.toISOString().slice(0, 10),
        lokasi: row.event.lokasi,
        petugas: row.event.petugas,
      },
    }))
  })

export const listPemeriksaanEvent = createServerFn({
  method: 'GET',
})
  .validator(listPemeriksaanEventSchema)
  .handler(async ({ data }) => {
    await requireHealthAccess()
    const rows = await db.pemeriksaanKesehatan.findMany({
      where: {
        eventId: data.eventId,
      },

      orderBy: {
        createdAt: 'desc',
      },

      select: {
        id: true,

        tensiSistolik: true,
        tensiDiastolik: true,
        gulaDarah: true,
        kolesterol: true,
        asamUrat: true,
        beratBadan: true,
        tinggiBadan: true,

        keluhan: true,
        hasilRingkas: true,

        createdAt: true,
        updatedAt: true,

        createdBy: {
          select: {
            id: true,
            nama: true,
            username: true,
            role: true,
          },
        },

        updatedBy: {
          select: {
            id: true,
            nama: true,
            username: true,
            role: true,
          },
        },

        event: {
          select: {
            petugas: {
              orderBy: {
                nama: 'asc',
              },
              select: {
                id: true,
                nama: true,
              },
            },
          },
        },

        anggota: {
          select: {
            id: true,
            nama: true,

            nikCiphertext: true,
            nikIv: true,
            nikAuthTag: true,
          },
        },
      },
    })

    return rows.map((row) => {
      const nik = decryptValue({
        ciphertext: row.anggota.nikCiphertext,
        iv: row.anggota.nikIv,
        authTag: row.anggota.nikAuthTag,
      })

      return {
        id: row.id,

        anggotaId: row.anggota.id,
        nama: row.anggota.nama,
        nik: maskIdentityNumber(nik),

        createdAt: row.createdAt.toISOString(),
        updatedAt: row.updatedAt.toISOString(),

        createdBy: row.createdBy,
        updatedBy: row.updatedBy,

        tensiSistolik: row.tensiSistolik,
        tensiDiastolik: row.tensiDiastolik,
        gulaDarah: row.gulaDarah,
        kolesterol: row.kolesterol,
        asamUrat: row.asamUrat,
        beratBadan: row.beratBadan,
        tinggiBadan: row.tinggiBadan,

        keluhan: row.keluhan,
        petugas: row.event.petugas,
        hasilRingkas: row.hasilRingkas,
      }
    })
  })

export const updatePemeriksaanKesehatan = createServerFn({
  method: 'POST',
})
  .validator(updatePemeriksaanKesehatanSchema)
  .handler(async ({ data }) => {
    const currentUser = await requireHealthAccess()
    const existing = await db.pemeriksaanKesehatan.findUnique({
      where: {
        id: data.id,
      },

      select: {
        id: true,

        event: {
          select: {
            isClosed: true,
          },
        },
      },
    })

    if (!existing) {
      throw new Error('Data pemeriksaan tidak ditemukan.')
    }

    if (existing.event.isClosed) {
      throw new Error(
        'Event ini sudah ditutup. Hasil pemeriksaan tidak dapat diubah.',
      )
    }

    const updated = await db.pemeriksaanKesehatan.update({
      where: {
        id: data.id,
      },

      data: {
        tensiSistolik: data.tensiSistolik,
        tensiDiastolik: data.tensiDiastolik,

        gulaDarah: data.gulaDarah,
        kolesterol: data.kolesterol,
        asamUrat: data.asamUrat,

        beratBadan: data.beratBadan,
        tinggiBadan: data.tinggiBadan,

        keluhan: data.keluhan || null,
        hasilRingkas: data.hasilRingkas || null,

        updatedByUserId: currentUser.id,
      },

      select: {
        id: true,
      },
    })

    return updated
  })

import { createServerFn } from '@tanstack/react-start'

import { db } from '../db.server'

import { hapusPetugasEventSchema, tambahPetugasEventSchema } from './schemas'
import { requireHealthAccess } from '../auth/session.server'

export const tambahPetugasEvent = createServerFn({
  method: 'POST',
})
  .validator(tambahPetugasEventSchema)
  .handler(async ({ data }) => {
    await requireHealthAccess()

    const event = await db.eventKesehatan.findUnique({
      where: {
        id: data.eventId,
      },
      select: {
        id: true,
        isClosed: true,
      },
    })

    if (!event) {
      throw new Error('Event kesehatan tidak ditemukan.')
    }

    if (event.isClosed) {
      throw new Error(
        'Event ini sudah ditutup. Petugas tidak dapat ditambahkan.',
      )
    }

    const existing = await db.petugasEvent.findFirst({
      where: {
        eventId: data.eventId,
        nama: {
          equals: data.nama,
          mode: 'insensitive',
        },
      },
      select: {
        id: true,
      },
    })

    if (existing) {
      throw new Error('Petugas tersebut sudah terdaftar pada event ini.')
    }

    return db.petugasEvent.create({
      data: {
        eventId: data.eventId,
        nama: data.nama,
      },
      select: {
        id: true,
        nama: true,
      },
    })
  })

export const hapusPetugasEvent = createServerFn({
  method: 'POST',
})
  .validator(hapusPetugasEventSchema)
  .handler(async ({ data }) => {
    const petugas = await db.petugasEvent.findUnique({
      where: {
        id: data.id,
      },
      select: {
        id: true,

        event: {
          select: {
            isClosed: true,
          },
        },
      },
    })

    if (!petugas) {
      throw new Error('Petugas event tidak ditemukan.')
    }

    if (petugas.event.isClosed) {
      throw new Error('Event ini sudah ditutup. Petugas tidak dapat dihapus.')
    }

    await db.petugasEvent.delete({
      where: {
        id: data.id,
      },
    })

    return {
      success: true,
    }
  })
